TL;DR: 本文提出安全评估中的"审计差距"概念，即模型行为层面的安全性与潜在空间鲁棒性之间的不一致。通过构建"解离模型"（保持安全行为但潜在空间脆弱），并引入基于干预的评估框架和潜在脆弱性评分（LVS），证明行为安全指标不足以反映模型的表征级鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估主要关注行为层面（输出），缺乏对表征层面在干预下鲁棒性的考量，存在评估盲区。作者希望量化这一差距并揭示其严重性。

Method: 1) 构建"解离模型"（dissociated models），使模型在外显行为上保持安全，但潜在表征空间存在脆弱性；2) 提出基于干预的评估框架，包括参数空间的有害微调（harmful fine-tuning）和逐层潜在扰动（layer-wise latent perturbations）；3) 提出潜在脆弱性评分（LVS）度量在有限潜在扰动下诱发有害行为的难易程度。

Result: 多个安全/非安全对齐的最先进模型上，行为安全指标与表征级鲁棒性存在显著差异；解离模型在保持相当水平的拒绝有害行为能力下，LVS显著升高；中间表征层对干预最为敏感。

Conclusion: 仅依赖行为安全评估无法完整反映模型鲁棒性，需要同时关注潜在脆弱性与可观察行为的表征感知审计。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) safety has often been evaluated at the behavior level, which provides limited evidence of internal robustness, as these evaluations target outputs rather than representation-level vulnerability under intervention. We formalize this discrepancy as the audit gap: the difference between behavioral safety and robustness under intervention. To study this gap, we construct dissociated models that preserve safe outward behavior while remaining vulnerable in the latent space. We introduce an intervention-based evaluation framework to test model robustness through soft interventions in parameter and latent spaces, including harmful fine-tuning and layer-wise latent perturbations. To formalize the evaluation, we propose the Latent Vulnerability Score (LVS) to measure how easily harmful behavior can be elicited by bounded latent perturbations. Using this evaluation framework, we show that behavioral safety metrics are insufficient measures of representation-level robustness across multiple safely and unsafely aligned state-of-the-art models. Notably, dissociated models show substantially elevated LVSs despite comparable refusal behavior under harmful intervention, with intermediate representations being the most sensitive to intervention. Our results suggest that behavioral safety evaluation alone provides an incomplete picture of model robustness, motivating representation-aware audits of latent vulnerability and observable behavior.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Homophily: Towards Generalized Graph Reconstruction Attack and Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08067)
*Zhanke Zhou,Bo Han,Xuan Li,Jiangchao Yao,Sanmi Koyejo,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究图神经网络（GNN）的训练图邻接关系重构攻击（GRA），提出基于马尔可夫链近似的攻击方法MC-GRA(+)和防御方法MC-GPB(+)，在保持分类精度的同时实现邻接重构与隐私保护的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN广泛部署于关系数据上，但可能泄漏训练图邻接中的敏感或专有信息（如社交关系、交易记录等）。现有工作缺乏对邻接可恢复性条件的系统刻画，以及相应的攻防方法。

Method: 首先系统刻画了特征、标签、嵌入和预测中邻接信息可恢复的条件，揭示图同质性/异质性与模型归纳偏置的调节作用。将GNN前向计算视为拓扑依赖表示的马尔可夫链，提出MC-GRA(+)攻击方法，通过优化替代邻接使其各层GNN表示与目标模型对齐；提出MC-GPB(+)防御方法，在表示链中抑制邻接依赖信息，同时保持分类精度以实现隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在同质/异质图基准和多种GNN上的实验表明，MC-GRA(+)在重构保真度上优于现有方法；MC-GPB(+)在仅轻微牺牲分类精度的前提下有效降低重构成功率。

Conclusion: 提出了一套系统的图邻接重构攻防框架，揭示了邻接信息在GNN各层表示中的泄漏机制，并提供了有效的攻击与防御工具。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely deployed on relational data, yet they can leak sensitive or proprietary information about the training graph adjacency, e.g., social ties, transactions, and interactions. This work studies graph reconstruction attacks (GRA), a form of model inversion that reconstructs the training adjacency from a trained GNN, given different levels of attacker-side information. We first provide a systematic characterization of when and why adjacency becomes recoverable through features, labels, embeddings, and predictions, with leakage modulated by graph homophily, heterophily, and the model's inductive bias. Motivated by these findings, we view GNN inference through a Markov chain approximation lens, treating the layered forward computation as a chain of topology-dependent representations. Building on this view, we develop complementary attack and defense methods. On the attack side, we propose MC-GRA (+), which reconstructs the adjacency by optimizing a surrogate adjacency whose GNN-induced representations align with those of the target model at each layer. On the defense side, we propose MC-GPB (+), which suppresses adjacency-dependent information throughout the representation chain while aiming to preserve classification accuracy under a privacy-utility trade-off. Experiments across homophilic/heterophilic graph benchmarks and GNNs show that our attacks improve reconstruction fidelity over prior methods, while our defenses reduce reconstruction success with only minor accuracy loss.

</details>


### [104] [DICE: Entropy-Regularized Equilibrium Selection for Stable Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08068)
*Yi Xie,Zhanke Zhou,Chentao Cao,Bo Liu,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统因均衡选择不当（振荡与漂移）导致稳定性差；本文提出异质分位数响应均衡（HQRE）作为良定义目标，并基于此设计DICE-PC和DICE-FT两种算法，在11个基准上分别提升4.3%和8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统经常无法可靠地超越配备Best-of-N采样的单个强模型，核心原因是均衡选择不当：当前系统规定了智能体共享哪些信息，但未指定应选择哪种协调惯例。

Method: 将多智能体LLM系统形式化为折扣不完全信息马尔可夫博弈；引入异质分位数响应均衡（HQRE），一种具有智能体和状态依赖温度参数的熵正则化均衡概念；基于HQRE设计了两种算法：DICE-PC（通过提示控制协调冻结模型）和DICE-FT（参数高效的镜像微调）。

Result: 在四个领域的11个基准上，DICE在准确率-成本权衡上优于强同类基线；DICE-PC平均提升4.3个百分点，DICE-FT平均提升8.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 满足单调性条件下，HQRE具有唯一性、镜像更新线性收敛和有限贝叶斯遗憾，且该条件可产生基于rollout的稳定性诊断指标，为多智能体LLM协调提供了良定义的优化目标。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems often fail to reliably outperform a single strong model equipped with best-of-N sampling. We argue that a core source of this instability is ill-posed equilibrium selection: current systems specify what information agents share, but not which coordination convention should be selected. We formalize a broad class of such systems as discounted incomplete-information Markov games and show that two common pathologies, oscillation between competing conventions and drift across them, can both induce unstable learning and linear Bayesian regret. To obtain a well-posed target, we introduce the Heterogeneous Quantal Response Equilibrium (HQRE), an entropy-regularized equilibrium concept with agent- and state-dependent temperatures. Under a monotonicity condition, HQRE is unique, admits linearly convergent mirror updates, and yields bounded Bayesian regret; the same condition yields rollout-measurable stability diagnostics. We instantiate this objective in two algorithms: DICE-PC, which coordinates frozen models through prompt-control actions, and DICE-FT, which performs parameter-efficient mirror fine-tuning. Across eleven benchmarks in four domains, DICE improves accuracy-cost trade-offs over strong within-class baselines; on reasoning and planning tasks, DICE-PC improves by 4.3 percentage points on average and DICE-FT by 8.5 points.

</details>


### [105] [ConSteer-RL: Steering Reasoning Capabilities in Large Language Models via Confidence-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08088)
*Qing Miao,Yiming Zhao,Jing Yang,Chenxi Liu,Yuehai Chen,Yuewen Liu,Shaoyi Du,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ConSteer-RL框架，通过在GRPO中融入基于token对数概率的置信度信号，构建置信度感知的奖励塑造机制，在多个模型规模上平均提升2.3%-4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅依赖稀疏的二元奖励信号，且忽视了模型内部的不确定性信息，限制了推理能力的提升。

Method: 基于GRPO框架，聚合每个token的对数概率为标量置信度分数，构建置信度感知的奖励塑造机制：惩罚过度自信的错误，同时强化正确且自信的推理过程。

Result: 在不同模型规模上，ConSteer-RL一致优于强GRPO基线，平均提升2.3%-4.0%。

Conclusion: 将模型内在的置信度信号融入RLVR训练是一种简单有效的方法，能够显著提升LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has recently become a key paradigm for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it remains limited by sparse binary rewards and its ignorance of model-internal uncertainty. In this paper, we propose ConSteer-RL, a simple yet effective framework that integrates token-level confidence signals derived from model log-probabilities into RLVR training. Specifically, building upon the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework, we construct a confidence-aware reward by aggregating per-token probabilities into a scalar confidence score and incorporating it into an awareness-based reward shaping mechanism that penalizes overconfident errors while reinforcing correct and confident reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that ConSteer-RL consistently outperforms strong GRPO baselines, achieving average improvements of 2.3%-4.0% across different model scales.

</details>


### [106] [Constraint-Aware Optimization for Robust Protein Stability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08100)
*A Shivram,Aneesh S. Chivukula,Manik Gupta,Sourav Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需修改架构的约束感知优化框架（结合平衡均方误差、Siamese反对称正则化器和OOD边界一致性损失），在SPURS骨干网络上显著提升了蛋白质稳定性预测的分布外泛化能力（S669 Spearman从0.486提至0.540），但反对称训练并未消除前向-反向偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ΔΔG预测器在分布外蛋白质上鲁棒性有限，存在前向-反向偏差，且对稀有稳定突变表征不足。已有方法主要通过添加架构组件来解决，优化层面的干预探索不足。

Method: 提出约束感知优化框架：(1) 平衡均方误差（Balanced MSE），(2) Siamese反对称正则化器，(3) 基于每个位置特征表示的OOD边界一致性损失。无需修改SPURS骨干网络架构。

Result: 在11个基准和3个随机种子下，S669 Spearman从0.486提至0.540（σ=0.002），S461从0.653提至0.711，在5个额外OOD数据集上一致提升。诊断实验表明反对称训练并未消除系统性前向-反向偏差，增益来自隐式正则化而非精确热力学约束。

Conclusion: 优化层面的干预可有效提升蛋白质稳定性预测的分布外泛化性能，无需架构修改；但反对称正则化带来的性能提升源于隐式正则化效应，而非精确的热力学约束满足。

Abstract: Multimodal $ΔΔG$ predictors integrating protein language models with inverse-folding representations achieve strong in-distribution accuracy on the Megascale dataset but exhibit limited robustness on out-of-distribution (OOD) proteins, persistent forward-reverse bias on paired-mutation benchmarks, and under-representation of rare stabilizing mutations. Existing approaches address these limitations primarily through additional architectural components, leaving optimization-level intervention comparatively underexplored. We introduce a constraint-aware optimization framework combining Balanced Mean Squared Error, a Siamese anti-symmetric regularizer, and a novel OOD-margin consistency loss on the per-position feature representation, requiring no architectural changes to the SPURS backbone. Across eleven benchmarks and three random seeds, the framework improves Spearman correlation on S669 from 0.486 to 0.540 ($σ=0.002$ across seeds), matching the published SPURS baseline (0.50) without architectural modification, and on S461 from 0.653 to 0.711, with consistent smaller gains on five additional OOD datasets. A controlled diagnostic on Ssym reveals that anti-symmetric training does not eliminate systematic forward-reverse bias, indicating that gains arise through implicit regularization rather than exact thermodynamic constraint enforcement.

</details>


### [107] [A Unifying View of Attention Sinks: Two Algorithms, Two Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08105)
*Lukas Fesser,Mozes Jacobs,Thomas Fel,Andy Keller,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 注意力漩涡（sink）看似相同，实则包含两种不同机制：自适应空操作（nop）和广播（broadcast）。现有干预方法分别隐含针对其中一种机制，但都不能完全解决问题。结合门控（gating）与寄存器（register）才能互补提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax Transformer中普遍存在注意力汇聚到单个token（sink）的现象，但这一视觉相似的表象可能掩盖了完全不同的计算机制。作者旨在揭示sink背后真实发生的计算，并据此指导有效的干预策略。

Method: 在合成任务上形式化了两种机制（nop和broadcast）及其可区分特征（nop的value范数可忽略，broadcast导致低秩输出）；在预训练视觉Transformer上进行诊断分析，验证两种机制同时存在；对比门控与寄存器两种干预方法，并测试组合方案。

Result: 两种机制在大规模模型中确实共存：sink从早期层的CLS转移到深层层的patch，并集中在专门的注意力头中。寄存器token虽然为广播设计，但也被重新用作nop。单独的门控或寄存器都不能完全解决问题，两者结合在稳定性和性能上取得互补增益。

Conclusion: 相同的注意力模式可能反映完全不同的计算，有效的干预必须首先理解模型实际在做什么。没有任何单一干预策略能同时覆盖两种机制，组合方案是必要的。

Abstract: When attention concentrates on a single token, a sink, what is the model actually computing? Attention sinks are ubiquitous in softmax transformers, yet this shared visual signature can hide fundamentally different algorithms. We show that visually similar sink patterns can reflect two distinct mechanisms: {i} adaptive nop, where a head suppresses its update by routing to a null token, and {ii} broadcast, where a sink aggregates and redistributes global information. In that case, sinks serve an analogous role: a safe destination when there is nothing useful to compute. Proposed interventions like gating or registers work because they implicitly target one or the other, revealing a duality between method and assumed mechanism: gating implicitly assumes nop; registers implicitly assume broadcast. Each mechanism leaves distinct traces (nop sinks exhibit negligible value norms; broadcast sinks induce low-rank outputs) which we formalize on synthetic tasks and use to derive practical diagnostics. Applied to pretrained vision transformers, these diagnostics reveal that both mechanisms exist at scale: sinks transition from CLS in early layers to patches in deeper layers, and concentrate in specialized heads. Strikingly, register tokens, designed for broadcast, are repurposed to also serve nop, confirming that neither intervention alone suffices. Combining gating with registers yields complementary gains in stability and performance. Overall, we find that the same attention pattern can reflect two very different computations and effective intervention requires first asking what the model is actually computing.

</details>


### [108] [Conditional Random Ordered Transport Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08113)
*Lei Luo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件随机有序传输空间（CROTS），一种用于可靠分布学习的新空间理论框架，其核心在于区分Wasserstein距离与序允许性（admissibility），并证明即使Wasserstein距离很小，也不能保证变换是序允许的。通过引入硬/软有序传输差异、条件风险泛函等工具，建立了序允许传输几何，并证明了环境Wasserstein收敛与局部序允许性泄漏可分离演化的稳定性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Wasserstein距离仅衡量概率分布之间的距离，但在证据受限、语义、因果、物理、单调或风险敏感学习中，我们需要知道质量移动的方向是否被可用信息所允许。小的Wasserstein距离并不能保证变换的允许性（admissibility），因此需要建立一个新的理论框架来区分"分布接近"与"变换允许"这两个概念。

Method: CROTS框架的核心组件包括：(1) 以Wasserstein为环境度量的L^0值随机概率测度空间；(2) 封闭的随机序（stochastic order）；(3) 硬有序传输差异（hard ordered transport discrepancy）和软有序传输差异（soft ordered transport discrepancy）；(4) 用于评估证据σ域下序违例的条件风险泛函。方法涉及序允许传输几何的构造、对偶理论、变分收敛、可测性和完备性证明、测地线构建、约束重心和投影、条件风险-传输对偶性，以及序违例分布的分离。

Result: (1) 建立了硬有序传输和软有序传输的适定性和对偶理论；(2) 证明了软到硬的变分收敛；(3) 证明了随机提升空间的可测性和完备性；(4) 得到了经典Wasserstein和有序几何的约化；(5) 构建了有序测地线、约束重心和投影；(6) 建立了条件风险-传输对偶性；(7) 实现了序违例分布的分离；(8) 核心稳定性定理：随机学习动力学可以在环境Wasserstein度量下收敛，而局部允许性泄漏遵循独立的条件序-风险递归，最终形成渐近序-风险下限。

Conclusion: CROTS为理解证据过度推断（evidence overreach）、有序分布偏移（ordered distribution shift）、鲁棒性失效（robustness failure）和允许分布动力学（admissible distributional dynamics）提供了数学语言。该框架区别于锥值度量、有序Kantorovich构造、随机Wasserstein空间和生成路径的模型特定残差等方法，是分布学习中可靠性的基础空间理论。

Abstract: A small Wasserstein distance does not certify that a transformation is admissible. In evidence-constrained, semantic, causal, physical, monotone, or risk-sensitive learning, one must ask not only how far two probability laws are, but whether mass has moved in a direction allowed by available information. We introduce conditional random ordered transport spaces (CROTS), a class of \(L^0\)-valued spaces of random probability measures equipped with a Wasserstein ambient metric, a closed stochastic order, hard and soft ordered transport discrepancies, and a conditional risk functional for evaluating order violation under an evidence sigma-field. The central object is an order-admissible transport geometry for random measure-valued dynamics, distinct from cone-valued metrics, ordered Kantorovich constructions, random Wasserstein spaces alone, and model-specific residuals for generative paths. We develop the foundations of CROTS as a space theory for reliable distributional learning. The results include well-posedness and duality for hard and soft ordered transport, soft-to-hard variational convergence, measurability and completeness of the random lifted space, reductions to classical Wasserstein and ordered geometries, ordered geodesics, constrained barycenters and projections, conditional risk-transport duality, and separation of order-violating distributions. The main stability theorem shows that random learning dynamics may converge in the ambient Wasserstein metric while its local admissibility leakage follows a separate conditional order-risk recursion. The resulting asymptotic order-risk floor provides a mathematical language for evidence overreach, ordered distribution shift, robustness failure, and admissible distributional dynamics.

</details>


### [109] [TRUST-SCF: Transformer-based Risk Understanding and Scoring for Transactional Supply Chain Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08140)
*Mohammadamin Davoodabadi,Amirabbas Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的供应链金融信用评分框架TRUST-SCF，通过交易级风险预测和动态信用评分，在30万+真实交易数据上优于序列基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链金融和LendTech平台需要能响应交易行为演变、还款延迟和暴露风险的信用评分系统，现有方法无法有效处理交易级动态风险。

Method: 将用户交易历史建模为包含利用率、还款延迟和交易位置信息的token序列，采用Transformer架构。核心创新：(1)结合利用率相似性和时效性的金融对齐注意力偏置；(2)对数变换空间中的连续还款延迟预测；(3)基于预测延迟、模拟利用率风险、未偿还暴露和非线性校准推导信用分数，无需外部信用评分标签。

Result: 在30万+笔真实交易数据上，TRUST-SCF在延迟预测任务上优于序列基线，生成的信用分数与未来还款行为强相关。

Conclusion: TRUST-SCF是适用于SCF和LendTech环境的自适应信用评分和交易级风险缓解的实用框架。

Abstract: Supply Chain Finance (SCF) and LendTech platforms need credit scoring systems that respond to evolving transaction behavior, repayment delays, and active exposure. We propose TRUST-SCF, a transformer-based framework for transaction-level risk prediction and dynamic credit scoring. Each user history is represented as a sequence of transaction tokens containing utilization, repayment delay and transaction position. The main contributions are: (1) a financially aligned attention bias that combines utilization similarity and recency, enabling the model to compare repayment behavior under comparable exposure conditions; (2) continuous repayment-delay prediction in a log-transformed target space, reducing the influence of extreme delays while improving sensitivity to short-delay behavior and (3) a label-efficient credit-scoring pipeline in which the final credit score is not trained using any explicit external credit-score label, but is instead derived from predicted delay, potential risk over simulated utilization, actual unpaid exposure, and nonlinear calibration. Experiments on real transaction data from more than 300,000 transactions show that TRUST-SCF improves delay prediction over sequential baselines and produces scores that are strongly associated with future repayment behavior. These results suggest that TRUST-SCF is a practical framework for adaptive credit scoring and transaction-level risk mitigation in SCF and LendTech environments.

</details>


### [110] [LogNEO: A GPT-Neo Reinforcement Learning Framework for Accurate Real-Time Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08153)
*David Eje,Tanmay Sharma,Khush Patel,Manuel Mazzara,Leonard Johard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LogNEO 是一个基于 GPT-Neo (1.3B) 的日志异常检测器，采用部分信用、指数衰减的位置感知奖励机制结合 PPO 和交叉熵正则化进行微调。在 HDFS、BGL 和 Thunderbird 基准上分别取得 0.927、0.913 和 0.984 的 F1 分数，召回率相比 LogGPT 提升最多 6 个百分点，并在生产级微服务部署中实现 45ms 端到端延迟和 15K events/s 吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模系统日志中的异常检测对于现代计算基础设施的可靠性和安全性至关重要，现有方法（如 LogGPT）在召回率上仍有提升空间。

Method: 基于 EleutherAI 的 GPT-Neo (1.3B 参数) 构建，提出一种新颖的部分信用、指数衰减的位置感知奖励方案，结合交叉熵正则化，通过 Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) 进行微调。位置感知奖励显式建模预测难度：早期位置正确预测获得更高奖励，后期位置错误预测受到更强惩罚。

Result: 在 HDFS、BGL 和 Thunderbird 基准上分别达到 0.927、0.913 和 0.984 的 F1 分数；召回率相比先前最优方法 LogGPT 提升最多 6 个百分点，同时保持相当的精确度。生产级微服务部署（Apache Kafka、Redis、TensorRT 加速推理）实现 45ms 端到端延迟和 15,000 events/s 吞吐量。

Conclusion: LogNEO 通过位置感知奖励机制有效提升了日志序列异常检测的召回率，同时在准确率和推理效率上表现出色，具备实际生产部署能力。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in large-scale system logs is critical for the reliability and security of modern computing infrastructure. We present LogNEO, a log anomaly detector built on EleutherAI's GPT-Neo (1.3B parameters) and fine-tuned with a novel partial-credit, exponentially decaying position-aware reward scheme combined with cross-entropy regularisation via Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO). The position-aware reward explicitly models prediction difficulty: early positions receive higher rewards for correct predictions, while later positions incur stronger penalties for errors. LogNEO attains F1-scores of 0.927, 0.913, and 0.984 on the HDFS, BGL, and Thunderbird benchmarks, improving recall by up to 6 percentage points over the prior state-of-the-art LogGPT while maintaining comparable precision. A production microservice deployment over Apache Kafka, Redis, and TensorRT-accelerated inference demonstrates 45 ms end-to-end latency at 15,000 events per second.

</details>


### [111] [Have I Solved This Before? Retrieving Similar Segmentation Problems for Evolutionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08155)
*Andreas Margraf,Henning Cui,Jörg Hähner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种偏离传统监控系统开发流程的研究方向，通过将注意力从算法设计转向对检测问题的深入分析，逐步收集知识并存储在抽象系统模型中，实现跨领域迁移学习，避免昂贵的模型从头训练，并允许对现有基础配置进行增量优化，从而降低后期修订的风险和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监控系统开发流程中，传感器类型和系统架构的设计决策需在早期且高度不确定的情况下做出，且算法设计主导的范式导致后期修订成本高昂。缺乏对跨领域滤波器流水线可迁移性的系统研究，亟需探索解决方案复用机制以减少重复训练开销。

Method: 提出知识逐步收集与抽象系统模型存储的方法，支持相似解决方案的检索与复用；通过跨领域迁移学习分析滤波器流水线的可迁移性，并统计评估该变体在图像分割问题上的效果；引入简单模型来平衡复杂性、技术需求和可靠性之间的权衡。

Result: 研究表明，通过检索和迁移现有的滤波器流水线到不同但相似的分割问题，可以有效避免昂贵的模型从头训练；统计分析了迁移学习变体在图像分割中的优势；简单模型有助于在设计过程中平衡复杂度、技术需求与可靠性。

Conclusion: 偏离传统开发流程、转向知识积累与系统模型复用的方向是可行的，能够降低后期修订风险与成本，跨领域滤波器流水线的迁移具有实际应用价值，简单模型在系统设计中的权衡作用值得重视。

Abstract: Reliable integration and solid configuration of monitoring systems constitute a fundamental prerequisites for achieving high efficiency and productivity in contemporary manufacturing environments. Design decisions on sensor type and system architecture have to be made at an early stage and under comparably high uncertainty. This work investigates a research direction that deviates from the traditional monitoring-system development process by shifting the attention from algorithm design to a deeper analysis of the inspection problem. In contrast to traditional design cycles, this paper proposes to gradually collect knowledge and store it in an abstract system model. This enables the retrieval of similar solutions for future use cases, preventing the need for expensive model training from scratch and allowing instead for the incremental refinement of existing base configurations. Reuse of previously generated pipelines reduces the risk of late and costly revisions. As there is little knowledge on cross-domain transferability of filter pipelines, this study analyzes the potential of retrieving filter pipelines to transfer them to different but similar segmentation problems. Finally, we statistically analyze the benefits of this `transfer learning' variant which is predominantly applied to image segmentation problems. In addition, we discuss how simple models help balancing the trade-off between complexity, technical requirements, and reliability in the design process.

</details>


### [112] [AttentionCap: Transformer Based Capacitance Matrix Learning Toward Full-Chip Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08161)
*Jiechen Huang,Hector R. Rodriguez,Dingcheng Yang,Zuochang Ye,Yibo Lin,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AttentionCap，一种基于Transformer的电容矩阵学习模型。通过Gram表示框架、物理对齐的对称注意力输出层和归一化拉普拉斯损失，结合工艺节点嵌入实现多节点学习。在多层多节点设置下，对未见真实设计的自电容/耦合电容误差仅0.67%/3.99%，相比CNN-Cap降低4.6×/5.7×误差且推理速度提升192×，预训练模型仅需5K样本和4K微调步即可迁移至新节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的模式匹配在先进工艺节点下电容提取精度难以维持，而现有的MLP和CNN方法受限于固定金属层组合和特定工艺节点，实用性受限。

Method: 提出AttentionCap：1）Gram表示框架将电容矩阵转化为适合Transformer处理的形式；2）物理对齐的对称注意力输出层保证预测符合物理对称性；3）归一化拉普拉斯损失函数；4）工艺节点嵌入机制支持多节点学习。

Result: 在多层多节点设置下，自电容误差0.67%，耦合电容误差3.99%；相比CNN-Cap，自/耦合误差降低4.6×/5.7×，推理速度提升192×。预训练模型仅需5K样本和4K微调步即可迁移至新工艺节点。

Conclusion: AttentionCap在未见真实设计上具有足够精度，且对新工艺节点具备强迁移能力，为现代EDA工作流提供了高实用价值的电容提取方案。

Abstract: As capacitance extraction accuracy of rule-based pattern matching becomes difficult to sustain at advanced nodes, a growing trend emerges to develop deep-learning-based 2D capacitance models. However, existing MLP- and CNN-based methods constrain their input to fixed metal-layer combinations in a specific process node, limiting their usability in practice. Recognizing the inherent similarity between capacitance matrix and the prevailing attention mechanism, we propose AttentionCap, a customized Transformer for capacitance matrix learning, with a Gram representation framework, a physics-aligned symmetric-attention output layer, and a novel normalized Laplacian loss. We also introduce a process-node embedding to enable multi-node learning. Trained on synthetic data, AttentionCap attains 0.67\%/3.99\% self/coupling-capacitance error on unseen real designs under a multi-layer and multi-node setting, surpassing the CNN-Cap baseline with 4.6$\times$/5.7$\times$ lower self/coupling error and 192$\times$ faster inference speed. A pretrained AttentionCap accurately transfers to an unseen node with only 5K samples and 4K finetuning steps. With sufficient accuracy on unseen real designs and strong transferability to new process nodes, AttentionCap offers highly practical value for modern EDA workflows. Code and data are available at https://github.com/THU-numbda/AttentionCap.

</details>


### [113] [Explaining Data Mixing Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08167)
*Rui Dai,Shuran Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架来解释多领域数据混合的缩放定律，基于领域间共享基础技能、分化专业技能的分布假设，识别出容量竞争和噪声减少两个关键因素，在更少参数下实现了比经验缩放定律更优的拟合和跨规模外推性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究建立了多领域数据混合的经验缩放定律来预测模型性能，但缺乏对这些损失行为的理论理解。

Method: 将标准神经缩放定律（如Kaplan和Chinchilla）的理论视角扩展到多领域设置，基于领域在基础技能上重叠、在专业技能上分化的分布假设，提出容量竞争（有限模型容量在全局上耦合领域损失）和噪声减少（最优权重偏向更难学习的领域以最小化整体噪声）两个关键机制。

Result: 该框架在拟合损失景观方面比现有基线具有更低的平均相对误差，能够识别更高性能的训练混合比例，并且成功实现了跨规模外推——使用小规模参数拟合即可预测未见的大规模上的高效混合策略，同时使用的参数远少于之前的经验定律。

Conclusion: 本文提出的理论框架有效解释了多领域数据混合的缩放行为，在拟合精度、跨规模外推能力和参数效率三个维度上均优于现有的经验方法。

Abstract: Recent research has established empirical scaling laws to predict model performance on multi-domain data mixtures. However, a theoretical understanding of these model loss behaviors remains absent. In this work, we propose a unified framework to explain the underlying mechanics of data mixing. Our approach extends theoretical perspectives originally developed for standard neural scaling laws (e.g., Kaplan and Chinchilla) to the multi-domain setting. Based on the distributional assumption that domains overlap on fundamental skills while diverging on specialized skills, we identify two key factors that govern the domain losses of models trained on different data mixtures: \textit{Capacity Competition}, where the allocation of finite model capacity couples domain losses globally, and \textit{Noise Reduction}, where optimal weights shift toward harder-to-learn domains to minimize overall noise. Empirical evaluations show that our framework outperforms existing baselines by fitting the loss landscape with a lower Mean Relative Error and identifying higher-performing training mixtures. Most importantly, our model successfully extrapolates across scales, predicting highly effective mixtures for large, unseen scales using parameters fitted on smaller ones. In addition, our model achieves these results using significantly fewer parameters compared to previous empirical laws. Our code is available at https://github.com/meiqwq/Explaining-Data-Mixing-Scaling-Laws.

</details>


### [114] [Frequency-Domain Latent Attention Gating for Cross-Domain Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08191)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLaG是一个基于快速傅里叶变换的可插拔聚合模块，通过频域变换、可学习潜查询和通道门控机制改进token聚合，在抗菌肽活性预测、图像分类和文本分类任务上取得竞争力，并在抗菌肽任务上进行了深入的频域行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有池化方法仅在原始token域操作，token聚合成为从token表示映射到样本级预测的瓶颈，缺乏利用频域信息进行更有效聚合的手段。

Method: 1) 使用实FFT将token表示变换到频域；2) 通过可学习的潜查询(latent queries)汇总频谱成分；3) 应用通道级门控(channel-wise gate)；4) 重构增强的时域token用于最终池化。

Result: 在ESM2-8M抗菌肽任务和CIFAR-100上取得最明显增益，在IMDB和GLUE上与强文本基线相当。进一步分析发现：低频带贡献最大，高频模式更具样本特异性；门控充当广泛共享的频谱重加权阶段；交叉注意模式具有样本特异性且查询间分化温和；高螺旋肽在两类细菌中均表现出更强的平均频谱敏感性。

Conclusion: FLaG通过频域变换的策略有效改进了token聚合，尤其适用于序列级别预测任务，并通过频域分析揭示了模型行为的可解释性规律。

Abstract: Token aggregation is a common bottleneck in models that map token representations to sample-level predictions, yet most pooling methods operate only in the original token domain. We propose FLaG, a plug-in aggregation module that transforms token representations with the real FFT, summarizes spectral components with learnable latent queries, applies a channel-wise gate, and reconstructs enhanced time-domain tokens for final pooling. We evaluate FLaG on antimicrobial peptide (AMP) activity prediction with ESM2, image classification with ResNet18 on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and text classification with RoBERTa on IMDB and GLUE. FLaG achieves its clearest gains on the ESM2-8M antimicrobial peptide tasks and on CIFAR-100, while remaining competitive with strong text baselines on IMDB and GLUE. Then we probe its behavior on the AMP setting with band knockouts, gate summaries, residue perturbations, latent-query readouts, and structure-proxy stratification. We find that low-frequency bands contribute the most overall, and the remaining higher-band pattern is more sample-specific. The gate acts as a broadly shared spectral reweighting stage and the cross-attention patterns are sample-specific with mild query-wise differentiation, and higher-helix peptides exhibit stronger average spectral sensitivity in both bacteria. The supplementary materials, source code and data are released at https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/ and https://github.com/Kewei2023/AMPCliff/tree/FLaG.

</details>


### [115] [Neural Field Tokenizations with Hierarchy and Spatial Locality Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08204)
*Alonso Urbano,David W. Romero,Max Zimmer,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LH-NeF框架，利用局部性和层次性先验，通过前馈编码代替元学习内循环，实现无模态特定假设的通用神经场表示学习，在图像、3D形状和气候场任务中性能优异且内存效率提升42倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场表示学习方法中，基于每个样本的元学习方法因内循环优化导致内存开销大、扩展性差；而前馈编码方法又常引入模态特定假设，牺牲了神经场的通用性。因此需要一种既高效又保持模态无关性的通用表示学习方案。

Method: 提出LH-NeF框架，包含一个保持局部性的层次化编码器，将原始坐标-值场观测映射为结构化token，再从中重建场。前馈方式替代了元学习的内循环，无需内存密集的优化过程。

Result: 相比最强的模态无关基线，LH-NeF内存使用减少42倍，支持133倍更大的批次；在图像、3D形状和气候场数据上，重建和下游任务性能匹配或超越模态无关、模态特定及专门化生成神经场基线。

Conclusion: 局部性和层次性是在不牺牲模态无关性的前提下提升神经场表示学习效率的有效先验，LH-NeF实现了高效且通用的连续信号表示学习。

Abstract: Neural fields parameterize data as functions from coordinates to values, providing a unified framework for representation learning across modalities. Existing approaches are dominated by per-sample meta-learning, which scales poorly due to memory-intensive inner-loop optimization. The natural alternative -- feed-forward encoding -- typically introduces modality-specific assumptions, sacrificing the generality that makes learning with neural fields attractive. We argue that locality and hierarchy are useful priors for learning field representations that can be injected without compromising modality-agnosticism. We propose LH-NeF, a framework to learn general-purpose tokenized representations of continuous signals. A locality-preserving hierarchical encoder maps raw coordinate-value field observations to structured tokens, from which the field is reconstructed during training. By replacing meta-learning's inner loop with a single forward pass, LH-NeF uses 42$\times$ less memory and supports 133$\times$ larger batches than the strongest modality-agnostic baseline. Across images, 3D shapes, and climate fields, our learned representations match or exceed performance of modality-agnostic, modality-specific, and specialized generative neural field baselines on both reconstruction and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [116] [Public Machine Learning Solver Framework for Novices in the Machine Learning Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08212)
*Lokman Saleh,Hafedh Mili,Mounir Boukadoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合专家 cheat sheets 和决策支持系统的半自动化平台，为非专家用户提供完整的机器学习流水线推荐，而非单一算法。平台集成迁移学习、自动数据特征提取、一阶逻辑推理，并通过众包持续更新知识库。这是首个免费、公开、系统化捕获并操作化专家知识以指导非专家解决ML问题的在线框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习问题解决通常需要专家知识，现有系统主要分为三类：全自动化AutoML、专家cheat sheets和决策支持系统。但这些方法要么推荐单一算法，要么不够用户友好，缺乏为非专家提供完整、结构化、可解释的解决方案推荐的能力。

Method: 平台结合类别2和3，采用半自动化方式：集成专家定义的选择准则与迁移学习；自动从用户提供的数据集中提取数据特征（如类别不平衡、缺失值）；使用一阶逻辑基于知识库进行推理；按相关性对推荐算法排序；连接众包平台实现持续更新；支持增量式构建以整合新算法、准则和领域知识。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首个免费、公开可访问的在线框架，能够系统地捕获并操作化专家知识，以结构化、透明的方式指导非专家解决ML问题。

Conclusion: 该平台通过结合专家知识和自动化推理，有效降低了非专家使用机器学习的门槛，提供了一个完整流水线推荐、用户友好且可持续更新的解决方案。

Abstract: Solving machine learning problems is complex and typically reserved for experts. Over the past two decades, systems have emerged to support non-experts. Based on our review, we identify three categories: (1) fully automated AutoML systems, (2) expert cheat sheets for algorithm selection, and (3) decision-support systems using selection criteria (accuracy, transparency, data requirements). We propose a new platform combining categories 2 and 3 to deliver semi-automated, intelligent solution recommendations for non-experts. Unlike existing approaches that recommend a single algorithm, our platform suggests a complete pipeline tailored to the user's problem. It integrates expert-defined selection criteria with transfer learning and automatically extracts data characteristics (e.g., class imbalance, missing values) from user-provided datasets. The platform uses first-order logic to reason over its knowledge base and recommends suitable algorithms ranked by relevance. It features a user-friendly interface and connects to a crowdsourcing platform for ML experts, ensuring continuous updates. The platform is built incrementally, allowing seamless integration of new algorithms, criteria, and domain knowledge. To our knowledge, this is the first free, publicly accessible online framework that systematically captures and operationalizes expert knowledge to guide non-experts in solving ML problems in a structured, transparent manner.

</details>


### [117] [How Deep Are Deep GPs, Really? A Sharp Threshold and a Non-Gaussian Limit for Compositional GPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08218)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度高斯过程先验在深度趋于无穷时的极限行为，发现了RBF核带宽的尖锐阈值 $r_c(d) = Θ(\sqrt{d})$：高于该阈值时先验退化为常数函数（退化态），低于该阈值时先验收敛到非退化、非高斯的极限分布 $\pi_{\bar{Z}}$，且该分布具有坐标间非零依赖和多模态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度贝叶斯模型中组合先验（compositional priors）在深度增长时的极限行为，特别是突破以往仅关注高斯过程核退化（常数函数）的局限性，探索是否存在非平凡、非退化的极限分布。

Method: 理论分析：推导RBF核带宽的尖锐阈值 $r_c(d) = Θ(\sqrt{d})$，证明阈值上下两种截然不同的极限行为；数值实验：在不同维度 $d$ 下验证阈值，并可视化极限分布 $\pi_{\bar{Z}}$ 的多模态特性。

Result: 1) 确定了尖锐阈值 $r_c(d) = Θ(\sqrt{d})$，改进了此前较松的上下界；2) 在阈值以下，先验收敛到非退化、非高斯的极限分布 $\pi_{\bar{Z}}$，且坐标间依赖非零；3) 实验验证了阈值的存在，并发现极限分布在 $d$ 增大时多模态区域变窄。

Conclusion: 深度高斯过程先验在深度趋于无穷时可以存在非平凡极限，打破了以往认为只有退化极限的传统认知，为深度贝叶斯模型的先验设计提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: Compositional priors describe the generic properties of layered functions in deep Bayesian models, where deep neural networks with random weights are a canonical example.In the wide-network limit, the prior is a Gaussian process with a depth-dependent kernel, and its behaviour as depth grows has been extensively studied through this kernel. Here, we study another case, where each layer itself is a vector valued Gaussian process, and our aim is similarly to understand the limiting behaviour of the prior as depth grows.
  Previous GP work has established that for the RBF kernel and a certain range of bandwidths $r$, the prior degenerates in the limit, converging to the set of constant functions -- which is not useful as a probabilistic model. In this paper we establish several new results. First, we identify a sharp bandwidth threshold $r_c(d) = Θ(\sqrt{d})$ above which the limit is degenerate, strengthening the earlier bounds. Second, and more importantly, we show that for $r$ below the threshold $r_c(d)$ the prior converges to a limit distribution $π_{\bar{Z}}$. We also prove that these distributions are non-degenerate and non-Gaussian, with non-vanishing dependence between coordinates. In contrast to the previously known degenerate regime, deep Gaussian process priors can therefore admit non-trivial limits.
  Empirically, we verify the threshold across a range of dimensions $d$, and demonstrate a complex multimodal behaviour of the limit distributions $π_{\bar{Z}}$ -- a regime that becomes increasingly narrow with $d$ and would be hard to identify without knowing the threshold.

</details>


### [118] [De novo molecular generation with optical property preconditioning at the token level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08221)
*Haozhe Huang,Manuel Gonzalez Lastre,Hyun Suk Park,Jorge A. Campos-Gonzalez-Angulo,Xinjian Liu,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在低数据场景下，使用带有离散属性标记的GPT2模型进行OLED分子生成，并通过TDDFT评估发现：模型能复现光学性质分布，但可控性在不同化学基序中存在显著差异，尤其是吸电子基团（如芳基腈）表现出系统性红移和可控性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计具有目标光学性质的OLED分子仍面临挑战，主要原因在于高质量数据稀缺以及生成模型中基于化学基序的条件控制可靠性不足。

Method: 采用标记条件自回归语言模型（GPT2），在大规模化学语料上预训练，加入离散属性标记，通过多任务优化微调。条件控制目标为垂直吸收能和振荡强度，辅助电子描述符为HOMO-LUMO能隙，生成分子通过TDDFT进行评估。

Result: 生成的分子库能复现训练分布的主要光学性质，但分子量更低、重原子更少。标记级控制具有方向一致性但非完全正交，存在局部校准不规则性。基于化学型的分析表明，中等共轭芳香碳基序可改善目标联合满足度，而吸电子基序（尤其是芳基腈）则表现出系统性红移和可控性降低。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了条件性OLED分子生成的定量基准，并指出模型可靠性需在化学上有意义的子空间而非仅从整体属性分布进行评估。

Abstract: Designing OLED molecules with targeted optical properties remains challenging due to the scarcity of high-quality data and the limited reliability of conditional control in generative models across chemical motifs. Here, we benchmark a token-conditioned autoregressive language model for OLED molecular generation in a realistic low-data regime. A GPT2 model is pretrained on large chemical corpora, augmented with discrete property tokens, and fine-tuned using multi-task optimisation. Conditioning targets vertical absorption energy and oscillator strength, with the HOMO-LUMO gap included as an auxiliary electronic descriptor.
  Generated molecules are evaluated at the TDDFT level to assess distributional fidelity and controllability. The generated library reproduces the dominant optical-property support of the training distribution while shifting towards lower molecular weight and fewer heavy atoms. Token-level control is consistently directional across conditioning bins, but is not fully orthogonal and exhibits local calibration irregularities. A chemotype-resolved analysis further shows that controllability depends strongly on local electronic environments: moderately conjugated aromatic-carbon motifs are associated with improved joint target satisfaction, whereas electron-withdrawing motifs, particularly aryl nitriles, show systematic red-shifting and reduced controllability.
  These results establish a quantitative benchmark for conditional OLED molecular generation and show that model reliability must be assessed in chemically meaningful subspaces rather than from aggregate property distributions alone.

</details>


### [119] [GPT-Micro: A large language paradigm for accelerated, inexpensive, and thermodynamics-consistent discovery of constitutive models in manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08238)
*Soumik Dutta,Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Sania Shree,Logan McNeil,Thomas Feldhausen,Hongyi Xu,Rajiv Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GPT-Micro框架，一种结合大语言模型、热力学守恒定律和稀疏数据集的自主本构模型发现方法，在印刷电子工艺测试中实现了70%以上的数据需求降低、400倍的发现时间缩短，并生成了符合热力学定律的全新解析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构模型发现依赖人工经验和直觉，效率低且进展缓慢；常规机器学习需要大量数据生成，成本高昂；现有基于LLM的模型发现方法忽视了热力学基本定律的不可违背性。亟需一种既能利用LLM语义能力又严格遵守物理定律的数据高效模型发现方法。

Method: GPT-Micro框架无缝集成三个核心组件：(1)从文献中提取语义知识；(2)强制执行基于热力学的守恒定律；(3)利用稀疏数据集，由LLM驱动模型假设的生成与迭代优化。

Result: 在印刷电子工艺测试平台上：(a)相比ML方法数据负担降低超70%而不损失精度；(b)相比人工驱动建模，数据生成后发现时间从数月缩短至数小时（400倍加速）；(c)发现了具有全新函数形式的模型，无需人为选择初始假设；(d)生成了紧凑、守恒合规、物理完整的解析模型，增强了物理可信度、可解释性和机理洞察。

Conclusion: GPT-Micro框架有望在制造领域实现快速、低成本、物理可信且可解释的微观结构建模，为自主科学发现开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling of the relationship between process-imposed material states and fundamental material properties is critical to control of material microstructure in manufacturing processes. The limited accuracy resulting from the typical reliance on fallible human expertise and intuition for postulation and revision of the models functional form results in incremental and time consuming model discovery. Conventional Machine Learning (ML) incurs significant cost and time of data generation. Model discovery using Large Language Models (LLMs) suffers from the above issues and/or ignores the inviolability of fundamental thermodynamics laws. This work creates a novel GPT-Micro paradigm for autonomous, data sparse, and thermodynamics-compliant discovery of de-novo constitutive models. This framework seamlessly integrates semantic knowledge extraction from literature, enforcement of thermodynamics-based conservation laws, and sparse datasets, with LLM-driven generation and refinement of model hypotheses. Validation is performed for a long-intractable constitutive modeling problem in a printed electronics process testbed. This reveals significant and simultaneous advantages over the state-of-the-art including: (a) More than 70 percent reduction in data burden relative to ML-based modeling without loss in accuracy; (b) 400X reduction in discovery time after data generation, from months to hours, relative to human-driven modeling; (c) Discovery of models with novel functional forms without subjective human choice of a starting hypothesis; (d) Enhanced physics-rooted trustworthiness, human interpretability, and mechanistic insight via synthesis of compact, conservation-compliant, and physically complete analytical models. The potential of GPT-Micro to realize rapid, low-cost, physically trustworthy, and interpretable microstructure modeling across the manufacturing landscape is discussed.

</details>


### [120] [Differentially Private Synthetic Data via APIs 4: Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08259)
*Toan Tran,Arturs Backurs,Zinan Lin,Victor Reis,Li Xiong,Sergey Yekhanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tab-PE，将私有进化（PE）框架扩展到表格数据生成，通过专用算子、隐私评分和样本选择，在差分隐私约束下高效生成高质量合成表格数据，尤其在高阶相关数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP表格数据合成方法主要关注低阶边际查询误差，忽略了高阶相关性带来的挑战，亟需一种能处理高阶相关性的高效方法。

Method: 将Private Evolution（PE）框架从图像/文本扩展到表格数据，提出Tab-PE算法。通过表格专用启发式算子迭代生成变异、私有评分并选择高质量样本进行传播，无需依赖大型基础模型，计算成本低。

Result: 在具有高阶相关性的真实和模拟数据集上，Tab-PE显著优于先前基线；相比最佳基线AIM，分类准确率提升高达10%，运行速度快28倍。

Conclusion: Tab-PE使PE框架在表格数据上变得实用且可扩展，能有效处理高阶相关性，在效果和效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of generating synthetic tabular data with differential privacy (DP) guarantees, enabling data sharing in sensitive domains. Despite extensive study, state-of-the-art methods often focus on minimizing low-order marginal query errors and overlook the challenges posed by high-order correlations. To address this gap, we extend the Private Evolution (PE) framework, originally developed for DP-compliant image and text synthesis, to tabular data. We introduce Tab-PE -- an algorithm for synthetic tabular data generation under DP constraints. Tab-PE iteratively improves a candidate dataset via an evolutionary process that leverages tabular-specialized operators to produce variations, privately scores them, and selects the highest-quality samples to retain and propagate. In contrast to the original PE, which relies on large foundation models, Tab-PE employs heuristic operators with significantly lower computational costs, making PE more practical and scalable for tabular data. Through extensive experiments on real-world and simulation datasets, we demonstrate that Tab-PE substantially outperforms prior baselines on datasets exhibiting high-order correlations. Compared to the best baseline -- AIM, Tab-PE improves classification accuracy by up to 10% while running 28 times faster.

</details>


### [121] [Causal Semantic Alignment for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08262)
*Kexuan Zhang,Xiaobei Zou,Cesare Alippi,Gary G. Yen,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CVAformer，一种针对时间序列预测的变量级对齐框架，通过将每个变量显式解耦为不变成分和动态成分，并对动态成分施加因果干预以消除混杂效应，同时用非因果注意力机制替代LLM中的标准因果注意力来支持变量级交互。实验表明该方法在长短期、少样本和零样本预测中达到了SOTA或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法忽视了时间序列的异质性，其中动态波动与不变语义纠缠在一起，导致对齐过程中动态分量作为混杂因子引入虚假相关，影响模型泛化能力。

Method: CVAformer提出变量级对齐框架，在LLM对齐前将每个变量显式解耦为不变成分和动态成分，并对动态成分施加因果干预以消除其对对齐的混杂效应；同时用非因果注意力机制替代标准因果注意力，以捕捉各时间步上变量间的交互关系。

Result: 在长周期、短周期、少样本和零样本预测的多个数据集上，CVAformer达到或超越了现有SOTA方法，部分数据集上取得了显著更优的预测精度。

Conclusion: 实验验证了变量级对齐和动态解耦在CVAformer中的有效性，为基于LLM的时间序列任务提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened new possibilities for time series forecasting by enabling alignment between temporal patterns and pretrained word embeddings. However, most LLM-based methods overlook the heterogeneous nature of time series, where dynamic fluctuations and invariant semantics are entangled. This entanglement introduces spurious correlations during the alignment, as dynamic components act as confounders by simultaneously influencing invariant components and the resulting aligned embeddings. To address this issue, a variable-level alignment framework CVAformer is proposed. CVAformer explicitly disentangles each variable into invariant and dynamic components just before alignment, and applies causal intervention to mitigate the confounding effect of the dynamics. To better support variable-level alignment, CVAformer replaces the standard causal attention in LLMs with a non-causal attention mechanism that captures interactions among variables at each time step. Extensive experiments across long-term, short-term, few-shot, and zero-shot forecasting settings indicate that CVAformer matches or exceeds state-of-the-art performance on most datasets, and in some cases achieves notably better accuracy. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of variable-level alignment and dynamic disentanglement in CVAformer, offering a new perspective for LLM-based time series tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Causal Agent Replay: Counterfactual Attribution for LLM-Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08275)
*Jaineet Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果智能体回放（CAR），通过结构因果模型和干预操作，精准定位LLM智能体故障的根本步骤，而非仅仅描述故障现象或评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具只能回答智能体故障中"发生了什么"（可观测性）或"是否通过"（评估），但无法定位导致故障的具体步骤。直观启发式方法错误：执行有害动作的步骤通常不是做出决策的步骤，且LLM裁判归因相关性强但不可靠（SOTA步骤级准确率仅约14%）。

Method: 将智能体运行建模为结构因果模型（SCM），对步骤施加do-操作干预，在相同随机策略下前向重新执行轨迹，测量结果分布偏移。定义了干预代数、单步对比估计器（包含解决随机前向特定混杂的承诺点规则），以及预算有界的蒙特卡洛Shapley估计器（用于跨交互步骤的信用分配），所有效果均报告置信区间。

Result: 在合成结构因果模型上验证：对比估计器能恢复关键步骤，Shapley估计器能恢复两步交互（系数0.44, 0.45, ~0；效率总和0.909，解析解为0.91）。

Conclusion: CAR通过因果干预精确定位LLM智能体故障的根本步骤，优于现有归因方法，且开源、可运行于托管或免费本地模型。

Abstract: When an LLM agent fails -- issues a refund it should not have, calls the wrong tool, leaks data -- existing tooling answers what happened (observability) or whether it passed (evaluation), but not which step caused the failure. The obvious heuristics are wrong: the step that executes the harmful action is usually not the step that decided on it, and LLM-judge attribution is correlational and unreliable (state-of-the-art step-level accuracy on the Who&When benchmark is about 14%). We present Causal Agent Replay (CAR), which answers the question by intervention: it models an agent run as a structural causal model, applies a do-operation to a step, and re-executes the trajectory forward under the same stochastic policy, measuring the shift in the outcome distribution. We define an intervention algebra over agent steps, a single-step contrastive estimator whose point-of-commitment rule resolves a confound specific to stochastic run-forward, and a budget-bounded Monte-Carlo Shapley estimator that splits credit across interacting steps. Every effect is reported with confidence intervals. We validate against synthetic structural causal models with planted ground truth: the contrastive estimator recovers the pivotal step, and Shapley recovers a two-step interaction (0.44, 0.45, ~0; efficiency sum 0.909 versus the analytic 0.91). CAR is open source and runs on hosted or free local models.

</details>


### [123] [Mesh Graph Neural Network Framework for Accelerating Finite Element Simulation for Arbitrary Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08287)
*Josiah D. Kunz,Kamal Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于网格图网络（MGN）的代理模型，用于预测具有任意孔洞几何的2D结构件的von Mises应力场。该模型利用节点类型、相对边特征和全局载荷特征构建平移和旋转不变的图结构，在未见几何和载荷条件下R²≥0.97，显著优于传统机器学习模型（R²≈0.01-0.86）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析计算成本高，尤其在设计迭代或多载荷场景下；现有机器学习代理模型难以泛化至不同几何形状。

Method: 基于网格图网络（MGN），使用节点类型（固定边界、自由表面、孔洞边缘）、相对边特征（邻居间距离）和全局特征（施加载荷）作为输入，编码平移和旋转不变性，在11种板几何和20种载荷条件下训练。

Result: 在最优情况下，模型在未见几何和载荷上R²≥0.97，而传统模型（随机森林、梯度提升、K近邻）仅达R²≈0.01-0.86。

Conclusion: 图神经网络可作为跨几何泛化的高效有限元分析替代方案，将Pfaff等人的网格仿真框架扩展至结构力学领域。

Abstract: Finite element analysis (FEA) is essential for structural design but remains computationally expensive, particularly when evaluating multiple design iterations or load scenarios. Machine learning surrogate models offer a promising alternative, yet most approaches struggle with a critical limitation: generalizing across varying geometries. This work presents a mesh graph network (MGN) for predicting von Mises stress fields in 2D structural components with arbitrary hole geometries. Unlike traditional machine learning approaches that use absolute node coordinates as features, the proposed model builds on existing MGN frameworks that encode node types (e.g., fixed boundary, free surface, hole edge), relative edge features (distance between neighbors), and global features (applied load). This architecture is inherently translation- and rotation-invariant, enabling generalization to unseen geometries without retraining. The MGN was trained on 11 plate geometries under 20 load conditions and evaluated on 7 unseen geometries and 3 unseen loads. In the most favorable case, the model achieves $R^2 \geq 0.97$ on an unseen geometry and unseen load, compared to $R^2 \approx 0.01$--$0.86$ for conventional models (Random Forest, Gradient Boosting , K-Nearest Neighbors) trained on identical data. However, even in less favorable cases, the MGN model still outperforms conventional models. This work extends the mesh-based simulation framework of Pfaff et al. (arXiv:2010.03409) to structural mechanics, demonstrating that graph neural networks can serve as efficient surrogates for finite element analysis across varying geometries.

</details>


### [124] [On solving symmetric multi-type orthogonal non-negative matrix tri-factorization problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08291)
*Rok Hribar,Gregor Papa,Janez Povh,Andrej Kastrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称多类型正交非负矩阵三分解问题，提出了两种启发式算法（基于KKT条件的定点法和基于ADAM的三阶段法），在合成数据和真实引文网络上验证了其有效性，在链接预测、节点聚类和分类任务上优于或媲美SVD、node2vec等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类和网络分析中需要可解释的因子分解，非负性提升可解释性，正交性赋予因子自然的分配结构，但该优化问题高度非凸，需要有效的求解算法。

Method: 1) 基于KKT条件添加正交惩罚项的定点方法；2) 三阶段ADAM方法：非负保持优化、正交化、可行集上的ADAM精调。

Result: 合成实验表明两种算法能恢复接近最优的分解且在噪声下保持稳定；真实网络上在链接预测、节点聚类和分类任务中优于或媲美SVD、node2vec等基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种启发式算法能有效求解对称多类型正交非负矩阵三分解问题，在多个任务上取得有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: We study the symmetric multi-type orthogonal non-negative matrix tri-factorization problem, where several symmetric non-negative matrices are simultaneously approximated by factors of the form $GS_{i}G^{\top}$, with a shared non-negative and orthogonal factor $G$. This model is motivated by clustering and network analysis, where non-negativity improves interpretability and orthogonality gives a natural assignment-type structure to the latent factor. Since the resulting optimization problem is highly non-convex, we develop two heuristic algorithms for computing high-quality local solutions. The first one is a fixed point method derived from the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions after adding a penalty term for the orthogonality constraint. The second one is a three-stage ADAM-based method that combines non-negativity-preserving optimization, orthogonalization, and restricted ADAM refinement on the feasible set. We evaluate both methods on synthetic data, including noisy instances, and on citation network benchmarks. The synthetic experiments show that both algorithms recover factorizations close to the optimum and remain stable under noise. On real networks, the learned embeddings are competitive with or better than standard baselines such as SVD, node2vec, and classical link prediction heuristics in link prediction, node clustering, and node classification tasks.

</details>


### [125] [QueryWeaver: Reliable Multi-Tool Query Execution Planning via LLM-Based Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08300)
*Aishwarya Chakravarthy,Vidhi Kulkarni,Duen Horng Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将自然语言查询转换为结构化图并通过确定性规划器执行的系统，使用深度优先搜索解决跨工具依赖问题，支持多步、跨工具的复杂查询，即使使用小型或本地LLM也能达到高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多个人数据查询跨越多个应用，需要结构化规划，因为单个工具只暴露部分信息。虽然LLM表现出强大的推理和工具使用能力，但可靠地执行多步、跨工具查询仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 将自然语言查询转换为结构化图，并通过确定性规划器执行。使用深度优先搜索解决依赖关系，跨工具组合结果。

Result: 即使使用较小或本地托管的LLM，系统也能实现高精度。

Conclusion: 所提方法提高了可靠性，支持超越传统关键词搜索的查询，为跨工具复杂查询提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Many real-world queries over personal data span multiple applications and require structured planning, as individual tools expose only partial information. While LLMs show strong reasoning and tool use, reliably executing multi-step, cross-tool queries remains challenging. We introduce a system that converts natural language queries into structured graphs and executes them via a deterministic planner. Our approach uses depth-first search to resolve dependencies and combine results across tools, improving reliability and enabling queries beyond traditional keyword-based search. We demonstrate high accuracy even with smaller or locally hosted LLMs.

</details>


### [126] [GeoGNN: Time Series Geo-Localization using Two-Tower Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08303)
*Toan Tran,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Supriya Chinthavali,Cyrus Shahabi,Li Xiong,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间序列地理定位的新概念，旨在推断每个原始时间序列的地理来源。作者形式化了该问题，借鉴图像地理定位的核心思想建立基线，并提出GeoGNN双塔架构。实验表明，GeoGNN在大规模全国电力消费数据集上平均提升约27%的地理定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据缺乏空间上下文信息，现有研究未系统探索如何从时间序列本身推断其地理来源。成功的地理定位能为时间序列提供空间语境，赋能下游位置感知应用。

Method: 提出GeoGNN双塔架构：(1) 空间塔利用地理邻接图学习地理单元格候选的嵌入表示；(2) 时间塔从时间序列中提取信息性表征。推理时，时间表征与地理嵌入通过点积相似度匹配，结合辅助分类头预测地理来源。

Result: 在大规模全国电力消费数据集上，GeoGNN在所有数据集上均取得最佳性能，细粒度和粗粒度地理定位精度平均提升约27%。

Conclusion: 时间序列地理定位是一个可行且有价值的研究方向，GeoGNN利用图神经网络和双塔架构有效解决了该问题，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates a novel concept of time series geolocalization, where the goal is to infer the geographic origin of each raw time series. Successful geolocalization can provide spatial context to time series, enabling downstream location-aware applications. We formalize the problem, adapt core ideas from image geolocalization to establish strong baselines, and propose GeoGNN, a two-tower architecture. During training, GeoGNN's spatial tower learns embeddings of geographic cell candidates by leveraging the geographic adjacency graph, while the temporal tower extracts informative representations from time series. During inference, each temporal representation is matched against candidate geographic embeddings using dot-product similarity, combined with an auxiliary classification head, to predict the time series' associated geographic origin. Experiments on large-scale, countrywide electricity-consumption datasets demonstrate that GeoGNN achieves the best performance across datasets and enhances both fine- and coarse-grained geolocalization accuracy by ~27% on average.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Graph Foundation Models for Dynamics in Complex Networked Systems: Lessons from Super-Spreader Identification in Multilayer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08306)
*Michał Czuba,Mateusz Stolarski,Adam Piróg,Piotr Bielak,Piotr Bródka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向网络动力学的跨网络归纳泛化方法ts-net，在合成多层网络上训练后，可零样本泛化到真实多层网络，为构建图基础模型（GFM）提供了设计原则和开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前网络动力学（传播、影响力最大化、流行病建模等）主要局限于直推式范式，模型在单一网络上训练后无法复用到未见过的图上。作者认为，实现跨网络的归纳式泛化是构建图基础模型（GFM）的必要前提。

Method: 提出四项设计属性以实现跨网络归纳泛化；作为概念验证，构建ts-net（TopSpreadersNetwork），仅在合成多层网络（MLNs）上训练，实现到真实多层网络的零样本泛化。

Result: ts-net在零样本设置下泛化到不同规模和层数的真实多层网络，在四项指标中的三项上优于经典启发式方法和直推式基线。

Conclusion: 基于ts-net的性能，提出构建网络动力学GFM的五大开放挑战：规模化、多层泛化、自监督预训练、跨任务迁移以及节点属性融合。

Abstract: Network dynamics - including spreading, influence maximisation, and epidemic modelling - remain largely confined to the transductive paradigm, where models are trained on a single network and cannot be reused on unseen graphs without retraining. We argue that inductive cross-network generalisation is a necessary prerequisite for Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) in this domain and propose four design properties towards this goal. As a proof of concept, ts-net (TopSpreadersNetwork), trained solely on synthetic multilayer networks (MLNs), demonstrates zero-shot generalisation to real-world MLNs of varying size and layer count, outperforming classical heuristics and transductive baselines on three of four metrics. Based on ts-net's performance, we further outline five open challenges towards building GFMs for network dynamics: scale, many-layer generalisation, self-supervised pretraining, cross-task transfer, and node-attribute integration.

</details>


### [128] [Fourier fractal dimension to predict the generalization of deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08308)
*Joao B. Florindo,Davi Wanderley Misturini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于傅里叶分形维数的泛化度量，通过分析Lévy驱动随机微分方程的特征函数，从频率域捕捉权重变化的几何复杂度，并设计傅里叶优化器主动正则化该分形维数，在CIFAR-10、SVHN和MNIST数据集上实现与真实泛化差距的高度相关，Kendall秩相关系数达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型参数量巨大，如何在不依赖验证集的情况下准确预测模型泛化性能是核心挑战。现有基于范数、边际和PAC-Bayes的度量方法尚不完善，需要更有效的泛化表征手段。

Method: 1) 基于傅里叶分形维数，分析Lévy驱动随机微分方程的示性函数在频率域的特性，提取度量权重变化几何复杂度的指标；2) 设计傅里叶优化器，在训练过程中主动正则化该分形维数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、SVHN、MNIST三个数据集上，所提度量与真实泛化差距呈现强相关，Kendall秩相关系数超越现有基于范数、边际和PAC-Bayes的各类度量方法，达到最优水平。

Conclusion: 频率域分形分析既能作为预测模型泛化能力的有效指标，也为设计更稳定的优化算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Predicting the generalization performance of deep neural networks without relying on hold-out validation data is a fundamental challenge in machine learning. While Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) drives the optimization of these highly parameterized models, its heavy-tailed, non-Gaussian dynamics induce complex, scale-invariant trajectories in the parameter space. In this paper, we propose a novel generalization measure based on the Fourier fractal dimension of the network's weight variations. By analyzing the characteristic function of the Lévy-driven stochastic differential equations in the frequency domain, we extract a metric that robustly captures the geometric complexity of the learning process. Furthermore, we introduce a customized Fourier-based optimizer designed to actively regularize this fractal dimension during training. Extensive empirical evaluations on the CIFAR-10, SVHN, and MNIST datasets demonstrate that our proposed Fourier generalization measure exhibits a strong correlation with the actual generalization gap. Our method achieves state-of-the-art Kendall rank correlation coefficients, outperforming a wide array of existing norm-based, margin-based, and PAC-Bayesian measures. Ultimately, this work highlights the potential of frequency-domain fractal analysis as both a powerful predictor for model generalizability and a principled foundation for developing more stable optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [129] [Where the Score Lives: A Wavelet View of Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08309)
*Emma Finn,Binxu Wang,T. Anderson Keller,Demba E. Ba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二维正交小波基展开的解析可解得分函数参数化方法，通过数据分布的矩来分析不同架构（如CNN、U-Net、Transformer）在扩散模型中的生成行为差异，为理解得分网络架构选择对生成效果的影响提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CNN、U-Net、Transformer等多种架构已被用于扩散模型的得分近似网络，但关于这些架构选择如何影响生成行为的理解相对有限。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供可解释的理论分析。

Method: 提出一种基于二维正交小波基展开的得分函数解析可解参数化方法，推导出以数据分布矩表示的可解释最优得分函数，实现了与架构无关的基于矩的分析。

Result: 该方法能够部分模仿包括U-Net和CNN在内的多种架构的相关归纳偏置，揭示了数据分布中哪些属性对去噪过程最为重要，展示了不同得分架构为何表现出不同的生成行为。

Conclusion: 通过可解析求解的得分函数，能够从数据分布的矩出发理解数据分布与得分网络之间的交互机制，为解释扩散模型中观察到的生成行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Score-based generative models have had remarkable success over the last decade in generating a diverse set of visually plausible images. A variety of architectures including CNNs, U-Nets, and Transformers have been used as the score-approximation network in such diffusion modeling; however, to date, relatively little is known about how these architectural choices impact generative behavior. In this work, to provide insight into this area, we propose an analytically solvable parameterization of the score function using an expansion in a 2D orthogonal wavelet basis. In particular, we derive interpretable optimal score functions in terms of the moments of the data distribution. We use this parametrization to provide an architecture-agnostic, moment-based analysis that reveals which attributes of the data distribution tend to matter most for denoising. Our score machine is flexible enough to partially mimic the relevant inductive biases of multiple architectures, including U-Nets, and CNNs, taking a step towards understanding why different score architectures can exhibit distinct generative behavior. Since our score is solvable in terms of the moments of the data, we can begin to understand how the data distribution interacts with the score network to produce the behavior we observe in diffusion models.

</details>


### [130] [Orthogonality and Dimensionality in Airline Cluster Analysis using PCA and Kernel PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08322)
*Andreas Schlapbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了Renold等(2023)对美国航空利润周期(1995-2020)的聚类分析，发现六聚类分类在几何上是稳健的，但通过核PCA和轮廓系数分析表明，数据集结构上仅支持三个聚类，原始七维空间的共线性掩盖了这一信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Renold等(2023)提出的六聚类分类法在几何上的稳健性，并检测数据流形是否存在非线性结构，从而评估聚类数的合理性。

Method: 在原始7维空间、3维和4维PC得分空间复现k-means聚类；应用六种核函数(涵盖三个核族及线性基线)的核PCA进行非线性检测；使用轮廓系数评估最优聚类数。

Result: 1) 3-PC空间的k-means与7维原始空间产生逐比特一致的聚类分配；2) 所有六种核函数均在2D空间中保持六聚类分配；3) 1D诊断中，线性核将COVID年份C_3与峰值利润簇C_0混淆，而所有非线性核将C_3移至仅与金融危机后簇C_5重叠；4) 轮廓系数显示数据集仅支持三个聚类，原始空间的共线性抑制了这一信号。

Conclusion: 数据集本质上是一个线性流形，不存在隐藏曲率；六聚类分类虽几何稳健但非结构最优，三个聚类才是数据驱动的合理选择；强调了降维前诊断共线性的重要性。

Abstract: To characterize the US airline profit cycles from 1995 to 2020, the authors of Renold et al. (2023) combine k-means clustering, principal component analysis, and system dynamic modelling. We replicate their clustering experiment in three spaces -- the original 7-dimensional raw-variable space, a 3-dimensional PC score space, and a 4-dimensional PC score space using their dataset gratefully included in the paper. We show that the six-cluster taxonomy is geometrically robust: k-means in 3-PC space produces bit-for-bit identical cluster assignments relative to 7D raw space. As a nonlinearity check we apply kernel PCA under six kernels spanning three families plus a linear baseline. All six kernels preserve the six-cluster assignment in 2D. A 1D diagnostic tightens this: the linear kernel conflates the COVID year C_3 with the peak-profit cluster C_0, whereas all five non-baseline kernels shift C_3 to overlap only the post-financial-crisis cluster C_5. Agreement across the kernel families confirms an intrinsically linear manifold with no hidden curvature. The silhouette criterion reveals that the dataset structurally supports only three clusters, not six. Collinearity in the raw 7D space suppresses the silhouette signal that would otherwise identify k=3 as the structurally motivated choice.

</details>


### [131] [GENERIC-FNO: Embedding Energy Conservation and Entropy Production into Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08343)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENERIC-FNO是首个在函数空间中嵌入完整GENERIC（度量-辛）非平衡热力学结构的神经算子，通过构造性方法精确满足退化条件（残差~10^-13），无需惩罚项或投影更新，在多种PDE和算子架构上实现零样本超分辨率预测，且与强基线方法性能相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构保持神经算子最多只 enforcing 单一守恒律或可逆（Hamiltonian）结构，而热力学一致的学习仅限于有限维、图或粒子系统。GENERIC-FNO旨在填补这一空白，将完整的GENERIC结构嵌入函数空间。

Method: 将能量E和熵S泛函学习为神经算子；将Poisson算子L和摩擦算子M参数化为对角Fourier乘子，夹在秩一投影之间，通过构造精确满足退化条件（L·∇S=0, M·∇E=0），无需惩罚项、更新投影或残差项。

Result: 退化恒等式在机器精度（残差~10^-13）下成立，与初始化、维度或分辨率无关；显式时间步进仅引入O(dt^2)的小漂移（每步残差~10^-6）。在1D/2D FNO和DeepONet三种算子骨干及四种PDE上，零样本跨越4倍超分辨率范围（64→256），恢复物理耗散排序，在多个耗散和混合问题上超越或媲美强基线。

Conclusion: GENERIC-FNO通过构造性设计实现了热力学一致性的精确保持，为在函数空间中学习非平衡热力学系统提供了有效框架，且在实际预测性能上具有竞争力。

Abstract: We introduce GENERIC-FNO, the first neural operator to embed the full GENERIC (metriplectic) structure of nonequilibrium thermodynamics -- reversible, energy-conserving dynamics and irreversible, entropy-producing dynamics coupled through the degeneracy conditions -- directly in function space. Existing structure-preserving neural operators enforce at most a single conservation law or reversible (Hamiltonian) structure, while thermodynamically consistent learning has been confined to finite-dimensional, graph, or particle systems. GENERIC-FNO closes this gap: it learns the energy and entropy functionals as neural operators and parameterizes the Poisson and friction operators as diagonal Fourier multipliers sandwiched between rank-one projections that enforce the degeneracy conditions exactly, by construction, with no penalty term, update projection, or residual. The degeneracy identities hold to machine precision (residuals ~10^-13) for any initialization, dimension, or resolution, so the continuous-time dynamics conserve the learned energy and produce entropy exactly; the explicit time stepping adds only a small O(dt^2) drift (per-step residual ~10^-6). We further note that the (E,S,L,M) decomposition of a given flow is not unique, and introduce a gauge-invariant dissipation diagnostic separating reversible from dissipative dynamics independently of the learned functionals. Across three operator backbones (1D/2D FNOs and DeepONet) and four PDEs spanning reversible, dissipative, and mixed regimes, GENERIC-FNO preserves its exact structural guarantees zero-shot across a 4x super-resolution range (64 to 256), recovers the ground-truth ordering of physical dissipation, and is competitive with strong unconstrained and energy-penalized baselines, outperforming them on several dissipative and mixed problems at comparable or fewer parameters.

</details>


### [132] [Generative Frontier Planning for Adaptive Peer-Referral Recruitment under Covariate-Dependent Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08360)
*Lingkai Kong,Hezi Jiang,Andrew Ma,Keyu Wang,Akseli Kangaslahti,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对隐藏人群的同伴推荐招募问题，提出了一种生成式前沿规划方法（GFP）。该方法放弃了传统同质群体假设，采用基于推荐者条件的审查计数模型和条件生成模型来建模真实招募过程，并利用离线摊销的潜变量覆盖度价值代理实现确定性回溯规划，避免了蒙特卡洛采样，同时利用边际贪婪分配策略在每轮问题中获得(1-1/e)近似保证。在真实数据校准的仿真中，GFP在多个折现因子下优于随机、强化学习和同质动态规划基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同伴推荐招募（如受访者驱动抽样）的资源配置方法通常假设样本来自同质群体（独立同分布），忽略了真实世界中推荐者之间的同质性和共享背景，导致规划不准确。需要一种能考虑推荐者条件依赖关系且规划可解的更现实方法。

Method: 提出GFP框架：(1) 用审查计数模型建模推荐能力，用条件生成模型建模新个体的协变量分布，两者均以推荐者为条件；(2) 设计潜变量协变量覆盖度价值代理，使得下一前沿的期望值仅依赖于离线摊销的有限维摘要，从而将每步蒙特卡洛采样替换为确定性回溯；(3) 证明每轮目标函数具有单调性和边际收益递减性质，使边际贪婪分配获得(1-1/e)近似最优。

Result: 在基于真实受访者驱动抽样数据校准的仿真环境中，GFP在四个不同折现因子下均优于随机分配、强化学习方法和基于同质假设的动态规划基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入推荐者条件依赖建模和确定性价值代理，GFP在不牺牲可解性的前提下显著提升了隐藏人群招募资源分配的规划质量，为公共卫生实践提供了更贴近现实的决策工具。

Abstract: Peer-referral recruitment systems such as respondent-driven sampling are critical for studying and intervening on hidden populations affected by infectious diseases. To accelerate recruitment, public health agencies must adaptively allocate limited referral resources across multiple rounds, where current decisions shape both the number and the covariates of future recruits. Prior work makes this problem tractable by assuming that referrals are drawn i.i.d.\ from a homogeneous population, an assumption that ignores the homophily and shared context that drive real peer recruitment. We instead consider a more realistic model in which both referral capacity and the covariates of newly referred individuals are conditioned on the referrer, learned from data with a censored count model and a conditional generative model. The resulting planning problem is challenging because each candidate allocation induces a different distribution over future recruits. We propose \emph{Generative Frontier Planning} (GFP), a model-based planner that replaces per-step Monte-Carlo sampling with a deterministic backup over a latent covariate-coverage value surrogate. The surrogate is designed so that the expected value of the next frontier depends on the offspring generative model only through finite-dimensional summaries that are amortized offline, and so that the resulting per-round objective is monotone with diminishing returns. Together, these two properties make planning tractable: the deterministic backup eliminates Monte-Carlo sampling, and the diminishing-returns structure lets a marginal greedy allocation achieve a \((1-1/e)\)-approximation for the per-round problem. On a simulation environment calibrated to a real respondent-driven sampling dataset, GFP outperforms random, reinforcement-learning, and i.i.d.\ dynamic-programming baselines across four discount factors.

</details>


### [133] [Pre-Intervention Prediction of Sparse Autoencoder Steering Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08365)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预干预筛选框架，利用SAE特征统计量预测特征 steering 的副作用（效果稳定性和附带扩散），并在多种模型和字典设置下验证了其预测能力，但发现有效的预测因子和模块化轴因模型和字典而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE特征虽常用于语言模型 steering，但同一干预在不同上下文中行为不一致且会扰动无关特征，缺乏有效的事前副作用预测手段。

Method: 构建预干预筛选框架，从SAE字典的解码器几何、激活统计、共激活结构、直接logit足迹等统计量出发，预测 steering 的效果稳定性和附带扩散两个模块化轴，在GPT-2-small、Pythia-70M、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.1-8B上结合ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK字典进行实验。

Result: 解码器几何、激活统计、共激活结构和直接logit足迹优于仅基于频率和激活幅度的基线；信号在GPT-2-small、Pythia-70M、Llama-3.1-8B中显著且在残差化后仍存在，但在Gemma-2-2B中较弱；留出筛选可选取更干净的steering特征，但不同模型提升的模块化轴不同；Llama字典宽度从32K扩至128K时预测信号仍存在但筛选收益稳定性下降。

Conclusion: SAE steering副作用可提前预测，但有效的预测特征和可迁移的模块化轴依赖于具体模型和字典设置。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features are increasingly used to steer language models, but feature steering is rarely clean: the same intervention can behave inconsistently across contexts and perturb unrelated features. We introduce a pre-intervention screening framework for forecasting SAE steering side effects from feature statistics computed before steering. We operationalize side effects along two axes of steering modularity, effect stability and collateral spread, and evaluate GPT-2-small, Pythia-70M-deduped, Gemma-2-2B, and Llama-3.1-8B across ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK SAE dictionaries. Across these settings, decoder geometry, activation statistics, co-activation structure, and direct-logit footprint predict steering modularity better than frequency-only and activation-magnitude baselines. The signal is strongest in GPT-2-small, Pythia-70M, and Llama-3.1-8B, where it survives residualization against magnitude-related confounds, and weaker in Gemma-2-2B. Held-out screening shows that ranking unseen features by predicted cleanliness can select features that steer more cleanly on fresh contexts, but the successful axis varies by setting: GPT-2 improves most cleanly, Pythia improves mainly on stability, Llama mainly on collateral, and Gemma only partially. A controlled Llama Scope width comparison shows that the predictive signal persists under a 32K-to-128K dictionary-width change, although the screening payoff becomes less stable. Overall, SAE steering side effects are predictable in advance, but the useful predictor signature and transferred modularity axis are model- and dictionary-setting dependent.

</details>


### [134] [An Information-Theoretic Definition for Open-Ended Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08369)
*Wanqiao Xu,Yifan Zhu,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的开端性定义（bit-equivalent），用于量化智能体在开放环境中达到每个期望奖励水平所需的信息量，并证明经典赌博机环境不具备开端性，同时提出了一个能够实现开端学习的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI系统在开放环境中持续扩展能力的研究缺乏对开端性的一致定义或探索理论，亟需一个形式化的理论框架来定义和量化开放环境中的学习。

Method: 引入bit-equivalent概念，从信息论角度定义开端性：若智能体能够实现bit-equivalent的线性增长，则该环境是开放的。通过对比经典赌博机环境（非开放）与设计的开放赌博机环境，验证定义的合理性，并提出相应算法。

Result: 证明了经典赌博机环境不具备开端性，成功构造了一个具备开端性的赌博机环境，并提出了一个在该环境中实现开端学习的算法。

Conclusion: 该信息论定义为开端性学习提供了理论基础，为设计能够在开放环境中持续扩展能力的AI系统开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A growing body of work points to the great promise of AI systems that can continually expand their capabilities as they operate in an open-ended environment. But yet there is no coherent definition of open-endedness or theory about how an agent ought to explore an open-ended environment. We introduce an information-theoretic definition based on a new concept -- the ${\textit bit-equivalent}$ -- which quantifies the information required to attain each level of expected reward. We consider an environment to be open-ended if an agent can attain linear growth in the bit-equivalent. We establish that classical bandit environments are not open-ended and formulate a bandit environment that is. We also introduce an algorithm that achieves open-ended learning in this environment.

</details>


### [135] [Few-step Cofolding with All-Atom Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08375)
*Gianluca Scarpellini,Ron Shprints,Peter Holderrieth,Juno Nam,Pranav Murugan,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli,Tommi Jaakola,Maruan Al-Shedivat,Nicholas Matthew Boffi,Avishek Joey Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeCAF是一个将全原子共折叠扩散模型蒸馏为流匹配模型的框架，仅需少量推理步骤即可生成高质量3D生物分子复合物结构，并引入奖励引导的推理时搜索方法，在多个基准上取得了优于或媲美原始扩散模型的效果，同时计算成本降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全原子生成模型（如扩散模型）虽能精确预测蛋白质和蛋白质-配体系统的3D结构，但迭代扩散过程计算成本高昂，限制了常规部署和推理时搜索技术的应用。本文旨在通过蒸馏技术将昂贵的扩散模型转化为高效的流匹配模型。

Method: 提出Denoiser Cofolding All-Atom Flowmap (DeCAF)框架：(1) 基于去噪器的流匹配公式，结合端点损失并天然支持SE(3)刚性对齐；(2) 推导简单的变量变换，使DeCAF能在EDM架构的σ-space噪声调度下运行，从而直接从预训练的共折叠扩散模型蒸馏；(3) 引入推理时奖励引导搜索框架，利用流映射的前瞻性改进采样质量。

Result: (1) DeCAF-Boltz在Runs N' Poses基准上，在严格NFE预算下，相较于Boltz-1x在RMSD和物理有效性分数上均有统计显著提升；(2) 在PoseBusters上，所有推理计算预算下均展现出更优的Pareto前沿；(3) DeCAF-Pearl蒸馏自最新Pearl共折叠模型，在成功率上匹配教师模型，但NFE减少5倍。

Conclusion: DeCAF成功将全原子共折叠扩散模型蒸馏为高效的流匹配模型，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持或提升了生成质量，为蛋白质-配体系统的结构预测提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: All-atom generative modeling of 3D biomolecular complexes has emerged as the dominant paradigm for predicting the structure of proteins and protein-ligand systems. Generating structures at the atomic level of fidelity, however, typically requires expensive iterative diffusion rollouts, making both conventional deployment and inference-time search techniques computationally costly. In this paper, we introduce the Denoiser Cofolding All-Atom Flowmap (DeCAF) framework for distilling state-of-the-art all-atom cofolding models into all-atom flow maps that produce high-quality samples in only a few inference steps. We build DeCAF on a denoiser-based formulation of flow maps with endpoint losses that naturally support SE(3) rigid alignment, which we show is critical for training accurate models. We further derive a simple change of variables that lets DeCAF operate in the σ-space noise schedule of EDM-style architectures, enabling direct distillation from pretrained cofolding diffusion models. Equipped with DeCAF's flowmap lookahead, we introduce a purpose-built inference-time framework that improves sampling through reward-guided search. Empirically, DeCAF-Boltz statistically improves over Boltz-1x in both accuracy (RMSD) and physical validity scores of protein-ligand poses at strict NFE budgets on the challenging Runs N' Poses, while also showing a more optimal Pareto frontier across all inference compute budgets on PoseBusters. Distilling the state-of-the-art Pearl cofolding model, DeCAF-Pearl outperforms diffusion-based cofolding models and matches its teacher on success rate while using 5x fewer NFEs. We release our code at https://github.com/genesistherapeutics/decaf.

</details>


### [136] [RiskNet: A large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents from news with alignment and multi-dimensional annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08376)
*Leihan Zhang,Wecheng Ye,Xianlong Ma,Haochuan Liu,Yang Li,Qianyu Zhang,Jinliang Chen,Qiang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiskNet是一个从大规模多语言新闻源构建的AI风险事件数据集，通过结构化流水线进行新闻识别、事件筛选、事件对齐和多维分类，支持AI安全、治理和风险分析研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架主要停留在高层原则层面，缺乏大规模、可计算的实证资源来持续追踪和分析真实世界的AI风险事件；现有的风险事件集合多为人工整理、规模较小，不足以支撑数据驱动的系统性监控和计算分析。

Method: RiskNet采用结构化流水线：1) 大规模多语言新闻源中识别AI风险新闻；2) 事件级报告筛选；3) 事件对齐（将分散报道聚类为事件记录）；4) 多维度事件分类。数据集包含数亿级源记录，提供事件分类、事件对齐和风险标注的基准数据集，并通过在线平台提供浏览和探索功能。

Result: 构建了覆盖数亿源记录的大规模AI风险事件数据集，包含对齐的事件簇和带标注的基准子集；提供了事件分类、事件对齐和事件级风险标注的标准基准数据；搭建了在线浏览和探索平台。

Conclusion: RiskNet作为结构化、可复用的实证资源，弥合了高层治理原则与AI风险事件现实记录之间的差距，支持AI安全、治理、风险分析、基准评估以及跨源纵向分析等下游研究。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed across socially consequential domains, reports of AI-related harms and failures have grown in frequency and diversity. Although existing governance frameworks articulate high-level principles for responsible AI, large-scale empirical resources for tracking and analyzing real-world AI risk incidents remain limited. Existing incident collections are often manually curated, relatively small in scale, and insufficient for continuous, data-driven monitoring and downstream computational analysis. To address this need, we present RiskNet, a large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents constructed from large-scale multilingual news sources. RiskNet applies a structured pipeline for AI risk news identification, event-level report screening, incident alignment, and multi-dimensional incident classification. The resulting resource organizes dispersed news reports into incident-centered records and provides benchmark datasets for event classification, incident alignment, and incident-level risk labeling. In its current release, RiskNet covers hundreds of millions of source records and yields a large-scale collection of AI risk-related reports, including aligned incident clusters and annotated benchmark subsets. The dataset is also accessible through an online platform for browsing and exploration. We describe the data sources, processing workflow, taxonomy design, and technical validation of the resource. RiskNet is intended to support downstream research on AI safety, governance, risk analysis, and benchmarking, as well as longitudinal and cross-source analyses of AI-related harms. By providing a structured and reusable empirical resource, RiskNet helps bridge the gap between high-level governance principles and the documented realities of AI risk incidents.

</details>


### [137] [STAR-KV: Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Soft Thresholding for Adaptive Rank Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08382)
*Priyansh Bhatnagar,Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi,Se-Hyun Yang,SeungJae Lee,Jungwook Choi,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAR-KV提出了一种自适应低秩KV缓存压缩框架，通过可微阈值机制实现多头和多块级别的精细秩选择，结合混合分解策略和低秩感知混合精度量化，在多种LLM上实现最高75%的KV缓存压缩，结合量化可达20倍压缩，并基于Triton内核实现最高6.9倍注意力模块加速和3.1倍端到端生成吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩投影方法在压缩KV缓存时依赖固定或启发式秩选择，难以在激进压缩下保持低精度损失，缺乏自适应且精细的秩控制机制。

Method: 1) 可微阈值机制实现注意力头和块级别的最优秩选择；2) 混合分解策略根据键和值投影的敏感性应用不同低秩分解；3) 低秩感知混合精度量化利用数据统计实现近乎无损的低比特量化；4) 基于Triton的自定义GPU内核加速。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准上，KV缓存压缩达75%，结合量化整体压缩达20倍；注意力模块加速6.9倍，端到端生成吞吐提升3.1倍。

Conclusion: STAR-KV通过自适应精细秩控制、混合分解和量化感知设计，在KV缓存压缩率、精度保持和推理加速方面均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Low-rank projection has emerged as a promising approach for compressing the KV cache by exploiting hidden-dimension redundancy. However, prior methods rely on fixed or heuristic rank selection and struggle to achieve aggressive compression with minimal accuracy degradation. We propose STAR-KV, an adaptive low-rank KV cache compression framework with fine-grained rank control.
