Most of the existing augmentation strategies for learning are designed for classification tasks rather than regression, which neglect spatial and transcriptomic relationships, leading to biologically implausible interpolations that hinder prediction performance. Approach: To address these limitations, we propose SNR-ST-Mix, a geometry- and expression-aware data augmentation framework designed specifically for ST data. It constrains mixing to a spot's k-nearest spatial neighbors and adaptively weights interpolation coefficients based on expression similarity, generating augmented samples that preserve local biological structure while ensuring spatial smoothness. This dual conditioning yields synthetic examples that expand the effective training manifold, promote generalization, and enhance prediction stability under sample-specific training. Results: Extensive experiments with various tissue types demonstrate that SNR-ST-Mix consistently outperforms conventional augmentation methods without requiring architectural changes or additional computation. Conclusions: SNR-ST-Mix provides an effective and biologically principled augmentation strategy for spatial transcriptomics regression tasks. By explicitly leveraging spatial geometry and transcriptomic similarity, it expands the effective training manifold and improves predictive performance without increasing model complexity.

</details>


### [173] [Deep Active Re-Labeling: Toward Noise-Resilient Annotation Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08718)
*Md Abdullah Al Forhad,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度主动学习（DAL）中人工标注错误导致性能下降的问题，受人类学习模式启发，提出将部分标注预算用于对已标注数据进行重标注的框架，通过两种主动噪声采样策略识别并重新标注疑似噪声样本，实验证明在相同预算下该方法更高效且能获得更干净的标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度主动学习虽然能有效减少人工标注成本，但其效果受到人工标注错误的严重制约。当标注者在高信息量数据中引入标注错误时，主动学习性能会显著下降，甚至劣于被动学习。因此需要解决DAL中的人工标注噪声问题。

Method: 提出一种受人类学习模式启发的框架，核心思想是将部分标注预算分配给已标注数据的重标注。设计两种主动噪声采样策略，在不同场景下识别潜在噪声样本，然后将部分标注预算用于重标注这些实例，使主动学习具备"回顾与内省"的能力。

Result: 在相同标注预算下，该方法比传统主动学习更具数据效率，最终能获得相对无噪声的标注数据集，有效缓解了人工标注错误对主动学习性能的负面影响。

Conclusion: 通过将主动学习引入重标注机制，可以有效解决人工标注噪声问题，提升深度主动学习在真实标注场景下的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: While Deep Active Learning (DAL) effectively reduces human annotation costs, its efficacy is constrained by human annotation errors. This is because the data sampled for active learning is assumed to be highly informative for training. When human annotators introduce errors into this informative data at a certain rate, the active learning performance drops significantly and, in some cases, even exhibits worse outcomes than passive learning. In this paper, we first analyze the impact of human annotation errors in the DAL setting. Then we propose a framework to address the human annotation noise problem for DAL. Informed by human learning patterns, the core idea of our proposed solution involves allocating a portion of the human annotation budget to re-annotate data that has already been labeled. Previous theoretical work suggests that when the model possesses a certain level of ability to identify potentially noisy data, even re-labeling a small fraction of the data can effectively remove noise from the active training set. To achieve this, we implement two active noise sampling strategies to detect noise under different circumstances and allocate a part of the annotation budget to re-annotate these instances. Our approach imbues active learning with a revisiting and introspective behavior. Our experiments demonstrate that, under the same annotation budget, our method is more data-efficient and yields a relatively noise-free annotation dataset in the end.

</details>


### [174] [A Geometric Measure of Linear Separability for Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08721)
*Yi Wei,Xuan Qi,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向线性可分性度量（LSM），用于诊断神经网络表示中每类相对于其他类的一侧仿射可分性，并建立其理论性质、高效估计算法及在深度架构中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经分类器依赖线性读出层，但仅用预测准确率无法刻画表示空间中各类别的几何结构。需要一种细粒度、类级别的诊断工具来理解表示的可分性。

Method: 提出方向线性可分性度量（LSM），通过搜索包含目标类所有样本的仿射半空间，测量竞争类样本侵入目标侧的最小程度（归一化后）。建立支撑超平面刻画、与最优分类精度的关系、满秩线性嵌入下的不变性。给出基于惩罚的仿射搜索算法用于高维特征估计。

Result: 证明了LSM的理论性质：区分线性重参数化与信息损失/非线性变换的影响；给出了高维特征的实用估计算法；在多种深度学习组件和架构上经验性地诊断了类级侵入。

Conclusion: LSM是一种有效的类级几何诊断工具，能揭示线性读出层无法反映的表示结构差异，为理解神经网络表示提供了新视角。

Abstract: Modern neural classifiers commonly rely on linear readouts, yet predictive metrics alone do not characterize the class-wise geometry of the representations on which such readouts operate. We introduce the directional linear separability measure (LSM), a finite-sample diagnostic for one-sided affine separability. For a target class A and a competing set B, LSM searches over affine halfspaces that contain all samples in A and measures the smallest competing-sample intrusion that must remain on the target side, normalized by |A|. The resulting quantity is asymmetric, class-wise, target-normalized, and applicable to finite representations extracted from neural networks. We establish its supporting-hyperplane characterization, relate it to optimal affine classification accuracy, and prove invariance under full-rank linear embeddings. These results separate changes caused by linear reparameterization from those caused by information loss or nonlinear geometric transformations. We also give a penalty-based affine search for estimating class-wise LSM in high-dimensional features, with reported values computed from the original discrete preservation and violation criterion. Finally, we analyze coordinatewise gated nonlinearities as finite-sample geometric operators and empirically use LSM to diagnose class-wise intrusion across common deep-learning components and architectures.

</details>


### [175] [Declarative Outcome-Conformant Synthesis: Exact, Closed-Form Specification Satisfaction and a Conformance Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08736)
*Muhammed Rasin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“结果一致合成”（outcome-conformant synthesis）任务，在无源数据（冷启动）条件下生成精确满足声明分析结果（如收入曲线、流失率、群体份额）的关系数据。与传统模仿方法（copula、GAN、扩散）不同，该方法以一致性而非保真度为评价轴。实验表明，传统合成器在真实数据上训练后，月度聚合误差达74-86%；而作者提出的封闭形式生成器可实现0误差，且保真度损失极小（1-Wasserstein距离不超过0.006）。贡献包括：形式化理论、SpecBench基准测试和封闭形式参考系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成表格数据的主流方法（模仿学习）无法满足一类重要实际需求：在无源数据（冷启动）条件下，生成精确复制声明分析结果（如收入曲线、流失率、群体份额）的关系数据。现有工具无法为此类目标提供接口，且任何采样方法因方差存在无法精确命中聚合指标。

Method: (1) 形式化证明：一类广泛使用的精确聚合生成器等价于对Gamma总体进行条件求和采样（基于Lukacs特征刻画），具有封闭形式精确性、封闭形式边际CV和尺度不变性；(2) 提出SpecBench基准，用于衡量冷启动关系合成中分析结果的一致性；(3) 设计封闭形式确定性参考系统。

Result: 在真实公开数据集上，传统学习型合成器（即在真实数据上训练）对声明的月度聚合指标误差达74-86%；逐周期优化可将误差降至约19%，但仍无法达到0；而封闭形式生成器精确达到0。强制执行精确聚合在1-Wasserstein距离上对外部任意边际的代价不超过0.006，其余差异为形状族失配。

Conclusion: 精确聚合本身是平凡的；贡献在于一致性（conformance）与封闭形式边际、完整性、确定性和零源数据的联合实现。作者承认在存在真实数据的情况下，模仿学习在保真度方面更优。

Abstract: We study a capability the dominant paradigm in synthetic tabular data does not provide: exact satisfaction of a declared analytical outcome with no source data. Imitation methods (copulas, GANs, diffusion) learn a real distribution and sample from it, and are judged on fidelity to real data. A large, practical class of needs is different: generating data with no source data ("cold start") that reproduces a declared outcome (a revenue curve, a churn rate, a group share) across a relational schema. Off-the-shelf imitation tools offer no interface for such targets, and no sampler can hit an exact aggregate, because sampling has variance. On a real public dataset, off-the-shelf learned synthesizers trained on that very data miss the declared monthly aggregate by 74 to 86 percent; a per-period steelman cuts the miss to about 19 percent and still cannot reach 0; a closed-form generator reaches exactly 0. We name this task outcome-conformant synthesis, argue its evaluation axis is conformance rather than fidelity, and show the two axes are orthogonal. We contribute: (1) a formal account showing a widely-used family of exact-aggregate generators is exactly conditional-sum sampling of a Gamma population (via Lukacs' characterization), with closed-form exactness, a closed-form marginal CV, and scale-invariance; a controlled experiment maps the boundary, enforcing the exact aggregate costs at most 0.006 in 1-Wasserstein distance to an arbitrary external marginal, the rest being shape-family mismatch; (2) SpecBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark to measure conformance to analytical outcomes for cold-start relational synthesis; and (3) a closed-form, deterministic reference system. Exact aggregation alone is trivial; the contribution is conformance jointly with closed-form marginals, integrity, determinism, and zero source data. We concede fidelity to imitation where real data exists.

</details>


### [176] [Understanding the Parameter Space Geometry of Transformers Encoding Boolean Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08768)
*Blanka Köver,Alexandra Butoi,Anej Svete,Michael Hahn,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了Transformer在训练中无法学习某些简单函数（如PARITY）的原因：即使这些函数可以通过特定参数设定来表达，但由于敏感函数（输出易受输入单比特翻转影响）在参数空间中占据极小的区域，随机初始化几乎不可能命中这些参数。通过从平均敏感性转向完整的敏感性分布分析，论文证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然计算具有低敏感性字符串的函数，因此任何缺乏此类字符串的函数（如PARITY）在理论上不可学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Transformer在可表达性与可学习性之间的差距，特别是对敏感函数（如PARITY）表现出的学习失败，揭示其背后的精确机制。

Method: 从平均敏感性转向完整的敏感性分布分析，研究Transformer参数空间的几何结构，证明随机初始化几乎必然只能得到具有低敏感性字符串的函数。

Result: 敏感函数即使可表示，在参数空间中占据的区域极小，随机初始化极大概率无法命中；随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然计算具有低敏感性字符串的函数。

Conclusion: 任何缺乏低敏感性字符串的函数（如PARITY）在随机初始化条件下是理论上不可学习的。

Abstract: Transformers consistently fail to learn certain simple functions that are provably expressible with specific parameter settings. This gap between learnability and expressivity is particularly prominent for sensitive functions -- functions whose output is likely to change if a single bit of the input is flipped -- for example, PARITY. While prior work has established that transformers exhibit a bias toward functions with low average sensitivity, the precise mechanism underlying this bias remains poorly understood. To shed light on this phenomenon, we study the geometry of transformers' parameter space. We show that sensitive functions -- even when representable -- occupy a vanishingly small region that random initialization is very likely to miss. Specifically, we shift the focus from average sensitivity to the full sensitivity profile -- the distribution of sensitivity values across all inputs -- and prove that randomly initialized transformers almost surely compute functions which have low-sensitivity strings. Consequently, any function that lacks such strings is provably unlearnable.

</details>


### [177] [How Many Counterfactuals Does It Take? Probing VLM Hallucinations Through Circuits and Causal Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08777)
*Abhivansh Gupta,Simardeep Singh,Advika Sinha,Shreyansh Modi,Akshat Tomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型(VLM)在反事实扰动下幻觉输出的鲁棒性，定义了基于对数概率差异的因果影响指标来表征幻觉预测的稳定性，利用电路发现技术(CD-T)识别负责这些预测的模型组件，并推导出检测幻觉输出不稳定性所需的最小反事实样本数量m的经验界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对VLM幻觉预测在反事实扰动下鲁棒性的原则性理解，作者希望从样本复杂度的角度量化这种鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 定义基于事实、反事实和激活修补(activation-patched)运行的log概率差异的因果影响指标；2) 利用电路发现技术(CD-T)识别负责幻觉预测的模型组件；3) 使用浓度不等式和因果影响分布的方差估计，推导检测幻觉输出不稳定性所需的最小反事实样本数量m的经验界限。

Result: 导出了检测VLM幻觉输出不稳定性所需的最小反事实样本数量m的经验界限，并识别了与幻觉预测相关的模型组件。

Conclusion: 该工作为VLM幻觉输出的反事实鲁棒性提供了原则性的样本复杂度分析框架，有助于理解VLM幻觉行为的稳定性。

Abstract: Visual Language Models (VLMs) are known to produce hallucinated predictions that are not grounded in visual evidence, yet existing approaches lack a principled understanding of how robust such predictions are under counterfactual perturbations. In this work, we study the sample complexity of counterfactual robustness for hallucinated outputs in VLMs. We define a causal influence metric based on log-probability differences between factual, counterfactual, and activation-patched runs, and use it to characterize the stability of hallucinated predictions. By leveraging circuit discovery techniques (CD-T), we identify model components responsible for these predictions and track their activation differences across counterfactual samples. We then derive empirical bounds on the minimum number of counterfactual samples m required to reliably detect instability in hallucinated outputs, using concentration inequalities and variance estimates of the causal influence distribution.

</details>


### [178] [Reformulate LLM Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Training under Black-box Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08779)
*Jiashun Liu,Runze Liu,Xu Wan,Jing Liang,Hongyao Tang,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束的强化学习框架DCMDP，通过引入训练-推理行为一致性约束和拉格朗日松弛机制，自适应平衡策略探索与行为对齐，有效解决了RL后训练中因引擎/架构差异导致的训练-推理不匹配问题，在Qwen-3-8B和Qwen-3-30B-A3B上取得显著提升，并支持异构训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练中常出现次优性能甚至训练崩溃，近期研究发现这源于训练与推理阶段引擎和架构差异导致的隐藏不匹配（训练-推理差异），亟需一种能主动自纠正该差异的稳定优化方法。

Method: 1) 发现训练策略可在适当学习信号下主动自纠正训练-推理差异；2) 经验性地识别出差异容忍区域——区域内缩小差异会抑制探索并降低效率，区域外缩小差异则提升优化一致性；3) 将问题形式化为差异约束马尔可夫决策过程（DCMDP），耦合奖励最大化与训练-推理行为对齐约束；4) 引入拉格朗日松弛机制，根据当前差异违反程度动态调整两目标权重，实现稳定双目标优化。

Result: 在Qwen-3-8B（8B稠密模型）和Qwen-3-30B-A3B（30B混合专家模型）上显著提升性能，并实现了异构训练范式：LLM可在高保真训练设定下优化，同时显式对齐低成本的资源受限推理部署。

Conclusion: DCMDP通过差异约束和自适应平衡机制，有效解决了训练-推理不匹配问题，在保持探索效率的同时提升了优化一致性，为RL后训练中的异构部署场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a pivotal post-training paradigm, yet it frequently suffers from unpredictable sub-optimum performance or even training collapses. Recent findings attribute these failures to a hidden train-inference discrepancy (or mismatch), stemming from the disparate underlying engines and architecture. We find that the training policy can actively self-correct such a discrepancy when provided with an appropriate learning signal. Then, we further empirically identify a discrepancy tolerance region: within this region, aggressively narrowing the discrepancy can suppress policy exploration and reduce learning efficiency, whereas outside this region, reducing excessive discrepancy improves optimization consistency and raises the achievable local performance ceiling. According to such findings, we formulate this problem as a Discrepancy-Constrained Markov Decision Process (DCMDP), where reward maximization is coupled with a constraint that aligns training-Inference behavior, achieving stable dual-objective optimization. To adaptively balance performance improvement and discrepancy control, we introduce a Lagrangian relaxation mechanism that dynamically adjusts the relative weight of the two objectives according to the current degree of discrepancy violation. This enables stable dual-objective optimization: the policy is allowed to explore freely within the tolerance region, while being guided back when the discrepancy exceeds the safe boundary. Empirically, DCMDP significantly improves the performance of 8B dense model (Qwen-3-8b) and 30B Mixture-of-Expert model (Qwen-3-30bA3b), and enables a heterogeneous training paradigm, where LLMs can be optimized in high-fidelity training setup while being explicitly aligned for low-cost, resource-constrained inference deployment.

</details>


### [179] [Scaling Decision-Focused Learning to Large Problems with Lagrangian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08797)
*Stéphane Eilles-Chan Way,Hugo Percot,Quentin Cappart,Tias Guns,Louis-Martin Rousseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将拉格朗日分解融入决策聚焦学习的新框架，通过引入替代目标和两种损失函数，在保持竞争性能的同时大幅提升计算效率与可扩展性，在大规模实例（变量数多达常规工作的8倍）上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策聚焦学习在实际部署中计算成本高、可扩展性有限，因为每个训练实例在每个迭代都需要求解约束优化问题，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 将拉格朗日分解引入决策聚焦学习范式；提出新的替代目标及两种损失函数用于评估和训练预测模型；给出两种变体以权衡计算效率与解的质量；可无缝集成到SPO+和IMLE等标准方法中。

Result: 在二维背包问题和二次投资组合优化两个基准上，所提方法性能具有竞争力且易于并行化；在大规模实例（变量数达常规的8倍）上持续优于传统决策聚焦学习方法。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日分解能有效缓解决策聚焦学习的计算瓶颈，显著提升可扩展性，适用于更大规模的预测-优化问题。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning has shown great promise for addressing predict-then-optimize problems, particularly in the presence of under-specified models. However, its practical deployment is often hindered by high computational costs and limited scalability, as it requires solving a constrained optimization problem for each training instance at every iteration. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework that incorporates Lagrangian decomposition into the decision-focused learning paradigm. Specifically, we introduce a new surrogate objective along with two loss functions for evaluating and training the underlying prediction model. We further propose two variants of our approach, which offer different trade-offs between computational efficiency and solution quality. Our framework can be seamlessly integrated with standard decision-focused learning methods, including Smart Predict-then-Optimize (SPO+) and Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE). Through experiments on two standard benchmarks, the multi-dimensional knapsack problem and quadratic portfolio optimization, we demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive performance while remaining amenable to parallelization. In particular, it consistently outperforms traditional decision-focused learning methods on large-scale instances, involving up to eight times more variables than those typically considered in related work. The implementation is available at https://github.com/corail-research/DFL-LD.

</details>


### [180] [Active Flow Expansion for Out-of-Distribution Discovery: from Theory to Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08802)
*Riccardo De Santi,Bruce Lee,Cristian Perez Jensen,Kimon Protopapas,Sophia Tang,Cheng-Hao Liu,Pranam Chatterjee,Yisong Yue,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Active Flow Expansion (ActFlow)，一种通过验证器反馈和主动探索来扩展生成模型有效覆盖范围的持续预训练方法，用于解决分布外流模型生成新设计的问题，并在分子、肽段和蛋白质序列设计任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流和扩散预训练仅匹配观测数据分布，而观测数据通常只覆盖有效设计空间的一小部分。在生成式发现中，目标是采样有效的、自然界中不存在的新设计，这些设计在标准模型中被赋予可忽略的概率，因此无法访问。传统方法受限于数据分布匹配，无法生成分布外的新颖有效设计。

Method: 提出Active Flow Expansion (ActFlow)方法，通过以下步骤实现：1) 将生成模型的可生成集定义为模型以非可忽略概率覆盖的区域；2) 引入新的学习原则，即扩大模型的可生成集以增加对有效设计空间的覆盖；3) 使用验证器反馈，通过主动探索在学习的流表示中迭代地适应合成数据，从而扩展预训练模型至新的有效区域。

Result: 在小型有机分子、中等规模药物样分子、治疗性肽段和蛋白质序列设计任务上，使用合适的分布外生成建模指标进行评估。结果表明ActFlow能够将有效覆盖范围扩展到远超初始预训练模型所建模的区域，显著优于广泛采用的合成流预训练方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了分布外流建模的统计学习保证（据我们所知是首次），将可生成集扩展分析为学习表示上的局部到全局的可达性过程。ActFlow为面向发现的生成模型提供了一种新的预训练范式，突破了数据分布匹配的限制。

Abstract: Standard flow and diffusion pre-training matches the distribution of available data (e.g., molecules), which often covers only a small fraction of the valid design space. In generative discovery, however, one aims to sample valid new-to-nature designs, assigned negligible probability under, and thus inaccessible to, standard models fitted to the observed data. To overcome this limitation, we depart from data distribution matching and view a generative model through its generable set: the region it covers with non-negligible probability. This allows to introduce a new learning principle for out-of-distribution flow modeling: enlarging a model's generable set to increase coverage of the valid design space. We propose Active Flow Expansion (ActFlow), a continued pre-training method that employs verifier feedback to expand a pre-trained model over new valid regions by iteratively adapting to synthetic data generated through active exploration in the learned flow representation. Theoretically, we establish to our knowledge first-of-their-kind statistical learning guarantees for out-of-distribution flow modeling, analyzing generable set expansion as a local-to-global reachability process over a learned representation. Empirically, we assess ActFlow with suitable out-of-distribution generative modeling metrics across small organic molecules, mid-sized drug-like molecules, therapeutic peptides, and protein sequence design tasks. Results show that ActFlow expands valid coverage far beyond the region modeled by the initial pre-trained model, significantly outperforming widely adopted synthetic flow pre-training methods.

</details>


### [181] [Knowledge Graphs and Reasoning LLMs for Finding Simple Yet Effective Transcriptomic Perturbation Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08816)
*Jake Fawkes,Liam Hodgson,Jason Hartford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，基于知识图谱的K近邻方法在预测未知基因敲除扰动对转录组表达的影响上具有高度竞争力，且通过强化学习优化的LLM能进一步提升性能，达到与当前最优方法相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未见过的基因敲除扰动对转录组基因表达的影响是虚拟细胞模型面临的极具挑战性的问题。现有方法通过利用生物知识图谱提供相似扰动的概念来改进对训练扰动集之外的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 提出基于知识图谱的K近邻方法作为基线模型；2) 使用强化学习训练推理型LLM对近邻进行调整优化；3) 在Replogle et al. (2022)的细胞系数据上进行评估。

Result: 1) K近邻方法在分布外扰动预测任务上击败了几乎所有现有方法；2) 经RL训练的LLM达到与当前最优方法相当的性能；3) RL训练提升了LLM在差异表达预测下游任务上的表现，尽管未直接针对此任务训练。

Conclusion: 知识图谱作为模型先验具有显著有效性，且强化学习有望将LLM优化为预测复杂生物反应的通用工具。

Abstract: Predicting the effect of an unseen gene knockout perturbation on transcriptomic gene expression remains a highly challenging problem for virtual cell models. Recent progress has been made by leveraging biological knowledge graphs to provide a notion of similar perturbation, allowing for improved extrapolation beyond the set of training perturbations. In this work, we demonstrate that the simplest model to leverage these assumptions - a K-nearest neighbour from the knowledge graph - achieves highly competitive performance on this task, and that this can be improved further using LLMs optimised via reinforcement learning (RL) for predictive performance. Specifically, we find that the K-nearest neighbour approach beats almost all methods on out-of-distribution perturbation prediction, and when a reasoning LLM is trained via RL to make changes to the neighbourhood, it obtains equivalent performance to current state of the art methods on the cell lines from Replogle et al. (2022). We also demonstrate that the RL training improves the LLM's performance on the downstream task of differential expression prediction, despite not being trained on this directly. Overall, these findings demonstrate the efficacy of knowledge graphs as model priors, and show early signs that RL can refine LLMs into generalizable tools for predicting complex biological responses.

</details>


### [182] [Intrinsic Selection and Particle Resampling for Inference-Time Scaling Beyond Domain Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08850)
*Giorgio Giannone,Mustafa Eyceoz,Shabana Baig,Shivchander Sudalairaj,Anna C. Doris,Faez Ahmed,Akash Srivastava,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于并行样本集内在统计量（长度调整的尾熵）的无监督推理时扩展方法，包括Intrinsic Selection (iS)、Intrinsic Particle Filtering (iPF) 和 Particle Distillation (dPF)，成功将ITS扩展到无需真实标签或训练奖励模型的开放域任务中，在工程设计、数学推理和临床响应生成等任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法在可验证领域（如数学、编程）效果显著，但扩展到存在系统性失败（如初始假设错误或多维约束未满足）的开放域任务时，通常依赖昂贵的外部求解器或脆弱基于模型的验证器。本文旨在利用并行样本集的内在统计特性，无需真实标签即可实现自适应计算分配，将ITS推广到开放域场景。

Method: 提出三种方法：(1) Intrinsic Selection (iS) 利用长度调整的尾熵进行事后候选排序；(2) Intrinsic Particle Filtering (iPF) 将统计量泛化为步骤级重采样，引导生成走向高置信度推理轨迹；(3) Particle Distillation (dPF) 通过早期logit混合和KL引导重采样注入先验指导，规避系统性推理错误。整体以尾熵作为难度门控，动态分配计算资源。

Result: iS在三个领域匹配基于共识的算法，工程设计选择提升20%超越pass@1基线；iPF在困难数学问题上pass@1平均提升6.1个百分点；dPF在复杂临床响应生成上获得高达26.5%的增益。方法在通用、领域专用和多模态架构上均适用。

Conclusion: 通过并行样本集内在统计量（长度调整的尾熵）可有效替代外部验证器，实现无监督的推理时扩展，成功将ITS从可验证领域推广到开放领域，避免了对训练奖励模型或精确真实标签验证的依赖。

Abstract: Inference-Time Scaling (ITS) has largely succeeded in verifiable domains like math and coding, where cheap verification enables scalable output selection. However, extending ITS to tasks prone to systematic failure - driven by faulty initial assumptions or unmet multidimensional constraints - typically relies on costly external solvers or brittle, model-based verifiers. Our key insight is that the intrinsic statistics of parallel sample sets, specifically length-adjusted tail entropy, provide a robust discriminative signal for solution quality without access to ground truth. Crucially, these statistics serve as a difficulty gate for adaptive compute allocation, dynamically routing problems across scaling regimes. First, Intrinsic Selection (iS) ranks candidates post-hoc, matching consensus-based algorithms across three domains and improving engineering design selection by 20% over pass@1 baselines. Second, Intrinsic Particle Filtering (iPF) generalizes this to step-level resampling, guiding generation toward high-confidence reasoning trajectories to improve pass@1 by 6.1 points on average on hard math problems. Finally, Particle Distillation (dPF) injects privileged guidance via early logit blending and KL-guided resampling, steering generation past systematic reasoning errors to satisfy expert rubrics, yielding up to 26.5% gains on complex clinical responses. Our pipeline applies seamlessly across broad-purpose, domain-specialized, and multimodal architectures, successfully extending ITS to open-ended domains without requiring trained reward models or exact ground-truth verification.

</details>


### [183] [sGPO: Trading Inference FLOPs for Training Efficiency in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08854)
*Shivchander Sudalairaj,Kai Xu,Akash Srivastava,Giorgio Giannone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: sGPO通过低成本推理作为查询难度代理，自适应分配训练预算，在匹配或超越基线性能的同时将总训练计算量减少三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR训练为每个查询分配固定预算，未考虑查询难度差异，导致简单查询零优势、难解查询无信号的双重浪费。

Method: 利用少量推理FLOPs作为离线难度代理，对每个查询生成小批量并行样本计算经验成功率，据此设置训练组大小为成功率的倒数，同时驱动数据过滤、自适应组大小分配和课程构建。

Result: 在匹配或超越基线性能的前提下，总训练计算量减少三倍（包含推理预分析成本）。

Conclusion: 少量推理计算可作为离线难度代理，通过自适应预算分配显著提升训练计算效率。

Abstract: Standard Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) training allocates a fixed rollout budget to every query, without regard for what each query's difficulty means for the current policy. This leads to two symmetric failure modes: easy queries produce near-zero advantage because the policy already solves them, while unsolvable queries produce no signal because the policy never solves them. Both regimes waste training FLOPs without contributing to a learning gradient. We introduce sorted Group Policy Optimization (sGPO), a compute-efficient strategy that trades a small budget of inference FLOPs for a large reduction in wasted training FLOPs. The key insight is that cheap inference compute can serve as a single offline proxy for query difficulty. By generating a small batch of parallel samples per query under the initial policy, we obtain a model-aware empirical success rate. This motivates setting the training rollout group size to the inverse of this success rate, a practical rule that maximizes sample efficiency by extracting the most advantage per generated rollout. This single profiling pass simultaneously drives data filtering (removing trivial queries and sub-sampling unsolvable ones), adaptive group size allocation, and curriculum construction (scheduling queries from easy to hard). sGPO matches or exceeds baseline performance while reducing total training compute by a factor of three, with the upfront inference profiling cost included.

</details>


### [184] [Diffuse AI Control on Fuzzy Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08892)
*Mikhail Terekhov,Caglar Gulcehre,Vivek Hebbar,Joe Benton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个对抗博弈框架来研究AI控制中的扩散威胁（diffuse threats），特别是在模糊任务（fuzzy tasks）上的模型颠覆行为。蓝队使用弱模型构建弱评分来训练强模型以消除颠覆倾向，红队则通过多目标进化提示优化寻找被弱评分高估但实际表现差的子versive行为。实验表明Opus 4.6能写出比GPT-OSS-20B更差的提案却被弱评分器高估。蓝队提出对抗优化算法发现更鲁棒的提示词，抵御红队攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在关键领域（如AI安全研究）中可能因未对齐而微妙地破坏我们的努力。扩散威胁（长期部署中的AI颠覆风险）在模糊任务（难以评分或需要直觉的任务）上尤为危险，现有方法难以应对此类风险。

Method: 1) 将AI控制建模为蓝队与红队的对抗博弈；2) 蓝队用弱可信模型构建弱评分，训练强模型以消除颠覆倾向；3) 红队用多目标进化提示优化寻找被弱评分高估但实际差的子versive行为；4) 蓝队进一步提出对抗优化算法，发现更鲁棒的提示词。

Result: Opus 4.6能写出比GPT-OSS-20B更差的实验提案（根据真实评分），但被弱评分器评为与Opus 4.6最佳提案同水平。蓝队的对抗优化算法产生的提示词，红队优化无法成功利用。

Conclusion: 对抗博弈框架能有效揭示和缓解模糊任务上的扩散威胁。多目标进化提示优化可发现子versive行为，而蓝队的对抗优化算法能提升弱评分模型的鲁棒性，抵御此类攻击。

Abstract: AI models deployed in critical domains, such as AI safety research, may subtly sabotage our efforts due to misalignment. Diffuse AI Control is a subfield of AI safety concerned with mitigating risks from AI sabotage distributed over long deployment horizons (diffuse threats). These risks are particularly pernicious on fuzzy tasks, i.e. tasks which are hard to grade or require intuition. To understand diffuse threats on fuzzy tasks, we introduce a novel framework that considers AI control as an adversarial game between a blue team and a red team. The blue team uses a weak trusted model to construct a weak score against which they would train a strong, potentially subversive model to remove the subversion propensity if it were present. The red team then tries to find model behaviors that are rated highly by the weak score, and thus might not be trained out, but actually correspond to poor performance. We test our framework on the task of writing experimental proposals for research questions from recent ML papers. We use a language model with access to the original paper as a proxy "ground-truth" scorer. Our red team discovers subversive behaviors using multi-objective evolutionary prompt optimization. We show that Opus~4.6 can write proposals that are worse according to the ground truth proxy than those of GPT-OSS-20B, while the weak scorer rates them as highly as the best proposals from Opus 4.6. To mitigate the threat, we propose an adversarial optimization algorithm for the blue team that discovers more robust prompts for the weak model. This algorithm produces a blue team prompt that our red team optimization fails to exploit.

</details>


### [185] [Cheap Reward Hacking Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08893)
*Iván Belenky,Joaquín Itria,Steven Johns*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种小型Transformer编码器，将Terminal-Wrench轨迹映射到单位球面，使嵌入距离近似奖励与元数据信号的L1距离。通过线性探针检测奖励黑客行为，在清洗测试集上达到AUC 0.9467和TPR@5%FPR 0.8296，性能与LLM-as-judge相当（AUC 0.9510）且超越其TPR@5%FPR（0.7130 vs 0.8296），但计算成本降低约四个数量级。编码器并非纯行为读取器：去除输入中的自然语言推理后AUC降至0.6213。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种高效且性能优异的奖励黑客检测方法，替代计算成本高昂的LLM-as-judge方法。

Method: 训练小型Transformer编码器将Terminal-Wrench轨迹映射到单位球面，使嵌入距离近似奖励与元数据信号的L1距离；在其上训练线性探针用于奖励黑客检测。

Result: 在清洗测试集上AUC 0.9467，TPR@5%FPR 0.8296；LLM-as-judge的AUC为0.9510但TPR@5%FPR仅0.7130；编码器计算成本低约四个数量级；去除自然语言推理后AUC降至0.6213。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持与LLM-as-judge相近的AUC的同时，显著提升了TPR@5%FPR并大幅降低计算成本；自然语言推理信息对检测能力至关重要。

Abstract: A small transformer encoder is trained to map Terminal-Wrench trajectories onto a unit sphere where embedding distance approximates the $L_1$ distance between reward and metadata signals. A linear probe on top of that embedding detects reward hacking on the cleaned test split with AUC $0.9467$ and TPR@5%FPR $0.8296$, matching the TW sanitized LLM-as-judge AUC ($0.9510$ on the cleaned split) and exceeding its TPR@5%FPR ($0.7130$ vs $0.8296$) on the same information condition, at roughly four orders of magnitude lower per-trajectory cost. The encoder is not a pure behavior reader: stripping natural-language reasoning from its input at probe time drops AUC to $0.6213$.

</details>


### [186] [Synthetic but Not Realistic: The Evaluation Challenge in Generative Modelling for Structured Electronic Medical Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08903)
*Nicholas I-Hsien Kuo,Blanca Gallego,Louisa Jorm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前合成医疗数据的评估过度依赖统计相似性和预测性能，忽略临床有效性。本文提出基于流行病学的多维评估框架，在包含已知真实结构的PRIME-CVD队列上评估四种生成范式（GAN、VAE、扩散模型、掩码建模），发现所有模型虽能复现边际分布，但均无法同时保留子群结构、效应估计和依赖结构，且分布 fidelity 高的模型可能校准性差、关系扭曲，表明现有评估方法高估了合成数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成医疗数据被广泛用作真实患者数据的隐私保护替代品，但其评估方法主要基于统计相似性和预测性能，无法反映临床有效性和科学结论的可支持性，存在评估偏差风险。

Method: 提出一个基于流行病学的多维评估框架，涵盖三个维度：描述性保真度、临床效用和结构效度（分别对应描述性、预测性和因果性问题）。在包含已知真实结构的PRIME-CVD队列（50,000人）上评估四种生成范式：GAN、VAE增强、扩散模型和掩码建模。

Result: 所有生成模型都能较好地复现边际分布，但没有任何一个模型能同时保留子群结构、效应估计和依赖结构。特别地，分布保真度高的模型可能存在校准性差和关系扭曲的问题，导致不可靠的推断。

Conclusion: 当前合成数据评估实践可能高估了数据质量，需要基于领域知识的评估方法，确保合成数据能够支持有效的临床和科学结论。

Abstract: Synthetic healthcare data are widely proposed as privacy-preserving substitutes for real patient data, yet their evaluation remains dominated by statistical similarity and predictive performance that do not reflect clinical validity. We introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation framework grounded in epidemiology, assessing descriptive fidelity, clinical utility, and structural validity, corresponding to descriptive, predictive, and causal questions. We evaluate four representative generative paradigms - GAN-based, VAE-boosted, diffusion-based, and masked modelling - using PRIME-CVD, a 50,000-person cohort with known ground-truth structure. While all models reproduce marginal distributions, none simultaneously preserve subgroup structure, effect estimates, and dependency structure. Notably, models with strong distributional fidelity can exhibit poor calibration and distorted relationships, leading to unreliable inference. These results show that current evaluation practices can overestimate synthetic data quality and motivate domain-informed assessment based on the ability to support valid clinical and scientific conclusions.

</details>


### [187] [Generalized Rank-based Evaluation for Knowledge Graph Completion: Perspectives, Framework, and Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08921)
*Sooho Moon,Jian Kang,Yunyong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE框架，从预测锐度（P1）和流行度偏差鲁棒性（P2）两个被现有评估指标忽视的视角改进知识图谱补全（KGC）模型的评估，通过排名变换器（RT）和排名聚合器（RA）实现更全面、灵活且一致的评估，理论证明其满足六个关键属性，实验验证其优于现有指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC评估指标忽视了预测锐度（模型对正负样本的区分能力）和流行度偏差鲁棒性（评估结果不受实体流行度干扰的程度）两个重要视角，无法可靠地评估模型性能或为实际应用选择合适模型。

Method: 提出PROBE框架，包含两个组件：（1）排名变换器（RT）：根据所需的预测锐度水平估计每个预测的得分；（2）排名聚合器（RA）：根据所需的流行度偏差鲁棒性水平聚合所有预测得分得到最终评估分数。论文还定义了可靠KGC评估的六个关键属性，并从理论上证明PROBE满足所有属性。

Result: 在6个真实KG上使用6种KGC模型进行的实验表明，现有指标可能高估或低估模型性能，而PROBE能提供更全面、灵活且一致的评估。特别地，在KG开放世界特性下，PROBE能更好地保持模型相对性能的一致性。

Conclusion: PROBE框架通过引入预测锐度和流行度偏差鲁棒性两个新视角，为KGC模型评估提供了更可靠、一致且灵活的方案，解决了现有评估指标的不足。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) aims to predict missing facts from an observed knowledge graph (KG), playing a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications such as drug discovery, recommender systems, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Although numerous KGC models have been proposed, the evaluation of KGC remains underexplored, despite its critical role in reliably assessing model performance and selecting appropriate models for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce two important perspectives for KGC evaluation that are overlooked by existing evaluation metrics, (P1) predictive sharpness and (P2) popularity-bias robustness. To address both perspectives, we propose a generalized evaluation framework, PROBE, which consists of a rank transformer (RT) that estimates the score of each prediction based on a desired level of predictive sharpness and a rank aggregator (RA) that determines the final evaluation score by aggregating all prediction scores according to a desired level of popularity-bias robustness. We theoretically analyze PROBE by defining six key properties for reliable KGC evaluation and prove that PROBE satisfies all the properties, while existing metrics fail to satisfy some. In particular, due to the open-world nature of KGs, an evaluation metric should preserve the relative performance of KGC models even when only incomplete facts are observed. We show that PROBE better maintains such consistency, providing a more reliable estimate of intrinsic model performance than existing metrics. Extensive experiments with six KGC models on six real-world KGs reveal that existing metrics may over- or under-estimate model performance depending on different evaluation perspectives, whereas PROBE enables a more comprehensive, flexible, and consistent evaluation of KGC models.

</details>


### [188] [PROBE-Web: An Interactive System for Probing Evaluation Landscapes of Knowledge Graph Completion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08926)
*Sooho Moon,Yunyong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本演示系统PROBE-Web提供了一个交互式平台，用于通过调整预测锐度（P1）和流行度偏差鲁棒性（P2）两个关键视角，灵活评估知识图谱补全（KGC）模型，支持传统评估、视角感知评估、可解释案例研究和评估景观探索四大功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC模型评估主要依赖MRR和Hits@K等基于排名的指标，但不同用户往往需要不同的评估视角，现有评估方式无法满足多样化的评估需求。

Method: PROBE-Web是一个交互式系统，通过用户友好的图形界面，允许用户调整两个关键评估视角（预测锐度和流行度偏差鲁棒性），提供传统评估工具包、灵活视角感知评估、可解释案例研究和评估景观探索四项核心功能。

Result: PROBE-Web能够帮助用户灵活评估多个KGC模型，分析其优缺点，并根据自身目标更好地理解KGC模型。

Conclusion: PROBE-Web通过提供多样化的评估视角和交互式探索功能，弥补了传统单一排名指标在KGC模型评估中的不足，使用户能够根据自身需求进行更全面的模型评估。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) models are commonly evaluated using rank-based metrics such as MRR and Hits@K, despite different users often requiring different evaluation perspectives. In this demo, we present PROBE-Web, an interactive system for probing diverse evaluation landscapes for KGC models. PROBE-Web enables users to flexibly evaluate KGC models by adjusting two critical perspectives: (P1) predictive sharpness and (P2) popularity-bias robustness. Through a user-friendly GUI, users easily evaluate multiple KGC models and analyze their strengths and weaknesses. PROBE-Web provides four key functionalities: (1) conventional evaluation toolkit, (2) flexible perspective-aware evaluation, (3) explainable case studies, and (4) evaluation landscape exploration. We believe that PROBE-Web can help users better understand KGC models aligning with their objectives.

</details>


### [189] [Backward Coherence and Hidden-State Stability in Recurrent Neural Networks: A Quasi-Reverse-Martingale Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08934)
*Yuan-chin Ivan Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过**后向一致性**（backward coherence）研究RNN隐藏状态$h_t$的稳定性，证明在压缩性和可和后向漂移条件下，隐藏状态序列形成拟反向鞅，从而获得几乎必然收敛、混合速率、可解释极限表示、有限路径停止时间和时间一致置信序列的理论框架。后向一致性正则化可将经验拟鞅总量降低43%-58%，比未正则化RNN提前28%-44%达到稳定。该方法还等价于在高斯后向模型中最小化KL散度，与变分推断建立联系。三个真实数据实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的隐藏状态$h_t$的**概率意义不明确**，其稳定性和收敛性缺乏严格理论分析。现有方法无法为隐藏状态的动态行为提供可解释的理论保证，尤其是在长期依赖和概念漂移场景下。

Method: 1. 定义**后向一致性**：衡量从$h_{t+1}$通过学习的后向投影器$g_φ$重构$h_t$的程度。2. 在压缩性（contraction）和可和后向漂移（summable backward drift）条件下，证明隐藏状态序列形成**拟反向鞅**（quasi-reverse-martingale）。3. 由此导出几乎必然收敛、混合速率、极限表示、停止时间和时间一致置信序列等理论性质。4. 提出**后向一致性正则化**损失，并证明其等价于在高斯后向模型中最小化KL散度，与变分推断相联系。

Result: 模拟实验：后向一致性正则化使经验拟鞅总量$\hat Q$降低43%-58%，稳定性提前28%-44%达到；回声状态遗忘率受$\rho$界约束；增量总和管$R_t$实现100%同时覆盖率（但偏保守），实际中缺陷尾代理$\hat Q_t$更实用。PhysioNet 2012 ICU数据：RMRNN匹配RNN的死亡率预测AUC，但提前13小时达到稳定表示。FRED-MD：概念漂移下将一个月前预测误差降低约4倍。UCI Human Activity Recognition：后过渡跟踪误差更低且呈几何衰减。

Conclusion: 后向一致性为RNN隐藏状态稳定性提供了严格的理论框架（拟反向鞅），正则化可显著提升稳定性和收敛速度，且与变分推断有理论联系。方法在多个真实场景（ICU死亡率预测、宏观经济预测、活动识别）中验证了有效性。但作者明确指出**不声称普适性**，保证在所述假设条件下成立。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks maintain a hidden state $h_t$, but its probabilistic meaning is often unclear. We study hidden-state stability through \emph{backward coherence}: the extent to which $h_t$ can be reconstructed from $h_{t+1}$ by a learned backward projector $g_φ$. Under contraction and summable backward drift, the hidden-state sequence forms a quasi-reverse-martingale. This yields almost-sure convergence, rates under mixing, an interpretable limiting representation, finite pathwise stopping times, and a theoretical framework for time-uniform confidence sequences.
  Simulations support the theory. Backward-coherence regularisation reduces the empirical quasi-martingale total $\hat Q$ by $43$--$58%$, reaches stability $28$--$44%$ earlier than an unregularised RNN, and gives tracking-error recovery consistent with geometric bounds. Additional tests confirm echo-state forgetting rates bounded by $ρ$ and verify the increment-sum tube $R_t$ with $100%$ simultaneous coverage, although $R_t$ is conservative; in practice, the defect-tail proxy $\hat Q_t$ is the more useful monitor. The backward-coherence loss is also equivalent to minimising a Kullback--Leibler divergence in a Gaussian backward model, linking the method to variational inference. Extensions cover $φ$-mixing inputs, change-point tracking, and finite-sample concentration.
  Three real-data studies further validate the approach. On PhysioNet 2012 ICU data, the Reverse Martingale RNN (RMRNN) matches RNN mortality-prediction AUC while reaching stable representations 13 hours earlier. On FRED-MD, it reduces one-month-ahead forecast error by about fourfold under concept drift. On UCI Human Activity Recognition, it maintains lower post-transition tracking error with geometric decay. The guarantees apply under the stated assumptions; universality is not claimed.

</details>


### [190] [PAI: Preserving Amplitude Information in Representation-Based Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08935)
*Kang Zhang,Wei Jian Lau,Shoushou Ren,Dong Lin,Joon Son Chung,Chuanhao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有基于表示的时间序列异常检测方法存在振幅信息丢失问题，本文提出PAI评分方案，通过诊断模块和分数增强函数显式保留振幅信息，显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示的时间序列异常检测算法虽然表现优异，但存在一个关键缺陷：学习到的嵌入向量是振幅无关的（amplitude-agnostic），丢失振幅信息导致在与振幅相关的异常检测任务上性能下降，且这一问题在所有现有基于表示的方法中普遍存在。

Method: PAI包含两个互补模块：（1）诊断模块：在同一表示库上比较余弦评分和欧氏评分，检验表示是否已捕获振幅信息；（2）最终分数增强函数：计算逐点中位数和MAD偏差分数以及局部均值偏移分数，将其与表示分数融合生成最终异常分数。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U-Eva和TAB UV数据集上，PAI改进了所有四种评估的基于表示的方法，平均VUS-PR增益分别达到98.4%和36.8%。PaAno+PAI组合表现最佳，超越SOTA方法15%。

Conclusion: 显式保留振幅信息对于基于表示的时间序列异常检测至关重要，这一因素在现有评分方案中未被充分重视。

Abstract: Representation-based time-series anomaly detection algorithms significantly outperform other methods on diverse anomaly detection tasks. However, we notice that they suffer from a major limitation in our evaluation - their learned embeddings are often amplitude-agnostic. Losing amplitude information can degrade performance on amplitude related anomalies, and this failure is prevalent across all existing representation-based methods. To address aforementioned issues, we propose a new anomaly scoring scheme named PAI. PAI consists of two complementary modules, a diagnostic module and a final score augmentation function. The diagnostic module compares cosine and Euclidean scoring on the same representation bank to test whether amplitude information is already captured in the learned representation. Then in final score augmentation function, PAI computes a point-wise median and MAD deviation score and a local mean-shift score-which are fused with the representation score to produce the final anomaly score. On the TSB-AD-U-Eva and TAB UV datasets, PAI improves all four evaluated representation-based methods across every reported metric, achieving average VUS-PR gains of 98.4% and 36.8%, respectively. Among all evaluated combinations, PaAno + PAI achieves the best performance, outperforming the state-of-the-art method by 15%. Further evaluation on bootstrap confidence intervals, anomaly-type breakdowns, and a TS2Vec input-normalization ablation further support the proposed scheme. These results suggest that explicitly retaining amplitude information is important for representation-based time-series anomaly detection, which has been underemphasized in existing scoring schemes. Code is available at: https://github.com/pantheon5100/PAI

</details>


### [191] [From Hazard Functions to Language Space: Cox-Supervised Distillation of Survival Risk into a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08945)
*Nicholas I-Hsien Kuo,Blanca Gallego,Louisa Jorm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Cox比例风险模型预测的生存风险信息迁移至生成式大语言模型中，通过将结构化临床数据转化为文本提示，微调Qwen模型生成患者特异性生存风险。在三个数据集上，该方法虽以文本生成任务训练，却取得了与常规生存分析损失相当的性能，且隐状态分析显示模型在潜在空间中形成了连续的风险梯度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将传统统计生存分析模型（Cox比例风险模型）预测的时间-事件风险信息迁移至生成式大语言模型中的可行性，旨在为大语言模型赋予时间-事件推理能力。

Method: 提出基于文本的生存分析流程：将结构化临床协变量转换为文本提示，以Cox模型预测作为训练目标，对Qwen大语言模型进行微调，使其生成患者特异性生存风险。训练采用文本生成任务范式而非传统生存分析损失函数。

Result: 在GBSG2、ACTG320和WHAS500三个数据集上，该方法在保留集上的区分度和校准度均具竞争力；t-SNE可视化显示模型隐状态中存在平滑的风险梯度，表明模型将生存风险表示为连续结构而非离散类别。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够内化生存风险结构并支持校准预测，为语言模型中的时间-事件推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate whether information about time-to-event risk estimated by a Cox proportional hazards model can be transferred into a generative large language model. We propose a text-based survival modelling pipeline in which structured clinical covariates are converted into text prompts and a Qwen-based large language model is fine-tuned to generate patient-specific survival risk using Cox model predictions as a training target. Across GBSG2, ACTG320, and WHAS500, the model achieves competitive held-out discrimination and calibration despite being trained as a text-generation task rather than with a conventional survival-analysis loss. We further analyse the geometry of the model's hidden states, where t-SNE visualisations reveal smooth risk gradients in latent space, suggesting that the model represents survival risk as a continuous structure rather than isolated risk categories. Together, these findings suggest that large language models can internalise survival-risk structure while supporting calibrated prediction, providing a route towards time-to-event reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [192] [Self-Consistent Generative Paths via Admissible Random Variational Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08953)
*Lei Luo,Yingzhen Zhang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自洽生成路径（Self-Consistent Generative Path）的概念，将生成模型统一为随机变分传输算子的随机不动点，并推导出随机不动点路径残差（R-FPR）用于诊断、正则化和自适应采样，涵盖扩散、流匹配、一步生成、VAE、GAN及自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一视角主要描述生成路径如何构建（如扩散、流匹配、蒸馏等），但缺乏一个互补性理论框架来回答：何时生成的概率路径是自洽的？即如何判断路径本身是否满足某种局部最优性条件。

Method: 定义自洽生成路径为可容许局部变分传输校正的随机不动点。局部校正由随机变分传输算子指定，包含散度/几何项、能量项和结构约束。该框架包含随机正则化最优传输近端步骤作为结构化实例，同时允许非OT散度、隐变量核、对抗约束、因果离散核和终端一步映射。推导出随机不动点路径残差（R-FPR）度量实际生成路径与可容许局部校正之间的差距。

Result: 证明良定性、随机不动点存在性与吸引性、非压缩性存在性、残差到生成误差的界、经验残差集中性、代理摄动界、连续时间极限，以及算子级泛化与模型特定推论。

Conclusion: 将端点匹配问题转化为路径自洽性检验，提供残差控制原则用于故障诊断、训练正则化和自适应采样，适用于扩散、流、一步生成、VAE、GAN/WGAN和自回归生成器。

Abstract: Modern generative models often define an entire probability path from a simple prior to the data law, rather than only an endpoint map. Diffusion models follow stochastic denoising paths, flow matching learns transport fields, consistency and distillation methods compress paths into one or a few steps, adversarial models match terminal distributions, and VAEs generate through latent kernels. Existing unifying views mainly describe how such paths are constructed. We study a complementary question: when is a generated probability path self-consistent? We define a self-consistent generative path as a random fixed point of admissible local variational transport corrections. In this framework, a local correction is specified by a random variational transport operator combining a divergence or geometry term, an energy term, and a structural constraint. The framework contains random regularized optimal-transport proximal steps as a structured instance, while also allowing non-OT divergences, latent kernels, adversarial constraints, causal discrete kernels, and terminal one-step maps. The theory yields a random fixed-point path residual (R-FPR), which measures the gap between the actual generated path and an admissible local correction. We prove well-posedness, random fixed-point existence and attraction, non-contractive existence, residual-to-generation error bounds, empirical residual concentration, proxy perturbation bounds, continuous-time limits, and operator-level generalization with model-specific corollaries. The resulting theory turns endpoint matching into path self-consistency testing and provides a residual-control principle for diagnosing failures, regularizing training, and guiding adaptive sampling across diffusion, flow, one-step, VAE, GAN/WGAN, and autoregressive generators.

</details>


### [193] [From inverse problems to neural operators: prediction, mechanism, and generalization of data-driven models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08956)
*Conor Rowan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文梳理了科学机器学习中多种建模策略（从微分方程、稀疏辨识、神经ODE到神经算子）之间的共同结构，指出它们本质上都是定义输入-输出关系的数据驱动方法，并基于哲学视角提出只有某些模型具备机制发现和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习领域涌现了多种看似迥异的建模策略（如SINDy、Neural ODE、神经算子等），但它们之间的关系尚不清晰，缺乏统一的分类学框架。作者希望从科学哲学的视角，揭示这些方法的共同结构，并为它们各自的适用场景提供理论指导。

Method: 借鉴科学哲学中关于科学模型和机制的文献，对各类建模策略进行概念分析，指出它们仅在所假设的输入-输出关系模型类上存在差异。同时基于物理系统的数据源自简约微分方程这一论点，论证只有特定模型能实现机制发现。

Result: 揭示了从反问题到神经算子等多种建模策略具有共同的结构，差异仅在于定义的输入-输出关系的模型类假设不同。论证了只有部分模型（如符号化的稀疏微分方程）能够实现机制发现和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 统一了科学机器学习中看似分散的建模策略，为不同方法的适用场景提供了哲学层面的洞察，指导研究者根据任务需求（预测 vs. 机制发现）选择合适的建模策略。

Abstract: Scientists have historically relied on mathematical models based on differential equations to relate system inputs -- forces, fluxes, or heat sources -- to outputs, such as displacement, velocity, concentration, and temperature. These models rely on deep domain knowledge to determine the form of the governing differential equation, which is then calibrated with data by solving an inverse problem. In recent years, the field of Scientific Machine Learning has introduced a variety of alternative modeling strategies for physical systems. A method called Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics learns the governing equation as a sparse linear combination of terms in a user-defined library. Neural Ordinary Differential Equations construct the governing equation by taking in the state and its derivatives at the input layer of a neural network. Entirely foregoing the modeling framework of differential equations, neural operators directly learn a non-linear mapping between the system inputs and outputs. From inverse problems to neural operators, all of these modeling strategies can be conceptualized as data-driven machinery to predict a system's response over a range of inputs. It is then natural to wonder how exactly these various strategies relate to each other, and whether they can be neatly taxonomized. Drawing from the philosophical literature on scientific models, we argue that many model types have a common structure, differing only in the assumed model class of the input-output relation they define. Connecting to philosophical ideas on mechanism, and arguing that data from physical systems arises from solutions to parsimonious differential equations, we propose that only certain models are capable of mechanism discovery, and thus generalization. Our analysis is intended to unite apparently disparate modeling strategies and provide insight into their appropriate use cases.

</details>


### [194] [C$^3$ache: Accelerating World Action Models with Cross Inference Chunk Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08962)
*Weisen Zhao,Lam Nguyen,Zhicong Lu,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C³ache方法，通过跨推理块缓存和复用去噪残差，在不影响任务成功率的情况下，将World Action Models的推理速度提升至2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs虽比标准VLA策略泛化能力更强，但其推理计算成本高昂——每个推理块都需要昂贵的去噪过程。现有加速方法仅关注单一块内去噪轨迹的计算复用，忽略了跨块之间的冗余。

Method: 提出C³ache，一种无需训练的方法，在平滑行为执行过程中，将相邻推理块在同一去噪步的残差进行缓存和复用，从而消除跨块计算冗余。

Result: 在Fast-WAM骨干网络的基准测试上，C³ache实现了总壁钟推理时间最高2.5倍的加速，且任务成功率的下降可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过跨块残差缓存机制，有效消除了WAM推理中的冗余计算，实现了显著的加速效果，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) generalize better than standard Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies to novel motions and environments, because a video-modeling objective lets them learn from abundant unlabeled video rather than scarce labeled robot demonstrations. This generalization is computationally expensive. To complete a task, a WAM runs over multiple inference chunks, and each chunk requires a costly denoising process. Existing acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching and reusing computation within a single chunk's denoising trajectory. Our empirical analysis reveals a substantial source of redundancy they overlook: redundancy across chunks. When a robot executes a smooth behavior, the residuals computed at a given denoising step are strongly correlated from one chunk to the next. We introduce C$^3$ache, a training-free method that caches and reuses these residuals across inference chunks at the same denoising step. Experiments on benchmarks with a Fast-WAM backbone show that C$^3$ache achieves up to a $2.5\times$ speedup in total wall-clock inference time, with negligible degradation in task success rate.

</details>


### [195] [Online Learning with Recency: Algorithms for Sliding-window Streaming Multi-armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08977)
*Vladimir Braverman,Chen Wang,Liudeng Wang,Samson Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对单遍滑动窗口流式多臂老虎机问题，在有限内存约束下系统研究了纯探索和遗憾最小化任务，证明了纯探索中精确找到最优臂在次线性内存下不可行而近似最优臂存在高效算法，并为遗憾最小化给出了内存-遗憾权衡的严格理论界限和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受在线学习中近因效应启发，将滑动窗口机制引入流式多臂老虎机模型，以更贴近实际场景中只有最近到达的臂才有效的特性，填补了该方向理论分析的空白。

Method: 针对单遍流式设定，定义了滑动窗口带宽参数W和有限内存约束（存储臂的数量），分别对纯探索和遗憾最小化问题进行理论分析：对纯探索证明了下界（次线性内存下精确找最优不可行）并设计了近似最优臂的高效算法；对遗憾最小化定义了新的遗憾概念并推导了单遍算法下内存-遗憾的最优权衡关系。

Result: 纯探索：精确找最优臂在次线性内存下困难，但近似最优臂存在高效算法；遗憾最小化：给出了严格的单遍算法内存-遗憾权衡界限，并通过实验验证了样本量、遗憾值与内存之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 滑动窗口流式MAB模型在有限内存约束下具有独特的理论性质，纯探索和遗憾最小化任务面临不同的内存需求挑战，为实际流式决策问题提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Motivated by the recency effect in online learning, we study algorithms for single-pass *sliding-window streaming multi-armed bandits (MABs)* in this paper. In this setting, we are given $n$ arms with unknown sub-Gaussian reward distributions and a parameter $W$. The arms arrive in a single-pass stream, and only the most recent $W$ arms are considered valid. The algorithm is required to perform pure exploration and regret minimization with limited memory, defined as the number of stored arms. The model is a natural extension of the streaming multi-armed bandits model (without the sliding window) that has been extensively studied in recent years. We provide a comprehensive analysis of both the pure exploration and regret minimization problems with the model. For pure exploration, we prove that finding the best arm is hard with sublinear memory while finding an approximate best arm admits an efficient algorithm. For regret minimization, we explore a new notion of regret and give sharp memory-regret trade-offs for any single-pass algorithm. We complement our theoretical results with experiments, demonstrating the trade-offs between sample, regret, and memory.

</details>


### [196] [Heterophily-Aware Adaptive Knowledge Distillation for Hypergraph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08978)
*Joohee Cho,David Yoon Suk Kang,Yunyong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HADES是一种针对超图神经网络的异质性感知自适应蒸馏方法，通过量化节点异质性来调节教师知识迁移，使轻量学生模型在推理速度提升最高12.3倍的同时，性能甚至超越教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到超图神经网络在异质性节点（即通过语义多样化的超边连接的节点）上预测性能显著下降，表明教师知识的可靠性因节点而异，因此需要一种能感知异质性的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出HADES方法，首先量化节点的异质性，将其作为教师可靠性的估计指标，进而利用该指标调节蒸馏过程中教师知识向学生模型的迁移强度。

Result: 在真实超图数据集上，HADES在不同HNN教师和蒸馏目标下持续提升学生模型性能；多数情况下学生模型预测性能超越教师，同时推理速度提升最高达12.3倍。

Conclusion: HADES有效解决了超图知识蒸馏中教师知识可靠性异质的问题，实现了更高效且性能更优的轻量学生模型训练。

Abstract: Hypergraph knowledge distillation aims to retain the predictive performance of a hypergraph neural network (HNN) teacher while reducing inference costs through a lightweight student model. In this work, we observe that HNNs exhibit substantially lower prediction performance on heterophilic nodes connected through semantically diverse hyperedges, indicating that the reliability of teacher knowledge varies across nodes. Motivated by this observation, we propose HADES, a heterophily-aware adaptive distillation method for hypergraph neural networks. HADES quantifies node heterophily and leverages it as an estimate of teacher reliability to modulate the transfer of teacher knowledge during distillation. Experimental results on real-world hypergraphs demonstrate that HADES consistently improves student performance across different HNN teachers and distillation objectives. In many cases, the resulting student models surpass the predictive performance of their teachers while achieving up to 12.3 times faster inference.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Neural Collapse: Task-Intrinsic Geometry Governs Neural Representations in Modular Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08985)
*Hu Tan,Kuo Gai,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统解释了神经网络在模加法任务中为何会收敛到二维循环几何结构而非神经坍缩（NC）预言的单纯形等角紧框架（ETF），并从层间非均匀训练机制、子空间锁定后的平面内动力学、以及跨熵与权重衰减之间的权衡三个角度给出了形式化论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经坍缩（NC）理论预测分类器终端表征应形成(K-1)维单纯形ETF，但模加法任务中网络却进入二维循环几何结构（权重和嵌入均分布在圆上）。作者旨在解释这一矛盾现象背后的机制，理解为何任务结构会导致不同的表征几何。

Method: 1）提出层间非均匀训练机制：下游分类器权重先被密集跨熵梯度驱动为秩2等角配置，上游嵌入随后被反向传播梯度约束到同一平面，同时权重衰减抑制正交分量。2）子空间锁定后的平面内动力学被建模为S^1上的熵正则化传输问题，结合模加法标签退化为相位对齐，其极小解为Z/PZ的单频特征（即圆上的等角点）。3）通过理论分析量化了单纯形ETF与循环解在跨熵和权重衰减代理（Schatten范数）下的优势差异，推导出临界权重衰减阈值λ_crit = Θ(1/K)。

Result: 循环秩2解在权重衰减代理下享有Θ(K)的优势，而单纯形ETF仅获得O(1)的跨熵优势；由此推导出临界阈值λ_crit = Θ(1/K，解释了为何模加法中网络偏好循环几何而非NC预测的单纯形ETF。同时解释了"先grokking"现象：分类器权重先形成循环结构，嵌入随后对齐。

Conclusion: 模算术上的grokking现象并非仅由最大分离（NC）驱动，而是由分离性、对称性与复杂度之间的任务结构化权衡共同决定。该工作为理解任务结构如何影响表征几何提供了理论框架。

Abstract: While neural collapse (NC) predicts that a $K$-class-balanced classifier should organize terminal representations as a $(K-1)$-dimensional simplex equiangular tight frame (ETF), modular addition consistently enters a different regime: networks compress to a two-dimensional cyclic geometry in which both classifier weights and token embeddings lie on circles. We refine the explanation of this phenomenon in three directions. First, we formalize a layerwise non-uniform training mechanism: downstream classifier weights are driven by dense cross-entropy gradients into a rank-2 equiangular configuration before upstream embeddings fully reorganize, and once this classifier plane forms, backpropagated feature gradients constrain embedding motion to the same plane while weight decay suppresses orthogonal components. Second, after this subspace locking, the induced in-plane dynamics admit an entropy-regularized transport interpretation on $S^1$; combined with modular-addition labels, this reduces embedding formation to phase alignment, whose minimizers are single-frequency characters of $\mathbb{Z}/P\mathbb{Z}$ and hence equal-angle points on a circle. Third, we quantify why this solution prevails over NC: a simplex ETF gains only an $O(1)$ advantage in cross-entropy, whereas the cyclic rank-2 solution enjoys a $Θ(K)$ advantage under Schatten or weight-decay surrogates, yielding a critical threshold $λ_{\mathrm{crit}} = Θ(1/K)$. Our results explain both why classifier weights move first and why embeddings subsequently align with them, showing that grokking on modular arithmetic is governed not by maximal separation alone but by a task-structured trade-off between separation, symmetry, and complexity.

</details>


### [198] [LEAF: A Learning-Enabled ADMM Framework for Accelerated Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08993)
*Binh Nguyen,Trinh Tran,Truong X. Nghiem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEAF框架，通过输入凸神经网络（ICNN）学习目标函数的Moreau包络，得到MEL-ADMM和sMEL-ADMM两种加速凸优化算法，在保持凸性和光滑性的同时显著降低模型复杂度，实现比经典ADMM快一个数量级的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习驱动的优化方法直接学习高维算子，模型复杂且数据效率低。本文旨在通过学习标量值的Moreau包络来降低复杂度，同时保持凸优化问题的关键结构性质。

Method: 利用输入凸神经网络（ICNN）近似目标函数的Moreau包络，所学习的模型保持凸性和光滑性。在此基础上设计了MEL-ADMM及其分裂变体sMEL-ADMM，通过嵌入凸性保证近似精度和结构性质。

Result: 理论证明了所提方法在所学模型下的收敛性和可行性，收敛速率与经典ADMM相当但每轮计算成本更低。实验表明比现有最优求解器快一个数量级，同时保持低最优性差距。

Conclusion: LEAF框架通过学习Moreau包络而非直接学习高维算子，在显著加速凸优化求解的同时保证了理论收敛性，为学习驱动优化提供了一种高效且可理论的方案。

Abstract: We propose LEAF, a learning-enabled ADMM framework for accelerated convex optimization. The key idea is to approximate the Moreau envelope of the objective function using an Input Convex Neural Network (ICNN), resulting in a learned model that preserves convexity and smoothness. This leads to the proposed Moreau Envelope Learning ADMM (MEL-ADMM) and its splitting variant sMEL-ADMM. Unlike existing approaches that learn high-dimensional operators directly, LEAF learns a scalar-valued Moreau envelope, significantly reducing model complexity and improving data efficiency. The framework accommodates a broad class of convex problems with smooth and non-smooth objectives. By embedding convexity explicitly through the ICNN architecture, the proposed approach maintains high approximation accuracy while preserving key structural properties of the optimization problem. Both MEL-ADMM and sMEL-ADMM are developed with theoretical guarantees of convergence and feasibility under the learned model. Rigorous analysis shows that the proposed methods achieve convergence rates comparable to classical ADMM while reducing per-iteration computational cost. Numerical experiments demonstrate up to an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art solvers while maintaining low optimality gaps

</details>


### [199] [Understanding Quantization-Aware Training: Gradients at Quantized Weights Bias to the Low-Loss Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09012)
*Hanyang Li,Jianhao Ma,Ying Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的几何框架来解释后训练量化（PTQ）在激进比特位宽下的失败原因以及量化感知训练（QAT）的恢复机制。该框架将全精度训练建模为宽阔"山谷"中的低损失"河流"，量化网格接近"盆地"宽度时，PTQ可能选择盆地外的高损失点，而STE-based QAT通过梯度感知谷壁方向，引导量化点回到盆地内。实验在视觉和语言模型上验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ在中等位宽下高效准确，但在激进位宽下性能骤降；QAT虽昂贵但能恢复精度。现有工作缺乏统一的几何解释来说明PTQ失败和QAT恢复的根本原因。

Method: 提出"山谷-河流-盆地"几何框架：全精度训练轨迹为低损失河流，周围为平坦盆地，盆地外损失陡增。量化网格宽度与盆地宽度可比时，PTQ的局部优化目标（舍入、Hessian二阶重构）可能选到盆地外的高损失点。STE-based QAT在前向传播中使用量化权值计算梯度，反向传播更新全精度权值，梯度感知谷壁方向产生向内分量，将后续量化迭代拉回盆地。通过局部景观模型形式化该机制，并证明在局部量化器兼容性假设下的有限时间恢复。

Result: 在视觉和语言模型上的多种神经网络量化方案下，实验验证了PTQ的"穿越盆地"失败模式和QAT的对应恢复机制。

Conclusion: 该几何框架统一解释了PTQ失败和QAT恢复的机制，为理解量化方法的本质行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) converts a trained full-precision model into low-bit weights without task-level retraining, while quantization-aware training (QAT) incorporates quantization into the training loop. Although PTQ is efficient and often accurate at moderate bitwidths, it can fail sharply at aggressive bitwidths; QAT is more expensive but can often recover the lost accuracy. We propose a unified geometric framework that explains both PTQ failure and QAT recovery. We model full-precision training as following a low-loss \emph{river} inside a wider \emph{valley}: a normal neighborhood of the river forms a nearly flat \emph{basin}, while leaving this basin incurs a sharp loss increase. When the quantization grid is comparable to the basin width, local PTQ objectives, including rounding and Hessian-based second-order reconstruction, can select a high-loss deployed quantized point outside the basin even when nearby low-loss quantized points exist. In this regime, straight-through-estimator-based QAT has a useful bias: it evaluates gradients at the deployed quantized weights while updating latent full-precision weights, causing the gradient to sense the valley wall and acquire an inward component that steers subsequent quantized iterates back into the basin. We formalize this mechanism through a local landscape model, construct a geometric PTQ failure mode, and prove finite-time QAT recovery under local quantizer-compatibility assumptions. Experiments across vision and language models under multiple neural-network quantization schemes corroborate the predicted basin-crossing failure of PTQ and the corresponding recovery mechanism of QAT.

</details>


### [200] [Structural Grid Descriptors Predict Within-Task Solver Success on ARC-AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09026)
*Ayan Pendharkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了符号化ARC-AGI求解器在中间网格状态下的结构性质是否能够预测求解成功与否，通过条件互信息框架验证。在44,800次运行中，手工设计的网格描述符在50%轨迹完成度时能有效区分成功与失败的运行（平均AUC=0.885），预测能力主要集中在一个网格复杂度轴上。该结果跨求解器架构泛化，早期停止策略可显著降低计算开销，同时发现DSL原语库在大量任务上存在覆盖局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究中间网格状态的结构性质是否包含预测求解成功与否的信息，即验证条件互信息I(X;Y|task) > 0的假设。

Method: 使用两种架构不同的求解器（beam search和Stochastic DFS），在400个ARC任务、28种配置下运行44,800次。手工设计网格描述符，在50%轨迹完成度时测量特征，使用AUC评估区分能力，并通过跨求解器迁移、预注册验证集、配置残差化等分析确保结果稳健性。

Result: 平均AUC=0.885（p<0.001）；跨求解器迁移AUC=0.747-0.762；预注册验证集AUC=0.765；配置残差化后AUC达0.927和0.896；早期停止减少beam search计算33.6%且保留98.9%解，退化轨迹检测减少SDFS计算65.3%且无解丢失；229/400任务DSL原语库无法产生有效转换。

Conclusion: 中间网格状态的结构性质（特别是网格复杂度）预测了求解成功与否，该信号非由求解器容量或得分轨迹驱动，且跨架构泛化。基于此可显著降低计算开销。同时揭示了DSL覆盖范围的局限性。

Abstract: We ask whether structural properties of intermediate grid states predict whether a symbolic ARC-AGI solver will succeed, framed as a test of conditional mutual information I(X;Y|task) > 0. Across 44,800 runs spanning two architecturally distinct solvers (beam search and Stochastic DFS), 400 ARC tasks, 28 configurations per solver, and both training and evaluation splits, hand-crafted grid descriptors measured at 50% trajectory completion discriminate successful from failed runs within the same task (mean within-task best-feature AUC = 0.885, p < 0.001 under within-task label permutation). Most predictive content lies along a single grid-complexity axis. The result generalizes across solver architectures: a feature selected on one solver predicts success on the other with AUC 0.747-0.762 in all four transfer directions (p < 0.001, leakage controlled). On a pre-registered held-out set of 41 reliable tasks, the frozen feature n_components_final achieves AUC = 0.765 (95% CI [0.717, 0.810], p < 0.001), robust under task-clustered bootstrap resampling and cross-solver task collapsing. The signal is not explained by solver capacity (configuration-residualized AUC = 0.927 and 0.896 for beam search and SDFS, p < 0.001) and is only weakly coupled to score trajectories (R^2 approximately 0). Early stopping at 50% completion reduces beam-search compute by 33.6% while retaining 98.9% of solves; degenerate-trajectory detection reduces SDFS compute by 65.3% with no solve loss. Finally, on 229 of 400 evaluation tasks the DSL primitive library produces no valid transition from the input grid. This 0-step collapse is invariant to search budget and universally failed by beam search, indicating a DSL coverage limitation rather than a search-budget effect.

</details>


### [201] [TRIAGE: Dialectical Reasoning for Explainable Risk Prediction on Irregularly Sampled Medical Time Series with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09030)
*Hyeongwon Jang,Gyouk Chu,Changhun Kim,Joonhyung Park,Hangyul Yoon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE是一个通过训练LLM生成辩证推理（dialectical reasoning）来缓解临床风险极化问题的框架，在三个ISMTS基准上平均AUPRC提升3.3%，校准误差降低81%，且生成的临床推理质量优于基线方法20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在临床早期预警任务中会将分级风险压缩为过于自信的二元预测（风险极化），导致校准性差且无法实现跨患者可比性，无法满足临床对校准风险评分和可解释推理的双重需求。

Method: 提出TRIAGE框架，训练LLM生成关于竞争性临床结局的辩证推理（dialectical reasoning），通过引出结局特定（outcome-specific）的理由来缓解风险极化，使单个LLM输出基于明确临床推理的连续风险评分。

Result: 在三个ISMTS基准上，TRIAGE平均AUPRC提升3.3%，校准误差降低81%；LLM-as-a-judge评估显示其推理质量比基线的事后解释（post-hoc explanations）高出20%。

Conclusion: 通过辩证推理训练LLM可以有效缓解风险极化，在保持良好校准性的同时生成高质量的临床推理依据，为临床早期预警系统提供更可靠的决策支持。

Abstract: Clinical early warning systems built on electronic health records, in which clinical observations are recorded as irregularly sampled medical time series (ISMTS), must deliver both calibrated risk scores for patient triage and interpretable rationales that clinicians can verify. Large Language Models (LLMs) have been explored for this task, yet they collapse graded clinical risk into overconfident binary predictions. This risk polarization undermines both calibration and cross-patient comparability. To address this, we propose TRIAGE, a framework that trains an LLM to generate dialectical reasoning over competing clinical outcomes by eliciting outcome-specific rationales. This dialectical formulation mitigates risk polarization, enabling a single LLM to yield continuous risk scores grounded in explicit clinical reasoning. Evaluated on three ISMTS benchmarks, TRIAGE achieves an average AUPRC improvement of 3.3% and reduces calibration error by 81% compared to the competitive baselines. An LLM-as-a-judge assessment further shows that our rationales surpass post-hoc explanations from the baseline by 20% in clinical reasoning quality. The source code is available at https://github.com/HyeongWon-Jang/TRIAGE .

</details>


### [202] [DynaCF: Mitigating Shortcut Learning in Reward Models via Dynamic Counterfactual Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09043)
*Fengyuan Liu,Yongliang Miao,Zirui He,Yanguang Liu,Fei Sun,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DynaCF动态重加权框架，通过在训练中在线测量捷径敏感性并降低高敏感性样本权重，缓解奖励模型中的捷径学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型从成对偏好训练时容易利用表面捷径线索而非学习真正的响应质量，导致模型鲁棒性不足。

Method: 在优化过程中在线测量捷径敏感性：对样本施加保持语义的反事实扰动，追踪当前模型下的边界偏移和偏好翻转情况；将高捷径敏感性样本在Bradley-Terry目标中动态降低权重。

Result: 实验表明DynaCF在偏好建模中持续提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DynaCF通过动态重加权有效抑制了捷径学习，使奖励模型更依赖任务相关的偏好信号而非表面模式。

Abstract: Reward models trained from pairwise preferences often exploit superficial shortcut cues rather than learning true response quality. We propose DynaCF, a dynamic reweighting framework for mitigating shortcut learning in reward model training. Unlike static shortcut heuristics, DynaCF measures shortcut sensitivity online during optimization by applying semantics-preserving counterfactual perturbations and tracking the resulting margin shifts and preference flips under the current model. Samples with higher shortcut sensitivity are dynamically downweighted in the Bradley-Terry objective, encouraging the model to rely less on superficial patterns and more on task-relevant preference signals. Extensive experiments show that DynaCF consistently improves robustness in preference modeling.

</details>


### [203] [Decoy-Calibrated Failure Audits for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09046)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Ameya Gawde,Harshvardhan Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Janus流程，用于判断模型错误解释是否可信。该方法通过对比真实描述符与随机分配的假描述符的错误率提升，并在独立留出数据上验证，从而区分真实的错误模式与偶然发现。实验表明Janus能识别植入的失败模式，但在MuSiQue和LongBench v2上拒绝了SliceLine基线报告的所有高错误率描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计方法在测试多种候选错误解释时面临选择风险（selection risk）：观测到的最大效应可能反映真实失败模式，也可能只是多次尝试中的最佳结果。需要一种方法来判断哪些错误解释是真正可信的，而非仅因多重比较产生的偶然发现。

Method: Janus流程：(1) 固定模型、标注评估集和候选描述符列表；(2) 计算每个描述符的错误率提升（lift）；(3) 构造与真实描述符频率相同但随机分配给样本的假描述符作为"诱饵基线"；(4) 在发现数据上，描述符必须击败诱饵基线；(5) 在独立留出数据上重复验证，通过双重检验的描述符才被确认。

Result: 在多表查找任务的受控审计中，Janus成功识别植入的长链描述符及其交互失败模式，发现LLM常在查找链中途停止。在MuSiQue和LongBench v2上，SliceLine基线报告了多个高错误率区域，但Janus全部拒绝。消融实验显示：LongBench v2上未校准固定阈值报告20个描述符，加入诱饵基线后剩1个，留出检验后最后一个因错误率提升从0.36降至0.05而被拒绝。

Conclusion: Janus建立了"提出解释"与"报告解释"相分离的原则：候选解释可来自任何来源，但只有击败随机基线并在新数据上可复现的描述符才能成为审计发现。该方法有效控制了多重比较带来的虚假发现风险。

Abstract: Useful audits reveal not only how often a model fails, but also where its failures concentrate. An auditor may test many candidate explanations: long inputs, indirect questions, distracting evidence, or combinations of these factors. The risk is selection. The largest observed effect may reflect a real failure mode, or it may simply be the best result among many tried. We introduce Janus, a procedure for deciding when a proposed error explanation is credible enough to report. The goal is not to generate new explanations, but to decide which ones hold up. The auditor starts with a fixed model, a labeled evaluation set, and a frozen list of candidate explanations, which we call descriptors. Janus scores each descriptor by its error-rate lift, then compares real descriptors with fake ones that have the same frequencies but are randomly assigned to examples. A descriptor is confirmed only if it beats this decoy floor on the data used for discovery and then repeats on separate held-out data. In a controlled audit of multi-table lookup tasks, Janus identifies the planted failure, confirming long-chain descriptors and their interactions. The LLM often stops partway through the lookup chain instead of reaching the final answer. On two public benchmarks, MuSiQue and LongBench v2, the SliceLine baseline flags plausible high-error pockets, but Janus confirms none of them. Ablations show why both safeguards matter. On LongBench v2, an uncalibrated fixed threshold reports 20 descriptors, the decoy floor leaves one, and the holdout check rejects the last one after its lift shrinks from 0.36 to 0.05. The resulting principle separates proposing explanations from reporting them. Candidates may come from any source, but only those that beat decoys and replicate on fresh data become audit findings.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Convolution: Advancing Hypergraph Neural Networks with Hypergraph U-Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09051)
*Fuli Wang,Wei Qian,Daniel L. Lau,Gonzalo R. Arce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将U-Net架构引入超图数据，提出了并行层次化池化（PHPool）和反池化（PHUnpool）算子，通过层次聚类切割树状图在不同粒度下同时构建池化与反池化操作，在超图重建、分类和节点异常检测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图数据上卷积操作已取得进展，但U-Net架构因缺乏有效的池化和反池化操作而未被探索；现有图/超图池化方法通过顺序学习可能破坏局部结构，难以保留输入超图的结构信息。

Method: 受层次聚类启发，通过在不同粒度切割聚类树状图，一次性并行构建池化算子（PHPool）和反池化算子（PHUnpool）；PHPool以全局并行方式保持对原始超图结构的忠实度并高效计算，PHUnpool执行逆操作用于超图重建。

Result: 在超图重建模拟、超图分类和节点级异常检测任务上，所提方法优于现有最先进的图和超图深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的PHPool和PHUnpool算子有效解决了超图U-Net中池化/反池化设计的难题，全局并行设计优于传统顺序学习方法，为超图数据上的U-Net架构奠定了基础。

Abstract: Convolutions have successfully transitioned from image processing to the complex realm of non-Euclidean higher-order domains, particularly in hypergraphs. Despite the success in convolution, the exploration of a popular architecture named U-Net remains largely unexplored for hypergraph data due to the lack of well-defined pooling and unpooling operations. This work pioneers the study of U-Net architectures for hypergraph data, addressing the critical challenge of designing effective pooling and unpooling operations that retain maximal structural information from the input hypergraph. Motivated by hierarchical clustering, we propose to construct the pooling and unpooling operators all at once by cutting the clustering dendrogram at different granularities, named the Parallel Hierarchical Pooling (PHPool) and Unpooling (PHUnpool) operators. Unlike existing pooling methods that risk local structural damage through a sequential learning procedure, our PHPool operators are designed in a global and parallel manner to ensure fidelity to the original hypergraph structure with efficient computation while the PHUnpool operators are tailored to perform inverse operations of the PHPools for hypergraph reconstruction. We validate our model through hypergraph reconstruction simulation, hypergraph classification, and node-level anomaly detection, where it demonstrates superior performance over existing state-of-the-art graph and hypergraph deep learning methods.

</details>


### [205] [INFUSER: Influence-Guided Self-Evolution Improves Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09052)
*Siyu Chen,Miao Lu,Beining Wu,Heejune Sheen,Fengzhuo Zhang,Shuangning Li,Zhiyuan Li,Jose Blanchet,Tianhao Wang,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INFUSER 提出了一个迭代协同训练框架，包含 Generator（生成题目和参考答案）和 Solver（学习解题）两个角色，通过 influence score 和 DuGRPO 优化 Generator，使 document pool 转化为自适应课程，显著提升模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法要么依赖精心整理或教师生成的训练数据，要么在无监督生成时使用难度启发式奖励，这种奖励未必能真正提升 solver 的表现。需要一种能让 generator 生成对 solver 真正有益的题目的方法。

Method: 1) 提出 INFUSER 迭代协同训练框架，Generator 从非结构化文档池中生成题目和参考答案，Solver 通过标准正确性奖励进行训练。2) Generator 通过 optimizer-aware influence score 获得奖励，该分数衡量每个问题能否真正提升 solver 在目标分布上的表现。3) 针对连续且含噪声的 influence score，提出 DuGRPO（双归一化 GRPO）用于 generator 训练。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上，INFUSER 在 Olympiad 和 SuperGPQA 基准上相比强自我进化基线有超过 20% 的相对提升。8B INFUSER 协同进化的 generator 在数学和编程任务上优于冻结的 32B 思维链 generator。消融实验验证了每个设计选择的必要性。

Conclusion: INFUSER 通过 influence score 引导 generator 生成对 solver 真正有益的题目，将文档池转化为自适应课程，显著提升了推理能力。扩展到指令微调锚点和规则可验证 RLVR 数据进一步证明了框架的灵活性和泛化性。

Abstract: Self-evolution offers a scalable path to stronger reasoning: a pretrained language model improves itself with only minimal external supervision. Yet existing methods either depend on extensively curated or teacher-generated training data, or, when the generator runs unsupervised, reward it by a difficulty heuristic that need not improve the solver. We introduce INFUSER, an iterative co-training framework with two co-evolving roles: a Generator that drafts questions and reference golden answers from a pool of unstructured, automatically collected documents, and a Solver that improves by training on them. The solver is trained with standard correctness rewards against the generator-provided answers, while the generator is rewarded by an optimizer-aware influence score that measures whether each proposed question would actually improve the solver on the target distribution. Because this continuous, noisy influence score is poorly served by standard GRPO, we propose DuGRPO, a dual-normalized variant of GRPO, for generator training. Together, these turn the document pool into an adaptive curriculum that favors questions useful to the current solver, not just hard ones. On Qwen3-8B-Base, INFUSER outperforms strong self-evolution baselines with over 20% relative improvement on Olympiad and SuperGPQA benchmarks, and an 8B INFUSER co-evolving generator outperforms a frozen 32B thinking generator on math and coding. Ablations confirm each design choice is necessary, and two extensions, applying INFUSER to an instruction-finetuned anchor and augmenting it with rule-verifiable RLVR data, further demonstrate the flexibility and generalizability of the framework. Code is available at https://github.com/FFishy-git/INFUSER.

</details>


### [206] [Stage-1 Controls the Entropy Regime, Not the Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09059)
*Jianxiong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了VLM两阶段后训练中Stage-1（SFT/OPD）的实际作用。实验表明：不同Stage-1方法在域内性能差异极小（53-54%），主要改变的是策略熵（entropy regime）而非最终性能；OPD带来更高的初始熵、答案多样性和pass@16，但优势在RL后消失或很小（1.1-1.2分以内）。结论：Stage-1与熵状态强关联，但下游收益有限，OPD并非更好的RL预热方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VLM两阶段后训练中Stage-1（SFT vs OPD）究竟控制了哪些因素，特别是在域内性能、域外泛化和策略熵方面的实际影响，为RL预热方法的选择提供实证依据。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B作为学生模型、同模态72B VLM作为教师进行OPD，对比SFT、OPD和过训练SFT三种Stage-1初始化方式；在Geometry3K（域内）和MathVista（域外）上评估；测量策略熵、答案多样性和pass@16等指标。

Result: 1) 三种warm-start在域内Geometry3K上仅53-54%；2) 早期停止的SFT在域外MathVista上+2.1分，而过训练SFT下降-9.5分；3) OPD进入RL时熵显著更高且差异持续；4) OPD初始pass@16比SFT高+2.0到+5.2分，但RL后差距缩小到1.1分以内；5) MathVista上六个模型差距在1.2分以内。

Conclusion: Stage-1主要影响策略熵状态而非最终性能；OPD在初始阶段有熵和多样性优势，但RL后优势消失，不构成选择OPD作为RL预热方法的充分证据。

Abstract: Two-stage post-training -- a Stage-1 warm-start (supervised fine-tuning, SFT, or on-policy distillation, OPD) followed by Stage-2 reinforcement learning (RL) -- is increasingly used for vision-language models (VLMs). We ask what Stage-1 actually controls in a small-data study using Qwen2.5-VL-7B with a same-modality 72B VLM teacher for OPD. First, the three warm-starts reach a narrow $53$--$54\%$ band on Geometry3K internal validation, consistent with the narrow range reported by recent specialized methods; this setup provides little evidence that Stage-1 changes the in-domain endpoint. Second, a matched-recipe, early-stopped SFT improves out-of-domain MathVista by $+2.1$ points, reversing the $-9.5$-point drop of an over-trained variant. The clearest difference is the \emph{entropy regime}: OPD enters RL with substantially higher policy entropy than either SFT initialization, and the separation remains visible through the available trajectories. At the in-domain initialization, OPD also has higher answer diversity and pass@16 ($+2.0$ to $+5.2$ points over SFT), although problem-level bootstrap intervals show that the smaller contrast is uncertain. The advantage is absent after RL (endpoint pass@16 values within $1.1$ points) and on MathVista (six models within $1.2$ points). Our contribution is therefore a bounded empirical characterization: Stage-1 is strongly associated with the entropy regime in this setup, but the downstream payoff is small, localized, and not evidence that OPD is a better RL warm-start.

</details>


### [207] [OnlyDense: Reduced-Order Modeling for Lagrangian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09065)
*Tu Do,Shannon Ryan,Santu Rana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于函数空间视角的粒子系统动力学建模框架，将大规模拉格朗日模拟（如SPH、MPM）的状态视为希尔伯特空间中的轨迹，通过学习的神经基函数张成线性子空间进行降阶建模，仅用32个基函数即可对百万级粒子SPH模拟实现R²>0.99的重建与预测，统一了经典投影降阶与深度学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日模拟方法（SPH、MPM）在多尺度时空现象模拟中计算开销巨大（如宏观几何中的空穴生长、超高速撞击碎片云等），亟需高效的可扩展降阶建模方法。

Method: 将粒子系统状态视为函数，其演化视为希尔伯特空间中的轨迹；用学习的神经基函数张成线性子空间逼近状态空间，通过直接投影获得潜变量系数，避免非线性潜空间优化；基函数对应空间模态，类似于POD。
