Result: 在百万级粒子SPH模拟（含极端变形与碎裂）上，仅用32个基函数即可达到R²>0.99的重建与预测精度。

Conclusion: 该方法统一了经典投影降阶与深度学习，对离散点数具有不变性，为大规模粒子系统的高效动力学建模提供了可扩展框架。

Abstract: In science and engineering, Lagrangian simulation methods such as Smooth Particle Hydrodynamics (SPH) or Material Point Method (MPM) are often employed to study the behavior of dynamic systems. However, these methods can be prohibitively computationally expensive, particularly when simulating multi-scale spatial or temporal phenomena, e.g., void growth and coalescence within macro-scale geometries, structural failure of spacecraft components resulting from hypervelocity impact of space debris particles, etc. In contrast to graph-based methods, where the state of the system is understood as a discrete set of particles, we propose a learning framework for scalable representation and dynamics modeling of massive particle systems by treating the system state as a function and its evolution as a trajectory in Hilbert space. Rather than representing the state as a discrete set of particles or embedding it in a nonlinear latent manifold, we approximate the state space with a linear subspace spanned by learned neural basis functions. This parameterization enables direct projection to obtain latent coefficients and explicit access to the basis functions, avoiding optimization over a nonlinear latent space. The resulting representation admits a natural interpretation: latent variables correspond to coefficients in Hilbert space, and basis functions correspond to spatial modes, analogous to Proper Orthogonal Decomposition. The framework thus unifies classical projection-based reduced-order modeling with modern deep learning, while remaining invariant to the number of discretization points. Experiments on large-scale SPH simulations with over one million particles, including dynamic events with extreme deformation and fragmentation, demonstrate that the proposed method accurately reconstructs and predicts dynamics, achieving an R$^2$ score above $0.99$ with as few as $32$ basis functions.

</details>


### [208] [A Unifying Lens on Reward Uncertainty in RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09073)
*Ely Hahami,Yoel Zimmermann,Ray Zhou,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布性奖励模型作为解决RLHF中奖励黑客问题的正确框架，推导出闭合形式的有效奖励表达式，并证明现有启发式方法（均值聚合、最坏情况优化、不确定性加权优化）是该表达式的极限或截断形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的奖励黑客问题严重制约其效果，现有启发式缓解方法（如集成聚合）缺乏理论依据，需要更 principled 的解决方案。

Method: 从贝叶斯推断和KL-DRO两个视角出发，推导出KL正则化RLHF目标下的闭合形式有效奖励；通过极限分析和截断分析，揭示该表达式与现有启发式方法的联系。

Result: 得到悲观分支下的有效奖励表达式，并证明均值聚合、最坏情况优化、不确定性加权优化均是该表达式的特例或近似。

Conclusion: 分布性奖励模型是处理RM不确定性的正确对象，所提出的框架统一并解释了现有启发式方法，为更 principled 的RLHF实践提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is bottlenecked by \emph{reward hacking}, where the policy exploits errors in a proxy reward model (RM) and produces high RM scores without genuine quality gains. A natural mitigation is \emph{pessimism}: penalizing rewards in regions where the RM is uncertain. However, standard scalar RMs provide no principled notion of uncertainty. We argue that the right object is a \emph{distributional} reward model $p(r\mid x,y)$. Under either a Bayesian inference or a KL-distributionally robust optimization (KL-DRO) lens, the KL-regularized RLHF objective admits a closed-form effective reward $\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$. The pessimistic branch unifies the prior heuristics for RM ensemble aggregation: mean aggregation, worst-case optimization (WCO), and uncertainty-weighted optimization (UWO) all emerge as limits or truncations of this single expression. This also clarifies the implicit assumptions of each existing rule.

</details>


### [209] [Neural Legendre-Fenchel transform with Hessian Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09077)
*Basile Plus-Gourdon,Frank Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿射不变性的Hessian预条件策略，通过将函数围绕极小点仿射变形至标准抛物面，利用残差网络学习简化映射，从而提升Legendre-Fenchel变换数值近似的收敛速度和精度，尤其适用于病态函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Legendre-Fenchel变换在凸分析和机器学习中至关重要，但许多函数无法获得闭式共轭表达式，现有数值方法（如深度Legendre变换）在处理病态函数时面临挑战，亟需更高效稳定的近似方法。

Method: 将LF变换重新表述为射影对极性，利用其仿射不变性引入Hessian预条件：在极小点处进行仿射变形，使函数的二阶泰勒近似与标准抛物面重合（其共轭映射为单位映射）；使用初始化为接近单位映射的残差网络学习简化后的映射，再通过逆变形恢复原始共轭映射。预条件仅需初始化时一次特征分解和每次查询时两次矩阵-向量乘法。

Result: 在多种凸函数（包括高维基准）上的实验表明，该方法在共轭变换中实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的数值精度，对病态问题的改善尤为显著。

Conclusion: 所提预条件策略计算开销小（单次特征分解+每次查询两次矩阵乘法），能有效提升LF变换数值近似的性能，同时文中也讨论了方法的适用范围和局限性。

Abstract: The Legendre-Fenchel (LF) transform is a fundamental tool in convex analysis and machine learning that maps lower semi-continuous functions to their convex conjugates. In practice, when closed-form formula are not available for expressing convex conjugates of given functions, one must approximate them using various techniques. One recent such versatile numerical method is the deep Legendre transform method which relies on neural networks although it remains challenging particularly for tackling ill-conditioned functions. This work builds on the reformulation of the LF transform as a projective polarity. A notable property of this framework is its affine invariance. We leverage this affine invariance to introduce a Hessian-based preconditioning strategy. Specifically, we apply an affine deformation around a minimizer so that the second-order Taylor approximation of the function coincides with the canonical paraboloid, whose conjugation map is the identity. A residual network initialized near the identity can then learn this simplified mapping, while the original conjugation map is recovered through the inverse deformation. The proposed preconditioning incurs only a modest computational overhead, consisting of a single eigendecomposition during initialization and two matrix-vector multiplications per query. Experiments on a diverse set of convex functions, including high-dimensional benchmarks, demonstrate improved convergence rates and enhanced numerical accuracy of the conjugation, with particularly significant gains for ill-conditioned problems. Finally, we discuss the scope of applicability of our proposed method and highlight several of its limitations.

</details>


### [210] [The Hidden Bias of Process Reward Models:PRISM for Rewarding the Right Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09078)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Souradip Chakraborty,Armin Saghafian,Nihal Sharma,Rizal Fathony,Nam H Nguyen,C. Bayan Bruss,Amrit Singh Bedi,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRM存在由步级训练数据不平衡导致的隐藏偏见，标准交叉熵训练会放大这种偏见，导致高误报率。作者提出PRISM框架，通过对比学习、时间前瞻策略生成难负样本以及难度感知课程学习，显著降低误报率并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别出PRM因步级训练数据严重不平衡而产生的隐藏偏见，标准交叉熵训练会放大这一偏见，导致模型过度奖励看似正确实则错误的步骤（高假阳性率），进而对下游推理任务产生不对称的负面引导作用。

Method: 提出PRISM框架：(1) 从点式标签拟合转向可靠的相对比较，通过对比步级比较进行训练；(2) 利用时间前瞻策略生成硬负样本；(3) 采用难度感知课程学习优化对比步级间隔。无需额外人工标注。

Result: 在PRMBench上假阳性率降低22%，宏F1优于强判别式PRM；在引导解码和Best-of-N选择中准确率分别提升22%和33%。

Conclusion: 可信的过程监督不仅在于分配高奖励，更在于以正确的方式奖励正确的推理。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) improve credit assignment for reasoning by providing step-level feedback. However, we identify a hidden bias in PRMs caused by severe imbalance in step-level training data. Standard cross-entropy training amplifies this bias, causing PRMs to overcredit plausible but incorrect steps and produce high false-positive rates. We show that these false positives have an asymmetric downstream effect: false negatives mainly slow exploration, whereas false positives actively steer Best-of-N selection, guided decoding, and policy optimization toward flawed reasoning. This suggests that PRM training should shift from pointwise label fitting to reliable relative comparisons. To address this, we propose PRISM (Precision Ranking for Improved Step Modeling), a policy-aware PRM training framework that learns from contrastive step-level comparisons and hard negatives generated by a temporal lookahead strategy, requiring no new human labels. We further use a difficulty-aware curriculum to optimize the contrastive step margin. Across PRMBench and ProcessBench, PRISM substantially reduces false positives (22% on PRMBench) and improves macro F1 over strong discriminative PRMs. When applied to policy optimization and search tasks, including guided decoding and Best-of-N selection, it consistently improves accuracy (up to 22% for guided decoding and 33% for Best-of-N) and robustness. More broadly, trustworthy process supervision is not just about assigning high rewards, but about rewarding the right reasoning for the right reasons.

</details>


### [211] [FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4: Lightning Index Ultra-Long Context via Lookahead Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09079)
*Yan Wang,Qifan Zhang,Jiachen Yu,Tian Liang,Dongyang Ma,Xiang Hu,Zibo Lin,Chunyang Li,Zhichao Wang,Jia Li,Yujiu Yang,Haitao Mi,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA)范式，通过神经记忆索引器（基于DeepSeek-V4架构）预测未来上下文需求，仅保留查询关键KV块在GPU内存中，将物理KV缓存压缩至全上下文基线的13.5%，同时保持或略微提升下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM在解码过程中需要保持完整KV缓存，导致超长上下文服务时GPU内存严重瓶颈，亟需一种能主动预测并选择性保留关键KV块的高效推理范式。

Method: 提出Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA)推理范式，核心是构建神经记忆索引器（基于DeepSeek-V4架构），采用无骨干解耦训练策略，将索引器建模为标准双编码器架构，使用标准检索训练框架独立训练，无需加载大规模骨干模型到GPU内存。

Result: 在LongBench-v2、LongMemEval和RULER等长上下文评测集上，平均物理KV缓存足迹压缩至全上下文基线的13.5%，下游准确率平均提升0.6%绝对边际。在500K极端长度下，物理KV缓存开销降低超过90%，且不破坏骨干模型核心推理能力。

Conclusion: "少即是多"范式显著提升服务效率，同时作为有效的注意力去噪器，在依赖长期全局记忆的任务中表现出色，为超长上下文LLM推理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional LLMs keep the full KV cache loaded during decoding, causing a severe GPU memory bottleneck for ultra-long context serving. In this report, we propose Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA), a novel inference paradigm powered by a Neural Memory Indexer built upon the DeepSeek-V4 architecture. Rather than passively attending to all historical tokens, LSA proactively predicts future context demands and preserves only the query-critical KV chunks in the GPU memory. Crucially, we instantiate this architecture via a backbone-free decoupled training strategy. By formulating the indexer as a standard dual-encoder architecture, we train it independently using standard retrieval training frameworks without ever loading the massive backbone model into GPU memory.
  We demonstrate that this "less is more" paradigm significantly maximizes serving efficiency while acting as an effective attention denoiser in tasks that rely on long-term global memory. Across primary long-context evaluation suites (e.g., LongBench-v2, LongMemEval, and RULER), FM-DS-V4 compresses the average physical KV cache footprint down to merely 13.5% of the full-context baseline, while consistently preserving or slightly elevating downstream accuracy (+0.6% absolute margin on average). Crucially, at extreme 500K scales, FlashMemory suppresses the physical KV cache overhead by over 90% without destabilizing the backbone's core reasoning capacities.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond FLOPs: Benchmarking Real Inference Acceleration of LLM Pruning under a GEMM-Centric Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09080)
*Haozhe Hu,Hao Wu,Anhao Zhao,Longwei Ding,Peiran Yin,Yunpu Ma,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GEMM的剪枝方法分类体系，将现有剪枝方法按矩阵乘法的M、N、K维度重新组织，并构建了统一的基准测试框架。研究发现静态深度剪枝是最强的帕累托最优基线，在低质量损失(0%-4%)时为预填阶段最优，中等损失(5%-16%)时动态深度剪枝最优，高损失(17%-26%)时静态宽度剪枝最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的剪枝方法虽然目标相同（加速LLM推理），但不同剪枝方法在实际加速效果上严重依赖硬件和内核实现，导致不同剪枝家族的实用加速收益尚不明确，缺乏统一的理解框架。

Method: 提出一种以GEMM为中心的剪枝分类法，根据矩阵乘法的M、N、K维度重新组织现有剪枝方法；构建统一的基准测试框架，支持实现一致的跨剪枝设计空间对比，系统刻画加速-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 静态深度剪枝是最强的帕累托最优基线，且在内存受限场景中最接近理论加速上限。预填阶段：低质量损失(0%-4%)静态深度剪枝最优，中等损失(5%-16%)动态深度剪枝最优，高损失(17%-26%)静态宽度剪枝最优。

Conclusion: 首次建立了基于剪枝的LLM加速实用极限的统一视图，为未来的剪枝研究提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Pruning has emerged as a dominant paradigm for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference, spanning a broad spectrum of methods that remove computation across tokens, layers, heads, dimensions, and attention patterns. Despite sharing the same objective, these pruning approaches induce fundamentally different execution behaviors, causing realized speedups to depend heavily on hardware and kernel implementations. Consequently, the practical acceleration benefits of different pruning families remain poorly understood. In this work, we introduce a GEMM-centric taxonomy that reorganizes existing pruning methods according to the logical \textbf{M}, \textbf{N}, and \textbf{K} dimensions of general matrix multiplication (GEMM). Leveraging this abstraction, we build a unified benchmarking framework that enables implementation-consistent comparison across the pruning design space and systematically characterizes the acceleration--quality Pareto frontier. Our results show that static depth pruning remains the strongest Pareto-optimal baseline and stays closest to its theoretical acceleration upper bound in memory-bounded scenarios. During prefill, the frontier transitions from static depth at low quality loss (0\%--4\%), to dynamic depth at moderate loss (5\%--16\%), and finally to static width pruning at higher loss levels (17\%--26\%). These findings establish the first unified view of the practical limits of pruning-based LLM acceleration and provide guidance for future pruning research.\footnote{Code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning/tree/main/PruningInferSim}

</details>


### [213] [Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation for MLLM Reasoning with Global Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09091)
*Dongze Hao,Zhiwei Jin,Chen Chen,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对同策略蒸馏中梯度不稳定问题，提出全局归一化蒸馏策略优化（GNDPO），通过将原始KL分数转换为批级相对优势来稳定优化，在多模态推理任务中显著提升了训练鲁棒性和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏中，朴素token级蒸馏因异常状态的量级不匹配导致梯度不稳定，需要一种能稳定优化同时保留token级指导优势的方法。

Method: 提出GNDPO，将原始KL散度分数转换为批级别相对优势（全局归一化），以此缓解梯度爆炸，同时保留token级指导的优势。

Result: 在多模态推理任务上，GNDPO显著提升了训练鲁棒性和下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 全局归一化是一种实用且有效的稳定同策略蒸馏优化的方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an important post-training paradigm. By using a stronger teacher model to provide dense, fine-grained supervision for sampled trajectories, OPD offers a clear advantage over reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), which typically depends on sparse binary or outcome-based environmental feedback. However, naive token-level distillation can suffer from gradient instability, due to magnitude misalignment in outlier states. To address this issue, we propose Globally Normalized Distillation Policy Optimization (GNDPO), a practical method that stabilizes optimization by transforming raw KL scores into batch-level relative advantages. This normalization effectively mitigates gradient explosions while retaining the benefits of token-level guidance. Experimental results show that GNDPO substantially improves training robustness and downstream performance across multimodal reasoning tasks. The code is released at https://github.com/OPPO-Mente-Lab/GNDPO.

</details>


### [214] [From Shortcuts to Reasoning: Robust Post-Training of Theory of Mind with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09092)
*Jike Zhong,Yuxiang Lai,Ming Li,Yuheng Li,Wuao Liu,Behzad Dariush,Konstantinos Psounis,Shao-Yuan Lo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现当前理论心智（ToM）后训练存在"捷径"问题（任务通过利用虚假因果相关性可达99%准确率），提出无捷径数据集并研究Thinking-RFT（带可验证奖励和显式推理链的强化微调）相比SFT的效果。结果表明Thinking-RFT在所有场景中提升ToM能力6%，高阶推理提升10%，多模态提升7%，且泛化性和鲁棒性更好。推理与强化学习的联合效应是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论心智（ToM）是现代基础模型系统在现实世界中有效且安全运行必须掌握的技能，但现有后训练方法存在"捷径"问题（任务通过利用虚假因果相关性可达99%准确率），导致对ToM能力的虚假认知，亟需系统性解决这一混淆因素并探索更有效的ToM训练方法。

Method: 1）建立系统性框架检查ToM数据集中的捷径问题；2）发现可简化为纯状态追踪的问题（如"信念"）特别容易受捷径影响；3）在三个ToM上下文、四个无捷径数据集上，比较Thinking-RFT（带可验证奖励和显式推理链的强化微调）与SFT的效果。

Result: 1）Thinking-RFT在所有场景中提升ToM能力6%，高阶推理提升10%，多模态提升7%；2）Thinking-RFT在未见领域和高阶查询上泛化更好，对反事实更鲁棒；3）推理与RL的联合效应是关键，Thinking-RFT比Non-Thinking-RFT平均高7%；4）RFT通过让模型将推理锚定在关键词和状态变化等因果线索上发挥作用。

Conclusion: Thinking-RFT有效提升了ToM能力，尤其是在复杂高阶推理和多模态场景中；推理链与强化学习的结合是关键因素；研究为开发有效且鲁棒的ToM后训练数据集和推进关键ToM能力提供了有用指导。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) is a must-acquire skill for modern foundation model systems to operate effectively and safely in the real world. Recent works have explored honing ToM via post-training; however, we show that such progress is confounded by a pervasive "shortcut" issue: tasks can reach up to 99% accuracy by simply exploiting spurious causal correlations, leading to a false sense of ToM. Motivated by this, we first develop a framework to systematically examine ToM datasets for shortcuts and provide guidance for future development. We find that questions reducible to pure state tracking, such as "belief," are especially shortcut-prone compared to mind questions, such as "intention," where reasoning beyond tracking is required. Using four shortcut-free datasets across three ToM contexts, we then comprehensively study whether Reinforcement Fine-Tuning with verifiable rewards and explicit reasoning chains, called Thinking-RFT, elevates ToM beyond Supervised Fine-Tuning, or SFT. Our key findings are as follows. First, Thinking-RFT effectively improves ToM in all scenarios, with a 6% improvement over SFT, particularly in complex higher-order reasoning, with a 10% improvement over SFT, and multimodal cases, with a 7% improvement over SFT. It also generalizes notably better to unseen domains and higher-order queries while being more robust to counterfactuals. Second, ToM benefits specifically from the joint effect of reasoning and RL: Thinking-RFT outperforms Non-Thinking-RFT by 7% on average. Third, RFT works by learning to ground its reasoning on anchor cues, such as keywords and state changes, that correspond to causal factors. We believe our study is useful for developing effective and robust ToM post-training datasets and advancing critical ToM capabilities.

</details>


### [215] [Addressing Market Regime Changes and Heavy-Tailed Returns in Portfolio Optimization via Bayesian VAR and Elliptical Black-Litterman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09104)
*Daniil Mikriukov,Ruoyu Sun,Angelos Stefanidis,Jionglong Su,Zhengyong Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BAVAR-BLED算法，融合贝叶斯平均向量自回归与Black-Litterman椭圆分布模型，在TD3架构下利用Transformer和CNN实现考虑肥尾分布和时变重要性（体制变化）的资产配置，在道琼斯29只成分股十年数据上取得显著优于现有方法的成绩（夏普比率1.72，总收益57.26%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习投资组合优化框架存在两个关键缺陷：1）未能考虑实际市场中更为频繁的极端事件（肥尾分布）；2）将历史数据同质化处理，忽视时间重要性差异，导致模型在市场体制变化时失效。

Method: 提出BAVAR-BLED算法，在TD3架构中融合两种方法：BAVAR捕获多尺度时间特征的向量自回归表示，生成体制感知的收益期望和离散矩阵估计作为先验；BLED采用Student's t分布进行更现实的肥尾收益估计。使用Transformer网络构建观点（views），CNN估计风险厌恶系数，根据市场条件动态调整分配决策。

Result: 在道琼斯工业平均指数29只成分股跨越十年的市场数据上评估，BAVAR-BLED显著优于最先进方法，夏普比率1.72，索提诺比率2.70，总收益率57.26%。

Conclusion: BAVAR-BLED通过引入肥尾分布建模和体制感知的时间重要性机制，有效改善了深度强化学习在投资组合优化中的表现，特别是在极端事件和体制变化条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) frameworks for portfolio optimization have shown promise for their ability to learn allocation rules dynamically from market data. However, these models fail to account for fat-tailed returns, which characterize actual market behavior with more frequent extreme events. Furthermore, historical data is treated homogeneously, without accounting for temporal importance, leading models to fail during regime changes. We propose a new BAVAR-BLED algorithm that combines methods derived from Bayesian-Averaging Vector Autoregressive (BAVAR) and the Black-Litterman model using Elliptical Distributions (BLED) within a TD3 architecture. BAVAR captures a set of vector autoregressive representations that consider multi-scale temporal features, enabling adaptive allocation decisions based on regime-aware estimates of return expectations and dispersion matrices. These estimates serve as prior inputs to BLED, a model that uses Student's t-distributions, allowing for more realistic fat tail return estimates. The BAVAR-BLED algorithm uses transformer networks for view construction and CNNs for risk-aversion estimates, which modify dynamic allocation decisions based on market conditions. An evaluation of 29 Dow Jones Industrial Average constituents over a decade-long market period shows that BAVAR-BLED significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving Sharpe and Sortino ratios of 1.72 and 2.70, respectively, and total returns of 57.26%.

</details>


### [216] [Hybridizing Equilibrium Propagation with Ising Machines for Efficient Energy-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09112)
*Chen-Rui Fan,Bo Lu,Xing-Yu Wu,Tie-Jun Wang,Chuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于伊辛动力学的平衡传播框架，用扩展相空间动力学替代耗散Hopfield松弛，在保持局部两相学习规则的同时，降低了有效能垒、加速收敛、提升噪声鲁棒性，并在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上实现了与反向传播相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GPU训练深度神经网络能耗极高，促使研究者探索基于物理动力学和能量学习的方案（如平衡传播EP），但EP常因相空间压缩而陷入局部极小值，亟需改进。

Method: 引入伊辛动力学启发的平衡传播框架，将耗散Hopfield松弛替换为具有共轭变量的扩展相空间动力学，保持EP的局部两相学习规则，通过改变神经网络达到平衡的物理路径来改善训练。

Result: 该方法降低了有效能垒，加速了收敛，提升了噪声鲁棒性，并在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上训练深度卷积Hopfield网络，性能可比肩反向传播。

Conclusion: 基于伊辛动力学的扩展相空间方法有效克服了平衡传播的局部极小值问题，保持了局部学习优势，在多个基准数据集上展现出与反向传播相当的性能，为低能耗物理学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of artificial intelligence has led to substantial advances in deep neural networks. Nonetheless, conventional GPU-based training remains highly energy-demanding, motivating the exploration of physical dynamics and compatible energy-based learning schemes, such as equilibrium propagation (EP). EP-based training, however, frequently suffers from convergence to local minima due to phase-space contraction. Here we introduce an Ising-dynamics-inspired equilibrium-propagation framework in which dissipative Hopfield relaxation is replaced by an extended phase-space dynamics with conjugate variables. The resulting training paradigm keeps the local two-phase learning rule of EP while changing the physical route by which neural states reach equilibrium. We show that this dynamics lowers effective energy barriers, accelerates convergence, improves noise robustness, and trains deep convolutional Hopfield networks on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 with performance comparable to backpropagation.

</details>


### [217] [Counterfactual Transport Flows for Offline Conservative Trajectory Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09115)
*Lena Krieger,Xuan Zhao,Zhuo Cao,Qin Wang,Hanno Scharr,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实传输流（Counterfactual Transport Flows），一种基于源条件的轨迹精细化框架，通过从离线数据中检索具有更高反馈的邻近轨迹构建局部偏好对，实现离线决策的保守改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临的关键困难是如何在避免外推到离线数据不支持的分布区域的同时，从历史数据中改进行为策略。

Method: 构建源条件轨迹精细化框架：对低反馈候选轨迹，在潜在轨迹空间中检索邻近的高反馈轨迹形成局部偏好对，将其作为弱监督信号进行保守改进；推理时通过精细化强度参数控制改进幅度，平衡原始行为保持与性能提升。

Result: 在D4RL基准（AntMaze和MuJoCo任务）上，该方法能够从历史回报这类世界反馈中改进行为，并提供可解释的轨迹级精细化路径。

Conclusion: 反事实传输流在避免分布外推的同时有效提升了离线决策性能，且能提供可解释的轨迹级精细化路径，实现了原始行为保持与性能改进之间的可控权衡。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers a path to policy improvement from logged data alone, using historical returns or other measurable outcomes as world feedback. A key difficulty is improving observed behavior without extrapolating beyond what the offline data supports. We propose \emph{counterfactual transport flows}, a source-conditioned trajectory refinement framework for offline decision-making guided by world feedback. Given a low-feedback candidate trajectory, we construct local preference pairs from offline data by retrieving nearby trajectories in latent trajectory space with higher task-specific feedback, and use them as weak supervision for conservative refinement. The framework learns instance-specific refinement directions: at inference time, a refinement strength parameter controls how far the candidate trajectory is transported, enabling a trade-off between preserving the original behavior and applying stronger improvement. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks, including AntMaze and MuJoCo tasks, show that our method improves behavior from historical returns as world feedback, while providing interpretable trajectory-level refinement paths.

</details>


### [218] [Optimizing Energy-based Neural Network Training with Coherent Ising Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09117)
*Chen-Rui Fan,Bo Lu,Zhi-Hong Zhang,Run-Qing Zhang,Jing-Wei Wen,Chuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用相干伊辛机（CIM）结合均衡传播算法训练基于能量的神经网络，并通过集成Adam优化器提升Hopfield能量网络的收敛速度和求解精度，展示了该方法在深层架构和卷积操作中的可扩展性，为下一代AI硬件提供了新型物理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊辛机作为伊辛模型的物理求解器，在组合优化和神经网络训练中有广泛应用，但受限于硬件连接性和次优训练方法，难以扩展到大规模神经网络。

Method: 1. 利用相干伊辛机（CIM）结合均衡传播（Equilibrium Propagation）训练能量基神经网络；2. 集成Adam优化器求解Hopfield能量网络的基态。

Result: 所提方法在性能上与现有软件实现相当，显著提升了收敛速度和求解精度，并在更深层网络架构和卷积操作中展现了可扩展性。

Conclusion: CIM动力学作为训练复杂神经网络的平台具有可扩展性潜力，可通过模拟电路、光电子学或集成光子学实现能效优化，为下一代AI硬件开发提供了新的物理框架。

Abstract: While Ising machines serve as advanced physical solvers for the Ising model,enabling applications in combinatorial optimization and neural network training,their scalability for large-scale neural networks remains constrained by hardware connectivity limitations and suboptimal training methodologies. In this work,we leverage a Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) to train an energy-based neural network using Equilibrium Propagation, achieving performance comparable to existing software-based implementations. We further enhance the algorithm by integrating the Adam optimizer to solve for the ground state of a Hopfield energy network, significantly improving convergence speed and solution accuracy. Additionally, we demonstrate the scalability of our approach across deeper network architectures and convolutional operations. Our results highlight the potential of CIM dynamics as a scalable platform for training complex neural networks, offering a pathway toward energy-efficient implementations via analog circuits, optoelectronics, or integrated photonics. This work establishes a novel physical framework for next-generation AI hardware development.

</details>


### [219] [Claw-R1: A Step-Level Data Middleware System for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09138)
*Daoyu Wang,Mingyue Cheng,Qingchuan Li,Shuo Yu,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Claw-R1是一个用于智能体强化学习的步骤级数据管理中间件系统，连接异构智能体运行环境与RL训练后端，将交互轨迹作为可管理的数据资产进行组织、检查和配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习工作主要关注策略优化算法和训练框架，忽视了从数据生产到训练消费的完整数据生命周期管理。

Method: 通过Gateway Server统一捕获多轮交互步骤，Data Pool将其组织为包含提示ID、响应ID、奖励等元数据的步骤级记录，支持交互式轨迹检查、数据质量筛选和训练批次配置。

Result: 构建了一个中间件系统，使用户能够交互式检查实时轨迹、查看每一步的状态-动作-奖励、按质量和就绪程度筛选数据，并为不同下游RL算法配置训练批次。

Conclusion: 智能体交互轨迹应被视为可管理的数据资产而非临时运行时日志，数据管理在智能体强化学习中至关重要。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has become an important post-training paradigm for turning LLMs from static chatbots into interactive agents, giving rise to representative applications such as OpenClaw. Existing work mainly focuses on policy optimization algorithms and training frameworks, but pays less attention to the full data lifecycle of agent-environment interactions, from data production to training consumption. To bridge this gap, we present Claw-R1, an interactive step-level data middleware system for agentic RL. Claw-R1 connects heterogeneous agent runtimes with RL training backends through two core components: a Gateway Server and a Data Pool. The Gateway Server captures multi-turn interaction steps through a unified LLM API entry point, while the Data Pool organizes them into step-level records consisting of prompt IDs, response IDs, rewards and other metadata. In our demo, users can interactively inspect live trajectories, examine the state, action, and reward of each step, curate data by quality and readiness, and configure training-ready batches for different downstream RL algorithms. Overall, Claw-R1 treats agent interaction traces as managed data assets rather than temporary runtime logs. Through this demonstration, we hope to encourage the community to recognize the importance of data management in agentic RL. Our code is available at https://github.com/AgentR1/Claw-R1 and the demonstration video can be found at link https://youtu.be/Pw47dAOw6B0.

</details>


### [220] [Improved Convergence Analysis of Topology Dependence in Decentralized SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09154)
*Yuki Takezawa,Anastasia Koloskova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更紧的分布式SGD收敛分析，揭示了混合矩阵的所有特征值（而非仅谱隙）都会影响收敛率，并通过实验验证了该分析能更准确地描述拓扑结构对收敛行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析仅用谱隙刻画拓扑的影响，无法解释实验中拓扑在异构场景下影响显著、在同构场景下影响微弱的现象，需要更精确的收敛分析来弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 提出了一种更紧的收敛分析框架，将混合矩阵的所有特征值纳入收敛率分析，而不仅仅依赖谱隙这一单一属性。

Result: 新分析表明所有特征值都影响收敛率，且实验验证该分析比现有基于谱隙的分析更能准确描述拓扑对收敛率的影响。

Conclusion: 本文的收敛分析更精确地解释了拓扑结构对分布式SGD收敛行为的影响，为理解网络拓扑在分布式学习中的作用提供了更深层次的理论依据。

Abstract: Decentralized SGD is a fundamental algorithm in decentralized learning, although the influence of an underlying network topology on its convergence behavior is not yet fully understood. Existing convergence analyses have shown that topologies with a small spectral gap significantly deteriorate the convergence rate of Decentralized SGD in both homogeneous and heterogeneous cases. However, many prior papers have reported that indeed the choice of the topology has a significant experimental impact in the heterogeneous case, but has little experimental impact on training behavior in the homogeneous case. In this paper, we present a tighter convergence analysis of Decentralized SGD, offering a more precise understanding of how topologies affect the convergence rate than the prior analysis. Specifically, unlike existing convergence analyses that used only the spectral gap as a property of the topology, our novel analysis shows that all eigenvalues of the mixing matrix affect the convergence rate. Throughout the experiments, we carefully evaluated the convergence behavior of Decentralized SGD and demonstrated that our novel convergence analysis can more accurately describe the effect of topology on the convergence rate.

</details>


### [221] [Crop Recommendation and Agricultural Query Answering System Using Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks and Hybrid Retrieval Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09160)
*Prajwal Thapa,Yagya Raj Pandeya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个集成天气预报、作物推荐和问答功能的精准农业统一系统，使用STGCN和Transformer-GNN进行30天天气预报，结合土壤属性生成作物推荐，并通过RAG聊天机器人提供农业知识问答，最终部署为移动应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为尼泊尔等资源有限地区的农民提供个性化农业指导，解决农村地区精准农业指导匮乏的问题，帮助农民应对气候变异性，提升作物产量和决策能力。

Method: 使用STGCN和Transformer-GNN两种深度学习模型对1359个地点进行30天天气预报；将预测结果与静态土壤属性（pH、湿度、有机质）通过评分算法匹配作物最优生长条件生成推荐；构建基于检索增强生成（RAG）的农业领域问答聊天机器人；整体系统通过移动应用部署。

Result: STGCN在天气预测上优于Transformer模型（MSE约0.011 vs 0.013）；用户反馈证实系统在农村环境中的可用性和相关性。

Conclusion: 结合机器学习模型与本地农业数据能够为农民提供可操作的建议，促进更明智的决策、提高作物产量并增强对气候变异性的适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified system designed to support precision agriculture by integrating advanced weather prediction, crop recommendation, and a question-answering tool for farmers. We propose two deep learning models -- a Transformer-based Graph Neural Network and a Spatio-Temporal Graph Convolutional Network (STGCN) -- to forecast weather conditions for the next 30 days using data from 1,359 locations in Nepal. The STGCN outperforms the Transformer-based model in accuracy (MSE ~0.011 vs. 0.013), effectively modeling both spatial and temporal dependencies in climate data. These predictions are combined with static soil properties such as pH, moisture, and organic content to generate localized crop recommendations through a scoring algorithm that matches each crop's optimal growing conditions. Additionally, we develop a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) chatbot that leverages domain-specific agricultural documents to answer farmers' questions in natural language. The entire system is deployed via a mobile application, offering real-time suggestions and conversational support. User feedback confirms the system's usability and relevance, especially in rural settings where personalized farming guidance is limited. Overall, our approach demonstrates how combining machine learning models with local agricultural data can empower farmers with actionable insights, promoting more informed decisions, better crop yields, and increased resilience to climate variability.

</details>


### [222] [CANS: Accelerating Multiuser Collaborative Edge Inference via Cooperative Autodidactic NeuroSurgeon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09175)
*Zheshun Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Changyao Lin,Zenglin Xu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CANS框架，通过FedLinUCB-DW算法让多设备在MEC协作推理中自适应学习最优DNN分区，在原型系统上延迟降低高达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户协作边缘推理中，由于无线链路波动和设备能力异构，难以确定每个设备的最优DNN分区。

Method: 提出CANS框架，集成FedLinUCB-DW算法，通过设备分组（同类设备聚类）和利用本地离线早期退出推理经验进行暖启动在线探索，实现自适应DNN分区学习。

Result: 在模拟环境和硬件原型上验证，CANS相比非协作基线将平均推理延迟降低高达50%。

Conclusion: CANS通过设备间共享反馈信息实现自适应协作边缘推理，在异构环境下显著降低推理延迟，且算法具有理论遗憾上界保证。

Abstract: Recently, mobile edge computing (MEC)-enabled collaborative deep neural network (DNN) inference has emerged as a promising approach for delivering intelligent services to resource-constrained mobile devices. A representative scenario is multi-user collaborative edge inference, where distinct devices independently partition their DNN models and offload backend computation to a common edge server over wireless networks. However, determining the optimal DNN partition for each device is challenging due to unknown and time-varying system conditions, including fluctuating wireless links and diverse device capabilities. To address this problem, we propose Cooperative Autodidactic NeuroSurgeon (CANS), a collaborative edge inference framework that enables devices to adaptively learn optimal DNN partitions by sharing informative feedback during online inference. To handle the challenge of device heterogeneity and better leverage offline inference experience, we integrate a novel FedLinUCB-DW algorithm that groups devices of the same type and warm-starts online exploration using local offline early-exit inference experience. Furthermore, we provide theoretical guarantees for FedLinUCB-DW by deriving the regret upper bound. We also validate our method on both a simulated environment and a hardware prototype system. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that CANS achieves lower inference latency compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Especially, in prototype experiments on two edge devices, the proposed CANS reduced average inference latency by up to 50% compared to the non-cooperative baseline.

</details>


### [223] [Asymptotic Optimality of Thompson Sampling for Risk-Averse Bandits with Sub-Gaussian Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09191)
*Joel Q. L. Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了ρ-NPTS_SG算法（一种无锚点非参数汤普森采样算法）在风险厌恶型多臂老虎机问题上达到了与实例相关下界匹配的遗憾界（对数阶最优），对任何连续风险泛函ρ（如CVaR、均值-方差、夏普比率、扭曲风险度量等）均为渐近最优，且仅需ρ的连续性条件，弱于先前参数化汤普森采样所需的支配性条件及UCB类算法所需的Lipschitz条件，首次为非Lipschitz泛函（如夏普比率）在非参数奖励假设下提供了实例最优保证。关键技术贡献包括有界支撑的离散化引理和次高斯尾的截断离散化引理，通过Dirichlet聚合性质将增长字母表的Dirichlet后验投影到固定网格上，打破了阻碍先前证明的超指数屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险厌恶型多臂老虎机问题中，现有汤普森采样算法要么需要参数化奖励假设和支配性条件，要么需要Lipschitz连续性，无法处理非Lipschitz风险泛函（如夏普比率）在非参数设置下的实例最优性。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立首个对广泛连续风险泛函适用的渐近最优非参数汤普森采样算法。

Method: 提出无锚点非参数汤普森采样算法ρ-NPTS_SG，利用离散化引理（有界支撑）和截断离散化引理（次高斯尾），通过Dirichlet聚合性质将增长字母表的Dirichlet后验投影到固定网格上，处理多项式因子使次数独立于样本大小，从而突破超指数屏障。

Result: 算法遗憾界与实例相关下界在对数阶上匹配，实现渐近最优；仅需风险泛函ρ的连续性，该条件严格弱于参数化汤普森采样的支配性条件和UCB算法的Lipschitz条件；首次为非Lipschitz泛函（如夏普比率）在非参数奖励假设下提供实例最优保证。

Conclusion: ρ-NPTS_SG是首个在连续风险泛函下达到实例最优的非参数汤普森采样算法，其所需条件更弱、适用范围更广，理论贡献在于通过Dirichlet聚合的离散化技术突破了超指数屏障，为风险厌恶型决策提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that $ρ\text{-}\mathrm{NPTS}_{\mathrm{SG}}$, an anchor-free nonparametric Thompson Sampling algorithm for risk-averse bandits, achieves regret matching the instance-dependent lower bound to leading order in $\log n$, establishing it as asymptotically optimal for any continuous risk functional $ρ$ (CVaR, mean-variance, Sharpe ratio, distortion risk measures, and more) on the class of distributions with bounded density and sub-Gaussian tails, including Gaussian arms. Both this result and its bounded-support counterpart require only continuity of $ρ$: strictly weaker than the dominance condition of prior parametric Thompson Sampling results, and strictly weaker than the Lipschitz condition of UCB-type algorithms, yielding the first instance-optimal guarantees for non-Lipschitz functionals such as the Sharpe ratio without parametric reward assumptions. The bounded-support case is developed first as a stepping stone sharing the same proof structure. The key technical contributions are a discretisation lemma (bounded support) and a truncated discretisation lemma (sub-Gaussian tails), each projecting the growing-alphabet Dirichlet posterior onto a fixed grid via the Dirichlet aggregation property, holding all polynomial prefactors at fixed degree independent of sample size and breaking the super-exponential barrier that blocked prior proofs.

</details>


### [224] [The Injection Paradox: Brand-Level Suppression in Safety-Trained LLM Recommendations via RAG Context Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09204)
*Hyunseok Paeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在基于RAG的LLM推荐系统中，安全训练会产生"注入悖论"：检索文档中的提示注入反而会抑制目标品牌的推荐率，甚至波及同品牌未修改文档。Claude Opus 4.6中目标品牌从54%基线降至零推荐，而GPT模型则相反地增加了推荐，表明不同模型家族存在差异，并可能被对手利用来压制竞品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示RAG-based LLM推荐系统中安全训练的一个可复现的失败模式——注入悖论，即安全训练导致提示注入反向抑制目标品牌推荐，并探讨这种机制被对手逆向利用的技术可能性。

Method: 在Claude和GPT模型上进行RAG推荐实验，在品牌文档中嵌入提示注入，比较注入前后目标品牌的推荐率变化，并通过反事实实验和跨品牌验证来检验方向性模式。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6中目标品牌从54%基线降至零top-2推荐；同品牌未注入文档也受到抑制；GPT模型则相反，注入反而增加推荐；不同模型家族表现出差异化行为。

Conclusion: 安全训练在RAG推荐中会产生注入悖论，导致提示注入反向抑制被注入品牌；Claude和GPT模型表现出相反方向，说明存在模型家族差异；这为对手通过注入攻击压制竞品品牌提供了技术可能性。

Abstract: We present a reproducible failure mode of safety training in RAG-based LLM recommendation -- the Injection Paradox -- in which prompt injections embedded in retrieved documents backfire against the attacker, suppressing the target brand below the injection-free baseline. In safety-trained Claude models, documents containing prompt injections suffer a sharp drop in recommendation rate, and this suppression propagates beyond the injected document to unmodified documents of the same brand. In Claude Opus 4.6, the target brand drops from a 54% baseline to zero top-2 recommendations across all 50 trials, even though only 1 of 4 brand documents in the corpus contains an injection. The directional pattern is reproduced in counterfactual experiments and across three brands. A contrasting result across the GPT models tested, where the same injection instead increases recommendations, suggests model-family differences in how injection-like context affects recommendation behavior. These findings raise the technical possibility of a reverse-attack scenario in which an adversary embeds injections in a competitor's documents to suppress the competitor's brand via safety-sensitive model behavior.

</details>


### [225] [Orange Lab: Lowering Barriers to Data Mining through Embedded Interactive Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09239)
*Matej Bevec,Aleš Erjavec,Vesna Tanko,Lena Trnovec,Lan Žagar,Ana Farič,Janez Demšar,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Orange Lab是一个基于Web的协作式可视化数据分析环境，其核心贡献是"组件暴露"范式，允许作者将工作流组件嵌入任意网页上下文，创建同步交互界面，从而降低数据科学准入壁垒，支持数据素养教育中的机器学习概念探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化编程系统局限于独立应用，缺乏将可视化分析方案过渡到交互式Web环境的能力，导致数据分析流程难以共享、嵌入和适配到面向用户的分析工具中。

Method: 提出"组件暴露"范式，允许用户将工作流中选定的组件或其界面部分嵌入任意Web上下文，创建同步交互界面，同时隐藏底层工作流复杂性。工作流组件间的交互会无缝传播，将静态流水线转变为动态响应式系统。

Result: 在数据素养教育场景中部署，嵌入式组件能引导学生进行机器学习概念的动手探索，无需了解底层系统知识。

Conclusion: Orange Lab有效降低了数据科学的准入门槛，支持数据科学的民主化。

Abstract: While visual programming of data analysis workflows has become an important vehicle for the democratization of data science, such systems remain largely confined to standalone applications and offer limited support for transitioning their visual analytics solutions into interactive web environments. As a result, data analysis pipelines are difficult to share, embed, and adapt into user-facing analytical tools. We present Orange Lab, a web-based collaborative environment for visual data analytics. At its core, Orange Lab enables users to visually construct machine learning workflows from modular components, where interactions in any component propagate seamlessly through the workflow, turning static pipelines into dynamic, reactive systems that support exploration and data-driven storytelling. Our key contribution is component exposition, a paradigm that allows authors to embed selected workflow components, or parts of their interfaces, into arbitrary web contexts, creating synchronized, interactive interfaces while hiding underlying workflow complexity. This enables the development of tailored analytical views and narrative-driven experiences that integrate data analysis directly into online materials. We demonstrate the approach through deployments in data literacy education, where embedded components guide students in hands-on exploration of machine learning concepts without requiring knowledge of the underlying system, showing that Orange Lab effectively lowers barriers to entry and supports the democratization of data science.

</details>


### [226] [BSTabDiff: Block-Subunit Diffusion Priors for High-Dimensional Tabular Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09257)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Md Younus Ahamed,Prashnna Gyawali,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高维小样本（HDLSS）表格数据（n<<m），提出BSTabDiff分块-子单元生成框架：将m维特征划分为M个潜在块（M<<m），每个块由共享的低维子单元变量生成，在紧凑的块潜在空间学习全局依赖，通过copula驱动依赖、灵活边缘分布和显式缺失机制解码到全特征空间，支持扩散/流等现代深度先验，在HDLSS场景下优于非结构化表格生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDLSS表格数据（如组学数据）存在高维小样本、局部相关组、稀疏跨组依赖、重尾非高斯边缘分布、异方差噪声和结构化缺失等问题，直接在高维空间进行密度学习因n<<m而病态，需要专门针对这些特性的生成框架。

Method: 提出BSTabDiff分块-子单元生成框架：(1) 将m个观测特征划分为M个潜在块（M<<m）；(2) 每个块通过共享的低维子单元变量生成；(3) 在紧凑的块潜在空间R^M中集中学习全局依赖；(4) 解码时采用copula驱动的依赖结构、灵活的逐特征边缘分布和显式缺失机制；(5) 支持扩散模型和归一化流等现代深度先验作为块潜在变量上的先验。

Result: 在HDLSS数据上，BSTabDiff相比非结构化表格生成器能够产生更真实、更稳定的高维合成数据。

Conclusion: BSTabDiff有效解决了HDLSS表格数据密度学习的病态问题，通过分块-子单元结构和紧凑潜在空间实现了稳定的高维数据合成和可控基准生成。

Abstract: High-Dimensional Low-Sample Size (HDLSS) tabular domains (e.g., omics) are characterized by $n \ll m$, where $n$ = number of samples, and $m$ = number of features. Such domains often exhibit strong local correlation groups, sparse cross-group dependencies, heavy-tailed non-Gaussian marginals, heteroscedastic noise, and structured missingness, making direct density learning in $\mathbb{R}^m$ ill-conditioned since $n \ll m$. We propose BSTabDiff, a block-subunit generative framework that partitions the $m$ observed features into $M$ latent blocks ($M \ll m$) and generates each block via a shared low-dimensional subunit variable, concentrating global dependence learning in the compact block-latent space $\mathbb{R}^M$ while decoding to the full feature space with copula-driven dependence, flexible per-feature marginals, and explicit missingness mechanisms. BSTabDiff supports modern deep priors on block latents, including diffusion and normalizing flows, enabling stable synthesis and controllable benchmark generation in the HDLSS regime. Empirically, BSTabDiff produces more realistic and stable high-dimensional synthetic data when compared with unstructured tabular generators on HDLSS data.

</details>


### [227] [ERBench: A Benchmark and Testsuite for Equation Discovery Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09276)
*Paul Kahlmeyer,Henrik Voigt,Michael Habeck,Joachim Giesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ERBench，一个专门用于评估符号回归算法在方程发现任务中方程恢复能力的基准框架，重点关注算法在不同维度、采样大小、采样分布和采样域下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归基准仅评估域内预测精度，且方程恢复任务中真实公式数量少、公开性不足，缺乏对算法在不同维度、采样大小、分布和域下鲁棒性的评估，而这对于从业者发现自然现象模型至关重要。

Method: 提出Equation Recovery Benchmark (ERBench)，一个专门评估符号回归算法方程发现能力的基准框架，强调方程恢复（而非域内预测精度）作为评价指标，并系统测试算法在不同维度、采样大小、采样分布和采样域下的鲁棒性。

Result: ERBench能够更严格地评估符号回归算法在方程发现任务中的真实表现，弥补了现有基准仅关注域内插值精度而忽视方程恢复能力和鲁棒性的不足。

Conclusion: 方程恢复是评估符号回归算法进行方程发现能力的更可靠指标；ERBench通过系统测试算法在各种数据条件下的鲁棒性，为从业者选择适合真实世界方程发现的算法提供了更有意义的评估工具。

Abstract: Equation discovery aims to automate the discovery of scientific models in the form of mathematical equations from data. Technically, equation discovery is implemented by symbolic regression algorithms. Performance of symbolic regression for equation discovery is measured along two dimensions: Prediction accuracy on test data, and recovery of known groundtruth formulas. For standard regression, accuracy is typically measured on in-domain test data, for instance, by splitting a data set randomly into training and test data. While this makes sense for in-domain interpolation, which is the common goal in ordinary regression, it can be a misleading proxy for true model discovery and generalization. The obvious alternative is to measure out-of-domain accuracy. However, obtaining challenging out-of-domain test data is a non-trivial problem. Therefore, we focus on equation recovery for evaluating symbolic regression algorithms for equation discovery. The rationale is that symbolic regression algorithms that perform well in recovering known groundtruth formulas are good candidates to perform well in unknown equation discovery. Existing benchmarks for symbolic regression include equation recovery tasks, however, with only a small number of groundtruth formulas that are publicly known. Moreover, these benchmarks place less emphasis on evaluating the robustness of algorithms in terms of their behavior under changing dimensionality, sampling size, sampling distribution and sampling domain. This, however, is of central importance to practitioners wanting to discover equations for modeling natural phenomena, since data is almost certainly noisy and comes from diverse domains, distributions, and sample sizes. To fill this gap, we introduce the Equation Recovery Benchmark (ERBench), a new evaluation framework designed to rigorously assess algorithms explicitly targeting the task of equation discovery.

</details>


### [228] [Internalizing Geometric Law: Learning from Solver Residuals for Precision-Critical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09278)
*Rafael Cabral,Pang Zixi,Ziyi Shou,Shen Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PyGeoX（可编程几何DSL）和PyGeoX-Bench（300个分层几何问题基准），发现了一种称为"离群梯度掩盖"的失败模式——全局范数奖励下单个离群约束会湮灭其他所有约束的学习信号，并提出了饱和加性奖励（SAR）方法，将奖励分解为有界逐项约束项，在严重违规下仍保持梯度一致性，相比MSE基线在困难问题上求解率提升2.3倍，8B模型即可匹敌更大规模系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在技术制图、机械设计等精度关键领域中频繁产生幻觉，输出必须满足严格的几何约束，现有方法难以处理自由形式自然语言到精确几何构造的开放端合成问题，其中实体需同时满足数十个相互作用的约束。

Method: 1) 发布PyGeoX，一个可编程几何领域特定语言（DSL），将声明式约束编译为可微损失函数；2) 构建PyGeoX-Bench，包含300个分层问题，每个约束均可验证奖励；3) 发现离群梯度掩盖（Outlier Gradient Masking）失败模式；4) 提出饱和加性奖励（SAR），将奖励分解为有界逐项约束项。

Result: SAR相比基于MSE的奖励在困难层级求解率提升2.3倍，8B参数模型与更大规模前沿系统在该基准上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 分解式奖励设计（如SAR）优于全局聚合式奖励（如MSE），能够有效解决多约束几何推理中的梯度掩盖问题，显著提升LLM在精度关键领域的几何合成能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models frequently hallucinate in precision-critical domains such as technical diagramming and mechanical design, where outputs must satisfy strict geometric constraints. We study open-ended geometric synthesis from natural language: translating free-form descriptions into precise constructions whose entities must simultaneously satisfy dozens of interacting constraints. To make this tractable, we release PyGeoX, a programmable geometric DSL that compiles declarative constraints into a differentiable loss, and PyGeoX-Bench, a stratified suite of 300 problems with per-constraint verifiable rewards. Using PyGeoX as a verifier, we identify a failure mode we call Outlier Gradient Masking: under global-norm rewards (any scheme that aggregates residuals through a single norm, for example, $\exp(-\mathrm{MSE})$), a single outlier constraint can nullify the learning signal across all others. To address this, we propose Saturating Additive Rewards (SAR), which decompose the reward into bounded per-constraint terms, preserving partial progress and ensuring consistent gradients even under severe violations. Against MSE-based rewards, the natural baseline for geometry solvers, SAR improves the hard-tier solving rate by $2.3\times$, and the resulting 8B model is competitive with much larger frontier systems on this benchmark. We release the engine, benchmark, and data at https://github.com/Huawei-AI4Math/PyGeoX.

</details>


### [229] [Trajectory Geometry of Transformer Representations Across Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09287)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Transformer前向传播视为高维表示流形上的离散群体轨迹，利用计算神经科学的几何工具，通过五种指标（轨迹长度、曲率、语义收敛指数、层间余弦相似度、表示稳定性）分析三层模型家族（GPT-2、TinyLlama、Qwen2.5）在五类提示下的表示演化。发现：语义相关提示在中后期层显著收敛；推理任务曲率大于词汇变化；歧义标记出现轨迹分叉；层间余弦相似度揭示编码-细化-输出准备的三阶段结构。所有效应在对照实验中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究多关注模型"编码了什么"，而忽略了表示在层间如何演化这一开放问题。作者希望通过几何工具提供一种无探针（probe-free）的机制理解视角。

Method: 将Transformer前向传播建模为高维表示流形上的离散群体轨迹，直接在原始空间计算五种几何指标（轨迹长度、曲率、语义收敛指数、层间余弦相似度、表示稳定性），无需预定义特征或训练探针。在GPT-2、TinyLlama、Qwen2.5三个模型和五类提示上进行实验，并设置打乱层序和随机嵌入的对照组。

Result: 1. 语义相关提示在中后期层显著收敛（峰值CI 0.41-0.58, p<0.001），符合吸引子动力学；2. 推理任务轨迹曲率（0.71-0.83 rad）显著大于词汇变化（0.27-0.31 rad），表明曲率编码计算复杂度；3. 歧义标记在最终层出现最高5.6x的表示分离，而清晰标记无此现象；4. 层间余弦相似度揭示所有架构共有的三阶段结构：编码、细化、输出准备。对照实验中所有效应消失。

Conclusion: 轨迹几何构成了一个原则性的、无探针的机械可解释性分析视角，并且在不同架构间具有普遍性。作者开源了完整的模型无关分析流程。

Abstract: Understanding how transformer representations evolve across layers, not merely what they encode, remains an open problem in mechanistic interpretability. We recast the transformer forward pass as a discrete population trajectory through a high-dimensional representation manifold, drawing on geometric tools from computational neuroscience. Rather than probing for pre-specified features, we characterize trajectory geometry using five metrics computed directly in the ambient space: trajectory length, curvature, a semantic convergence index, layerwise cosine similarity, and representational stability. Across three model families (GPT-2, TinyLlama, Qwen2.5) and five controlled prompt families, we report four findings. First, semantically related prompts converge significantly in middle-to-late layers (peak CI 0.41--0.58, p<0.001, Mann-Whitney U), consistent with attractor-like dynamics. Second, reasoning tasks produce trajectories of greater curvature than lexical variations (0.71--0.83 rad vs. 0.27--0.31 rad), suggesting curvature encodes computational complexity. Third, ambiguous tokens exhibit trajectory bifurcation with up to 5.6x representational separation by the final layer, absent in unambiguous controls. Fourth, layerwise cosine similarity reveals a universal three-phase structure: encoding, elaboration, and output preparation, consistent across all three architectures. All four effects vanish under shuffled-layer and random-embedding controls. We release a fully open-source, model-agnostic pipeline and argue that trajectory geometry constitutes a principled, probe-free lens for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [230] [Intention Driven Identification of In-Possession Match Phases in Association Football through Temporal Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09289)
*Yuesen Li,Daniel Link*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空追踪数据的有球阶段识别框架，利用时序图注意力网络（T-GAN）将连续比赛数据转化为可解释的战术阶段（如推进、控球、反击等），在德甲数据集上取得了良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球有球阶段难以直接从空间模式中观察，需要识别蕴含战术意图的相态，现有方法缺乏对时序关系和球员交互的联合建模。

Method: 构建了三级战术意图和六种阶段的分层模型，开发T-GAN模型融合帧级球员交互图（GAT）、上下文特征和Transformer时序建模，并提出序列感知评估指标IoT-D。

Result: 意图级宏平均F1为0.87，入侵阶段F1为0.76，得分阶段F1为0.79；后处理使序列级IoT-D F1从0.68/0.61提升至0.79/0.71。序列建模是主要驱动因素，图模型对反击识别提升显著。

Conclusion: 该框架能将连续追踪数据转化为战术可解释的有球阶段表示，可应用于自动比赛标注、战术分析和打法风格画像。

Abstract: Understanding tactical organisation of association football, hereafter referred to as football, requires identifying distinct match phases. Yet in-possession phases are rarely directly observable and are shaped by evolving tactical intentions, rather than spatial patterns alone. This study proposes a data-driven framework for identifying in-possession match phases from spatiotemporal tracking data. Seven German Bundesliga matches recorded at 25 Hz with TRACAB were analysed. A hierarchical phase model was defined with three tactical intentions (Invade Opponent Space, Keep Possession, Scoring) and six phases (Build Up, Progression, Counter Attack, Maintenance, Sustained Threat, Finishing). A Temporal Graph Attention Network (T-GAN) was developed to combine frame-level player-interaction graphs, contextual features, and Transformer-based temporal modelling. Performance was evaluated using frame-level F1 and a sequence-aware Intersection over Truth-Dominance (IoT-D) metric. T-GAN achieved macro-average frame-level F1 scores of 0.87 at the intention level, 0.76 for invasion-related phases, and 0.79 for scoring phases. At the sequence level, mean diagonal IoT-D F1 increased from 0.68 to 0.79 for intentions and from 0.61 to 0.71 for phases after post-processing, indicating improved temporal coherence. Model comparisons showed that sequence modelling was the main driver of segmentation quality, while graph-based relational modelling was particularly beneficial for Counter Attack recognition. Exploratory player attention analysis further suggested that wide and midfield positional groups contributed strongly to phase discrimination. Overall, the framework translates continuous tracking data into tactically interpretable in-possession phase representations, with potential applications in automated match annotation, tactical analysis, and playing-style profiling.

</details>


### [231] [PRISM: Topology-Aware Cross-Modal Imputation for Modality-Deficient Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09301)
*Zekai Chen,Miao Zhang,Jiayang Xing,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRISM框架，通过拓扑感知的联邦跨模态补全方法，解决多模态联邦图学习中客户端模态缺失问题（如仅含图像或仅含文本的客户端），在六个数据集上平均提升4.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图学习假设客户端共享公共模态基础，但实际场景中客户端可能存在模态缺失（如视觉搜索客户端只有图像交互图而无文本描述），且缺失模态在消息传递中会被拓扑结构放大误差。

Method: PRISM通过主动检索从联邦中获取缺失模态语义，再以拓扑感知的元提示方式将其注入本地图传播过程，而非仅从本地观测重构缺失模态。

Result: 在六个多模态图数据集上，PRISM在面向图和面向模态的任务中均持续改善模态缺失客户端性能，平均超越最先进基线4.48%。

Conclusion: PRISM有效应对客户端级模态缺失问题，拓扑感知的跨模态补全优于仅依赖本地数据的重构方法。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning (MM-FGL) aims to collaboratively learn from decentralized graphs with text and images. However, real-world clients may not share a common modality basis: a visual-search client may contain image--interaction graphs but no seller descriptions, while a catalog client may provide text but no product images. We refer to this practical setting as client-level modality deficiency. Unlike random instance-wise missingness, a deficient client lacks the local semantic basis needed to reconstruct the absent modality. More importantly, in graph learning, incomplete representations initialize message passing, so imputation errors can be filtered, mixed, and amplified by the receiving topology. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{PRISM} (\textbf{P}roactive \textbf{R}etrieval and \textbf{I}mputation via \textbf{S}tructural \textbf{M}eta-prompting), a topology-aware federated cross-modal imputation framework. Rather than reconstructing the missing modality solely from local observations, PRISM recovers missing-modality semantics from the federation and introduces them into local graph propagation under topology-aware control. Experiments on six multimodal graph datasets across graph-centric and modality-centric tasks show that PRISM consistently improves modality-deficient clients, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by \textbf{4.48}\% on average.

</details>


### [232] [Toward Compiler World Models: Learning Latent Dynamics for Efficient Tensor Program Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09312)
*Haolin Pan,Lianghong Huang,Xvlin Zhou,Mingjie Xing,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受世界模型启发的张量程序优化评估器，通过动作条件化的潜在动态模型对调度轨迹进行建模，在TVM AutoScheduler上实现了显著加速，同等预算下性能超越Ansor，且用1/10的测量次数达到Ansor-10K的99.8%效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调度器使用学习成本模型评估每个候选程序，但仅将其视为静态代码快照，忽略了生成该程序的调度轨迹，导致对动作依赖关系不敏感且易受表面代码变化影响。

Method: 提出世界模型启发的评估器，将调度评估建模为动作条件化的潜在动态过程。从初始程序出发，使用轻量级过渡模型在连续潜在空间中滚动调度动作，避免昂贵的AST变异和重复代码编码，最终结合动态表示、动作和硬件特征进行候选排序。

Result: 在TVM AutoScheduler上实现，64次试验预算下GPU代表性子图延迟比Ansor提升1.37倍，CPU提升1.54倍；使用1/10的测量次数在几何平均上达到Ansor-10K的2.2%以内；全模型推理比PyTorch/PyTorch-opt(cuDNN)加速4.61倍/3.67倍。

Conclusion: 通过对调度轨迹的显式建模，该方法在显著降低测量成本的同时提升了搜索质量，验证了世界模型范式在张量程序优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Tensor program optimization is essential for modern machine learning systems, but its search space is enormous. Existing auto-schedulers reduce measurement cost with learned cost models, yet they usually evaluate each candidate as a static code snapshot, ignoring the schedule trajectory that produced it. This makes them insensitive to action dependencies and vulnerable to superficial code variations. We propose a \emph{world-model-inspired} evaluator that models schedule evaluation as action-conditioned latent dynamics over program states. Starting from the initial program, it rolls out scheduling actions in a continuous latent space with a lightweight transition model, avoiding expensive AST mutation and repeated code encoding. The final dynamic representation is combined with action and hardware features to rank candidates. Implemented in TVM AutoScheduler, our method improves representative-subgraph latency over Ansor by 1.37$\times$ on GPU and 1.54$\times$ on CPU under the same 64-trial budget. It also matches Ansor-10K within 2.2% geometric mean using 10$\times$ fewer measurements, and accelerates full-model inference over PyTorch/PyTorch-opt(cuDNN) by 4.61$\times$/3.67$\times$ geometric mean.

</details>


### [233] [Machine-Learning Emulation of Satellite Greenhouse Gas Retrievals: Stability over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09313)
*Nugzar Gognadze,Motonobu Kanagawa,Yu Someya,Hisashi Yashiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用机器学习模型（特别是Lasso和神经网络）快速模拟温室气体（CO2和CH4）的卫星反演算法，发现模型随时间推移预测精度下降，而将时间作为输入特征可显著改善XCH4预测，且简单Lasso模型在稳定性和准确性上优于复杂模型，在TCCON验证中达到与GOSAT卫星观测相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型作为反演算法快速模拟器时，仅用同期数据评估，缺乏对模型随时间稳定性的研究，因此需要评估模型在时间迁移场景下的预测性能。

Method: 使用GOSAT卫星数据，对比Lasso、神经网络等模型在时间迁移场景下的预测稳定性；引入时间作为输入特征；使用TCCON地面观测网络进行外部验证。

Result: 预测精度随测试期远离训练期而下降；时间特征显著提升XCH4预测性能；Lasso模型在稳定性和准确性上优于神经网络；时间增强Lasso在TCCON验证中误差与GOSAT与TCCON之间的系统偏差相当。

Conclusion: 简单Lasso模型结合时间特征可作为GHG反演的高效且稳定的模拟器，在时间泛化能力上优于复杂模型；未来应重视模型的时间稳定性评估。

Abstract: Retrieval algorithms are used to estimate atmospheric concentrations of greenhouse gases (GHGs), such as carbon dioxide (CO2) and methane (CH4), by solving inverse problems from high-spectral-resolution satellite radiance measurements. However, these algorithms are computationally expensive, which makes real-time estimation at scale difficult. Machine-learning models have therefore been proposed as fast emulators of retrieval algorithms. Most existing studies, however, evaluate them only on test data from the same period as the training data.
  We study the stability over time of such emulators using data from the Greenhouse Gases Observing SATellite (GOSAT). We show that prediction accuracy generally deteriorates when the test period moves away from the training period. We also show that including time as an input feature substantially improves XCH4 prediction for Lasso and neural-network models. Among the methods considered, a simple Lasso model performs as well as or better than more complex methods such as neural networks, and yields more stable predictions over time. We further validate the results using the Total Carbon Column Observing Network (TCCON), a ground-based observation network. On the TCCON-matched dataset, the time-augmented Lasso achieves errors against TCCON that are comparable to the disagreement between GOSAT and TCCON for both XCO2 and XCH4.

</details>


### [234] [A Universal Dense Football Event Representation Based on TabTransformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09327)
*Weiran Yang,Daniel Memmert,Maximilian Klemp-Weins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的足球事件表征学习模型，通过将分类特征编码为稠密嵌入向量来捕获动作语义，在动作价值估计和比赛风格识别等下游任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要使用独热编码或序数嵌入处理足球事件数据中的分类特征，忽视了动作描述符的内在语义信息，限制了表征质量。

Method: 基于Transformer架构，将分类事件特征编码为可学习的嵌入向量，通过自注意力机制捕获分类特征间的潜在依赖关系，在预训练阶段学习运动特定的动作语义，生成稠密的事件表征。

Result: 在下游预测任务中，嵌入表征的Brier分数优于任务特定的基线方法，表现出更优的概率校准性能。

Conclusion: Transformer模型能够有效学习足球事件分类特征的语义嵌入，为动作价值估计和风格识别等下游任务提供高质量表征。

Abstract: Football event data constitute a rich spatiotemporal source for quantitative analysis of player actions in team sports. These datasets contain heterogeneous features, combining continuous location coordinates with categorical variables such as action type, action outcome, and body part. Such data have been applied in sports analytics for match outcome forecasting, player evaluation, and tactical pattern recognition. However, existing approaches predominantly encode categorical features using one-hot or ordinal embedding representations, overlooking the intrinsic semantics of action descriptors. The Transformer is a deep neural network architecture based on self-attention that captures dependencies between input features at arbitrary positions. We propose and implement a Transformer-based model to learn latent dependencies among categorical event features and produce dense representations of football events. By encoding categorical features as learned embedding vectors, sport-specific action semantics are captured during pretraining, enabling the representations to support downstream tasks such as action value estimation and play style recognition. Empirical evaluation shows that the embedding representations yield superior probability calibration over task-specific baselines on the downstream prediction tasks, as measured by Brier score.

</details>


### [235] [Thresholded Local Hyper-Flow Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09340)
*Meher Chaitanya,Sebastian Dalleiger,Luana Ruiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TL-HFD，一种保持局部活跃区域的第一性方法，用于子模超图上的种子聚类，通过阈值化边界激活实现局部更新，并证明其与全局更新等价，在理论和实验上均优于现有HFD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部超流扩散（HFD）求解器无法在每次迭代中保持中间计算的局部性，导致计算效率低下，尤其在有噪声的实例中扩散会吸收非目标顶点。

Method: 提出TL-HFD：维护种子周围的活跃区域，在该区域及其直接边界上执行投影次梯度更新，并通过阈值化（top-k）边界激活扩展区域。将阈值化更新视为带显式跳过边界误差的非精确投影次梯度步，推导了精确和阈值化更新的有限时间对偶次优性界，以及由局部次梯度范数和最小边界推动控制的加性激活体积界。

Result: 证明局部更新与全局更新精确等价；建立了有限时间对偶次优性界；推导了激活体积控制界；将近似对偶最优性与局部化支持转化为早期停止迭代的鲁棒扫描切割保证。实验表明TL-HFD在激活更小体积的同时匹配或优于HFD，在噪声实例上增益最大。

Conclusion: TL-HFD通过保持计算的局部性，在不牺牲收敛保证的前提下实现了更高效、更鲁棒的种子聚类，特别适用于存在噪声的超图场景。

Abstract: Local Hyper-Flow Diffusion (HFD) gives an edge-size-independent Cheeger-type guarantee for seeded clustering in general submodular hypergraphs, but existing HFD solvers do not keep intermediate computation local at every iteration. We introduce Thresholded Local HFD (TL-HFD), a first-order method that maintains an active region around the seeds, performs projected subgradient updates on that region and its immediate boundary, and expands via thresholded (top-k) boundary activation. We prove that the local update is exact: the degree-preconditioned projected subgradient step restricted to the active region and its boundary coincides with the unrestricted global update. We establish finite-time dual suboptimality for both exact and thresholded updates, treating the latter as inexact projected subgradient steps with explicit skipped-boundary error. We further derive an additive activated-volume bound controlled by realized local subgradient norms and the minimum boundary-push among newly activated vertices, and translate approximate dual optimality with localized support into a robust sweep-cut guarantee for early-stopped iterates. For general submodular cut-costs, each iteration is local in the scanned region and oracle-sensitive in the hyperedge primitive. Empirically, TL-HFD often matches or improves over HFD while activating less volume, with the largest gains on noisy instances where diffusion tends to absorb non-target vertices.

</details>


### [236] [PBSD: Privileged Bayesian Self-Distillation for Long-Horizon Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09348)
*Yang Tian,Rui Wang,Xumeng Wen,Junjie Li,Shizhao Sun,Lei Song,Jiang Bian,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBSD是一种基于贝叶斯校准的自蒸馏方法，通过将稀疏的最终奖励转化为细粒度的逐轮信用分配信号，解决了长程智能体任务中的信用分配难题，实验表明该方法在域内和域外场景中均能提升性能并改善泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务中基于结果的强化学习面临根本性的信用分配挑战：轨迹级别的奖励只能验证最终正确性，却无法对中间推理步骤或工具交互提供有效指导。尤其在多轮搜索智能体中，成功轨迹可能包含误导性动作，失败轨迹可能包含有价值的证据收集步骤，这使得细粒度信用分配尤为困难。

Method: PBSD通过验证答案的后验与先验概率比来衡量轨迹质量，并利用贝叶斯法则将难以估计的答案侧比值转化为可处理的学生模型与特权答案条件教师模型之间的似然比。通过自回归分解贝叶斯证据分数，获得能够识别每个中间回合是否支持或破坏已验证结果的逐轮信号，从而将稀疏结果监督转化为贝叶斯校准的逐轮信用信号，并与标准策略优化完全兼容。

Result: 实验表明PBSD在域内和域外场景中均能持续提升性能，并有效将短上下文训练的知识迁移到长上下文推理中，表明其细粒度信用分配机制促进了更有效的策略学习并改善了泛化能力。

Conclusion: PBSD提供了一种原则性且优雅的重新加权方案，将稀疏结果监督转化为贝叶斯校准的逐轮信用信号，有效解决了长程智能体任务中的信用分配问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks pose a fundamental credit assignment challenge for outcome-base reinforcement learning: trajectory-level rewards verify final correctness but provide limited guidance on which intermediate reasoning steps or tool interactions contribute to the outcome. The difficulty is especially pronounced in multi-turn search agents, where successful trajectories may contain misleading actions and failed trajectories may contain valuable evidence-gathering steps. We propose PBSD (Privileged Bayesian Self-Distillation), a Bayes-calibrated self-distillation method for fine-grained credit assignment under sparse final rewards. PBSD measures trajectory quality through the posterior-to-prior probability ratio of the verified answer and applies Bayes' rule to convert this hard-to-estimate answer-side ratio into a tractable likelihood ratio between a standard student model and a privileged answer-conditioned teacher model. Autoregressive decomposition of this Bayesian evidence score yields turn-level signals that identify whether each intermediate turn supports or undermines the verified outcome. Consequently, PBSD provides a principled and elegant reweighting scheme that transforms sparse outcome supervision into Bayes-calibrated turn-level credit signals, while remaining fully compatible with standard policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that PBSD consistently enhances performance across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, and effectively transfers knowledge from short-context training to long-context inference, suggesting that its fine-grained credit assignment mechanism facilitates more effective policy learning and yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [237] [Scaling Neural Network Verification with Tensor Parallelism and Fully Sharded Data Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09377)
*Sergei Vorobyov,Eugene Ilyushin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对形式化神经网络验证中的GPU内存瓶颈问题，将张量并行（TP）和全分片数据并行（FSDP）两种并行技术适配到auto_LiRPA/α,β-CROWN验证框架中。TP通过分片权重和A矩阵在2卡时获得约2倍峰值内存减少，但边界紧度因强制使用IBP而下降；FSDP仅分片权重矩阵并逐层AllGather，产生的边界与单GPU基准逐位一致，内存降低80-90%，峰值降低34-39%。实验还表明，在α-CROWN+BaB模式下，瓶颈在于每神经元的alpha张量而非权重矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化神经网络验证的边界传播算法（IBP、CROWN、α-CROWN）受限于GPU内存——权重矩阵和松弛系数矩阵必须完全驻留在单个加速器上，限制了可验证网络的规模。

Method: 将两种大规模模型训练中的并行技术适配到验证框架：1) 张量并行（TP）：将权重矩阵和A矩阵分片到多个GPU上；2) 全分片数据并行（FSDP）：仅分片权重矩阵，每层通过AllGather收集完整权重进行计算。

Result: TP在P=2时实现约2倍峰值内存降低，但边界紧度因分片区域被迫使用IBP替代中间界而下降。FSDP产生与单GPU逐位一致的边界，基线内存降低80-90%，峰值内存降低34-39%。FSDP成功集成到完整验证（β-CROWN+Branch-and-Bound）和卷积层中，获得了VNN-COMP 2024 CIFAR-100 ResNet-large的完整unsat结果。

Conclusion: FSDP能够在不牺牲边界紧度的情况下显著降低内存占用，可有效扩展到卷积网络和完整验证场景。α-CROWN+BaB模式下的内存瓶颈是每神经元的alpha张量而非权重矩阵，这为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Formal neural network verification -- proving that a network satisfies safety properties for \emph{all} inputs in a specified domain -- is bounded in practice by GPU memory: standard implementations of bound-propagation algorithms (IBP, CROWN, $α$-CROWN) require weight and relaxation-coefficient matrices to reside entirely on one accelerator. We adapt two parallelism techniques originally developed for large-scale model training to the \texttt{auto\_LiRPA}\,/\,$α,β$-CROWN verification framework. \textbf{Tensor Parallelism (TP)} shards both weight and $A$-matrices across GPUs, achieving ${\approx}2\times$ peak-memory reduction at $P{=}2$; soundness is confirmed on VNN-COMP 2022 MNIST-FC benchmarks, though bound tightness degrades with the number of sharded zones due to forced IBP substitution for intermediate bounds inside sharded zones. \textbf{Fully Sharded Data Parallelism (FSDP)} shards only weight matrices with a per-layer \texttt{AllGather}, producing bounds that are \emph{bitwise identical} to the single-GPU baseline: baseline memory drops by 80--90\%, peak memory by 34--39\% on wide MLPs. FSDP integrates cleanly with complete verification ($β$-CROWN + Branch-and-Bound) and with convolutional layers (\texttt{BoundConv}); a complete \emph{unsat} result is obtained for CIFAR-100 ResNet-large (VNN-COMP 2024) under FSDP. Across all experiments the memory bottleneck in $α$-CROWN+BaB mode proves to be per-neuron alpha tensors, not weight matrices, pointing to the key direction for future work.

</details>


### [238] [Reasoning Arena: Trace Tournaments When Verifiable Rewards Fall Short](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09380)
*Han Zhou,Adam X. Yang,Laurence Aitchison,Anna Korhonen,Albert Q. Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Arena框架，通过对零优势样本（组内所有轨迹获得相同奖励）构建轨迹锦标赛，利用Bradley-Terry模型从部分比较中提取细粒度相对奖励信号，从而将无梯度信号的非多样奖励组转换为有效梯度更新，在数学和编程基准上平均提升7.6%，训练加速27%-41%，节省近50%生成计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLVR范式中，当同一提示的所有采样轨迹获得相同可验证奖励时，组级优势估计无法提供梯度信号，但这些轨迹的推理质量可能差异显著，导致大量样本被浪费。现有方法直接丢弃这些非多样奖励组，未能利用其中的推理质量差异。

Method: 1) 将非多样奖励组的推理轨迹路由至裁判系统而非丢弃；2) 构建轨迹锦标赛，通过两两比较暴露组内细粒度偏好，将推理质量转化为相对奖励信号；3) 动态维护一个较小的锚点轨迹池，每个新轨迹仅与池中锚点比较，避免二次方配对；4) 在非完全比较图上拟合Bradley-Terry模型，实现可扩展的RL集成。

Result: 在竞赛数学和编程基准上，Reasoning Arena相比RLVR基线平均提升7.6%；训练加速27%-41%；节省近50%生成计算；有效将零优势样本转化为有用梯度更新。

Conclusion: 通过将原本被浪费的零优势样本转化为有效训练信号，Reasoning Arena显著提升了RLVR的训练效率和推理性能，证明了在组级奖励无差异时引入相对比较机制的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a leading paradigm for improving the reasoning ability of large language models through outcome-based supervision. However, verifiable rewards frequently become uninformative at the group level: when all sampled traces of a given prompt receive identical rewards, group-relative advantage estimation provides no gradient signal, even though the traces may differ substantially in reasoning quality. We propose Reasoning Arena, an adaptive training framework that routes such non-diverse reward groups to a judge system instead of discarding them. Beyond examining the final answer, Reasoning Arena constructs trace tournaments, where reasoning traces are compared head-to-head to expose finer-grained preferences within the group, converting reasoning quality into rich relative reward signals. To make reward estimation efficient, rather than exhaustively comparing every pair, each new trace is evaluated against a small, dynamically updated pool of previously generated traces as anchors to efficiently establish a relative ranking. We then fit a Bradley-Terry model on the incomplete comparison graph, enabling scalable RL integration without quadratic pairwise comparisons. Empirical results demonstrate that Reasoning Arena consistently outperforms the RLVR baseline by 7.6% on average in competition mathematics and coding benchmarks. By converting otherwise wasted zero-advantage samples into useful gradient updates, our method accelerates training by 27% to 41%, saving nearly 50% of generation compute, and substantially improves overall reasoning performance.

</details>


### [239] [Distilling Safe LLM Systems via Soft Prompts for On Device Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09388)
*Motasem Alfarra,Cristina Pinneri,Dana Kianfar,Mohammed Almousa,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限边缘设备上部署安全LLM的参数高效安全对齐方法。通过系统评估，发现软提示（soft prompts）结合基于蒸馏的训练方法在安全-有用性权衡上优于LoRA、引导向量和直接优化等方法，且推理时仅需极少的额外内存和计算资源，确立了软提示蒸馏作为设备端LLM部署的首选安全对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署安全的LLM面临关键挑战：传统的双模型系统（LLM+安全防护模型）虽然能提供有效安全保障，但其巨大的内存和计算需求使其在设备端部署成本过高，亟需参数高效的安全对齐方法。

Method: 系统性地评估了多种参数高效微调方法（包括软提示、LoRA适配器、引导向量、直接优化等），跨多个LLM架构、训练目标和参数高效微调策略进行对比实验。提出了基于总变差（total variation）和KL散度的蒸馏框架，用于将防护模型的安全行为迁移至可学习的软提示中。

Result: 软提示与基于蒸馏的训练相结合的方法在安全-有用性权衡上持续优于LoRA适配器、引导向量和直接优化方法，且推理时仅需极少的额外内存和计算资源。

Conclusion: 软提示蒸馏被确立为设备端LLM部署中安全对齐的首选方法，为资源受限环境下的安全LLM部署提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying safe large language models (LLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices presents a critical challenge: while dual-model systems combining LLMs with guard models provide effective safety guarantees, their substantial memory and computational demands make them prohibitively expensive for on-device deployment. This paper presents a comprehensive study of parameter-efficient safety alignment methods for resource-constrained settings. Through systematic evaluation across multiple LLM architectures, training objectives, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning approaches, we identify that soft prompts combined with distillation-based training consistently outperform alternative methods. We introduce distillation frameworks based on total variation and KL divergence that effectively transfer safety behaviors from guard models into learned soft prompts. Our evaluations on various benchmarks demonstrate that this combination achieves superior safety-usefulness trade-offs compared to LoRA adapters, steering vectors, and direct optimization methods, while requiring minimal additional memory and compute at inference time. These findings establish soft prompt distillation as the preferred approach for safety alignment in on-device LLM deployment.

</details>


### [240] [Benchmarking Empirical Privacy Protection for Adaptations of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09401)
*Bartłomiej Marek,Lorenzo Rossi,Vincent Hanke,Xun Wang,Michael Backes,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了差分隐私（DP）在大型语言模型（LLM）微调中的实际隐私保护效果。通过使用先进的成员推理和样本提取攻击，评估了不同数据分布（从与预训练数据完全重叠到完全分布外数据）、不同微调方法和不同隐私预算下的隐私风险。发现数据分布偏移显著影响隐私脆弱性：即使没有直接数据重叠，与预训练分布越接近的微调数据，实际隐私风险越高。LoRA等参数高效微调方法对分布外数据具有最佳实证隐私保护。最后提出了超越微调阶段的全流程隐私评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究应用差分隐私保护LLM微调，但其实际效果不明确。原因是预训练数据与微调数据之间存在重叠和依赖关系，可能削弱DP的理论保障。作者希望通过实证分析揭示这一问题的实际影响。

Method: 使用最先进的成员推理攻击和样本提取攻击，系统性地改变微调数据分布（与预训练数据完全重叠、同分布、完全分布外），评估不同微调方法和不同隐私预算下的隐私风险。

Result: 数据分布偏移强烈影响隐私脆弱性：微调数据与预训练分布越接近，同等理论保证下实际隐私风险越高。LoRA等参数高效微调方法对分布外数据具有最佳实证隐私保护效果。

Conclusion: 识别了影响DP LLM微调实际隐私保护的关键因素，为在敏感场景部署定制模型提供了可操作的见解。并提出了超越微调阶段的、覆盖预训练到微调全流程的隐私评估框架。

Abstract: Recent work has applied differential privacy (DP) to adapt large language models (LLMs) for sensitive applications, offering theoretical guarantees. However, its practical effectiveness remains unclear, partly due to LLM pretraining, where overlaps and interdependencies with adaptation data can undermine privacy despite DP efforts. To analyze this issue in practice, we investigate privacy risks under DP adaptations in LLMs using state-of-the-art attacks such as robust membership inference and canary data extraction. We benchmark these risks by systematically varying the adaptation data distribution, from exact overlaps with pretraining data, through in-distribution (IID) cases, to entirely out-of-distribution (OOD) examples. Additionally, we evaluate how different adaptation methods and different privacy regimes impact the vulnerability. Our results show that distribution shifts strongly influence privacy vulnerability: the closer the adaptation data is to the pretraining distribution, the higher the practical privacy risk at the same theoretical guarantee, even without direct data overlap. We find that parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, such as LoRA, achieve the highest empirical privacy protection for OOD data. Our benchmark identifies key factors for achieving practical privacy in DP LLM adaptation, providing actionable insights for deploying customized models in sensitive settings. Looking forward, we propose a structured framework for holistic privacy assessment beyond adaptation privacy, to identify and evaluate risks across the full pretrain-adapt pipeline of LLMs.

</details>


### [241] [LargeMonitor: Monitoring Online Task-Free Continual Learning via Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09430)
*Mingqi Yuan,Xiaoquan Sun,Shihao Luo,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LargeMonitor框架，利用预训练基础模型（LVM检测分布漂移+LMM诊断漂移语义原因），实现在线无任务连续学习中的解耦式漂移检测与诊断，并动态部署自适应优化策略，提升现有TFCL算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线TFCL方法依赖于训练耦合的优化动态（如经验损失波动或潜在距离演变）来驱动参数高效提示调优或动态结构扩展，无法区分分布漂移的结构性起源，对本质上不同的流式变化采用固定策略，缺乏针对性。

Method: LargeMonitor包含两个解耦模块：1) 利用冻结的大型视觉模型(LVM)的稳定表示空间进行零样本漂移检测，无需训练依赖或阈值调优；2) 检测到漂移后，由大型多模态模型(LMM)驱动的上下文感知诊断模块，解释流变化的精确语义病因（如新类出现vs环境域偏移）。据此动态部署自适应、特定于漂移类型的优化策略。

Result: 在多个TFCL设置和基准上的大量实验表明，LargeMonitor实现了对复杂数据流的精确、鲁棒检测与诊断，并持续提升现有在线TFCL算法的性能。

Conclusion: 利用预训练基础模型的解耦能力，可以有效解决在线TFCL中训练耦合方法对分布漂移结构起源不敏感的问题，实现更智能、自适应的连续学习。

Abstract: Online task-free continual learning (TFCL) requires intelligent agents to sequentially accumulate knowledge from an unbounded, non-stationary data stream under strict single-pass constraints and without any explicit task identifiers. Existing online TFCL paradigms primarily rely on parameter-efficient prompt tuning or dynamic structure expansion driven by training-coupled optimization dynamics, such as empirical loss fluctuations or evolving latent distances. As a result, these training-coupled solvers remain agnostic to the structural origins of distribution drift, mechanically enforcing a fixed strategy across fundamentally distinct streaming variations. To address this gap, we propose LargeMonitor, a framework that leverages large pretrained foundation models to autonomously orchestrate task-free continuous adaptation. Specifically, LargeMonitor introduces a decoupled detection module utilizing the frozen, stable representation space of large vision models (LVMs) to achieve robust, zero-shot drift detection without training-dependent interference or brittle threshold tuning. Upon a confirmed drift, the framework activates a context-aware diagnostic module driven by large multimodal models (LMMs) to interpret the precise semantic etiologies of the stream variation (e.g., novel class emergence vs. environmental domain shift). This dual-stage capability empowers the continuous learner to dynamically deploy adaptive and shift-specific optimization strategies. Extensive experiments across multiple TFCL settings and benchmarks demonstrate that LargeMonitor achieves precise, robust detection and diagnosis of complex data streams while consistently improving the performance of existing online TFCL algorithms.

</details>


### [242] [Graph Mamba Operator: A Latent Simulator for Interacting Particle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09432)
*Karn Tiwari,Niladri Dutta,N M Anoop Krishnan,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph Mamba Operator (GraMO)，一种将状态空间模型与图交互学习结合的潜空间模拟器，通过单一循环耦合图交互和时间状态更新，在N体系统、运动捕捉和机器人数据集上实现最低误差和最佳长程预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络（GNNs）在处理交互动力系统时存在两个主要局限：(1) 依赖自回归 rollout 并分开处理空间和时间动力学，导致长程预测误差累积；(2) 仅关注局部交互和短时间上下文，无法捕获多跳依赖和全局结构。

Method: GraMO 将图神经网络与状态空间模型（SSM）集成，在潜空间中通过单一循环同时进行图交互学习和时间状态更新。更新过程对潜状态是线性的，但系数是输入相关的，能够适应不同动力学机制。

Result: 在N体系统、运动捕捉和机器人数据集上，GraMO 在所有基准测试中取得最低误差，并在长程预测任务中获得最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将图交互和时间演化耦合在单一循环中，GraMO 有效解决了现有方法在长程预测中的误差累积问题，能够捕获多跳依赖和全局结构，为交互动力系统建模提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Modeling interacting dynamical systems requires capturing spatial interactions alongside long-range temporal dependencies. Graph neural networks (GNNs) provide a natural representation but typically rely on autoregressive rollouts and treat spatial and temporal dynamics separately, leading to error accumulation over long horizons.
